### [1] [Nijenhuis torsion and Frölicher-Nijenhuis brackets of recursion operators via their full-fledged forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29964)
*Petr Vojcak*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Jahnová和Vojčák（2024）引入的全结构（full-fledged）形式来计算递归算子Nijenhuis挠率和Frölicher-Nijenhuis括号的新方法。与传统的在覆盖中表示非局部对称影子映射的方法不同，该方法可直接通过定义公式计算Nijenhuis挠率，无需额外的数学构造。该方法还适用于计算递归算子的Frölicher-Nijenhuis括号并验证其兼容性。作者用多个四独立变量微分方程的实例进行了说明，包括一个此前文献中未出现的四维普适层次方程的新递归算子，并直接证明了该算子的Nijenhuis挠率消失。研究表明全结构形式为研究递归算子的遗传性与兼容性（包括高度非局部和多维情形）提供了有效框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将递归算子表示为给定覆盖中非局部对称影子之间的映射，在计算Nijenhuis挠率和Frölicher-Nijenhuis括号时需要额外构造数学工具，尤其对高度非局部和多维情形十分困难。作者旨在利用全结构形式直接应用定义公式计算这些几何不变量，简化流程并拓展可处理的范畴。

Method: 基于Jahnová和Vojčák（2024）提出的全结构（full-fledged）递归算子框架，直接利用Nijenhuis挠率的定义公式进行计算，无需在覆盖构造中引入额外工具。同一框架也用于计算Frölicher-Nijenhuis括号以及验证递归算子对的兼容性。作者通过多个四独立变量微分方程的实例来演示该方法。

Result: 给出了多个四独立变量微分方程的全结构递归算子实例，其中包括一个此前文献中未见过的四维普适层次方程的新递归算子。该新算子的影子分量在标准传统形式中缺乏合理表示，但作者直接证明了其Nijenhuis挠率消失。所有考虑实例的Frölicher-Nijenhuis括号也均消失，确认了相应递归算子对的兼容性。

Conclusion: 全结构形式为研究递归算子的遗传性（hereditary）和兼容性提供了有效框架，尤其适合处理现有方法难以应对的高度非局部和/或多维情形。该方法绕过了传统影子表示中的覆盖构造需求，直接通过定义公式验证Nijenhuis挠率与Frölicher-Nijenhuis括号的消失。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to computing the Nijenhuis torsion and Frölicher-Nijenhuis brackets of recursion operators for symmetries, based on their full-fledged forms introduced by Jahnová and Vojčák (2024). In contrast to the conventional approach, which represents recursion operators as maps between shadows of nonlocal symmetries within a given covering, our method allows the Nijenhuis torsion to be computed directly from its defining formula, without requiring any additional mathematical constructions. The same framework can also be used to compute the Frölicher-Nijenhuis bracket of recursion operators and to verify their compatibility directly in their full-fledged forms. The procedure is illustrated by several examples of full-fledged recursion operators for symmetries of differential equations in four independent variables, including a new recursion operator for the four-dimensional universal hierarchy equation that, to the best of our knowledge, has not previously appeared in the literature. A notable feature of this new full-fledged recursion operator is that its shadow component does not appear to admit a reasonable representation in any of the standard conventional forms. Nevertheless, our approach allows us to prove directly that the Nijenhuis torsion of this operator vanishes. For the examples considered, the corresponding Frölicher-Nijenhuis brackets also vanish, confirming the compatibility of the respective pairs of recursion operators. This demonstrates that full-fledged forms provide an effective framework for studying the hereditary and compatibility properties of recursion operators, including highly nonlocal and/or multidimensional cases that are difficult to handle by existing methods.

</details>


### [2] [Lattice KP type equations arising from eigenfunctions and Dbar problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30190)
*Leilei Shi,Peter van der Kamp,Cheng Zhang,Da-jun Zhang*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 该论文构造了格点Kadomtsev-Petviashvili (KP)型特征函数方程。通过齐次非局部\bar{\partial}问题定义格点KP方程Lax对的特征函数，该特征函数及其在无穷远和有限解析点的展开给出了格点修正KP方程、格点Schwarzian KP方程和Nijhoff-Quispel-Capel KP (NQC-KP)方程的不同表述。同时，通过非齐次非局部\bar{\partial}问题定义格点修正KP方程Lax对的特征函数，生成了NQC-KP方程的直接表述。文中获得了这些方程的显式解，以展示同一方程不同表述之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可积系统理论中，如何系统地在离散（格点）框架下构造不同类型的KP型方程（包括修正KP、Schwarzian KP及NQC-KP），并建立它们之间的层次关系，是一个重要而尚未充分解决的问题。论文旨在利用非局部\bar{\partial}问题这一逆散射方法工具，统一构造格点KP型特征函数方程，从而为这些离散可积方程的推导与求解提供统一框架。

Method: 论文采用两种非局部\bar{\partial}问题构造方案：(1) 齐次非局部\bar{\partial}问题，用于定义格点KP方程Lax对的特征函数；该特征函数在无穷远处和有限解析点处的展开分别导出格点修正KP方程、格点Schwarzian KP方程及NQC-KP方程。(2) 非齐次非局部\bar{\partial}问题，用于定义格点修正KP方程Lax对的特征函数，进而直接生成NQC-KP方程的表述（与前一种表述不同）。最后通过求解这些\bar{\partial}问题获得相应方程的显式解。

Result: 论文成功利用齐次和非齐次非局部\bar{\partial}问题，系统构造了格点KP型方程的特征函数方程，得到了格点修正KP方程、格点Schwarzian KP方程和NQC-KP方程的多种等价（但形式不同）表述。通过非齐次\bar{\partial}问题所获得的NQC-KP方程直接表述与之前通过齐次情形得到的表述存在差异。文中给出了各方程的显式解，并对比展示了同一方程不同表述间的本质区别。

Conclusion: 该工作表明非局部\bar{\partial}问题是构造离散可积系统特征函数的有效工具，能够统一导出多种格点KP型方程。不同表述对应着不同的特征函数展开方式，其显式解的形式差异揭示了同一离散可积方程在不同表示下的本质特征。这为格点可积系统的逆散射分析提供了新的方法论视角。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct the lattice Kadomtsev-Petviashvili (KP) type eigenfunction equations. A homogeneous nonlocal $\bar{\partial}$ problem is considered, from which we are able to define the eigenfunction of the Lax pair of the lattice KP equation. The eigenfunction together with its expansions at infinity and at a finite analytic point provide formulations of the lattice modified KP equation, the lattice Schwarzian KP equation and the Nijhoff-Quispel-Capel KP (NQC-KP) equation. We also consider an inhomogeneous nonlocal $\bar{\partial}$ problem. It defines the eigenfunction of the Lax pair of the lattice modified KP equation. This eigenfunction generates a direct formulation for the NQC-KP equation, which is different from the previous ones. Explicit solutions of these equations are obtained, from which we can see the difference of the different formulations for same equations.

</details>

### [3] [Spinor Structure from Relativistic Mass-Shell Factorisation in Phase Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29353)
*Mark Everitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文表明，自旋可能并非本质上量子化的现象，而是源于对相对论相空间施加完整质壳约束时，要求哈密顿量为标量且对动量线性这一过度严格的假设。这一要求迫使系数矩阵满足Clifford代数，其最小复表示为四维，从而在量子化之前自然涌现出旋量结构和2×2矩阵系综分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋通常被视为最本质的量子现象之一，但它在经典物理学中缺乏自然对应物，这为相空间形变量子化框架下解释其起源设置了障碍。作者认为这一困难可能源于对相对论相空间施加了过度严格限制的标量哈密顿结构。

Method: 文章从完整的大质量质壳约束出发，要求其被单个有限维表达式表示且对所有四个动量分量线性。这强制系数矩阵满足Clifford代数（最小复表示为四维）。随后要求在质壳因子选取的秩二子空间内实现统计完备性，得到4×4矩阵值系综分布。通过将Weyl排序的Liouvillle方程投影到每个在壳动量上的二维子空间，得到相对论输运方程。进一步展开矩阵Moyal星号对易子给出领头阶对称化经典矩阵Liouvillian，并推导左右Dirac-Wigner方程。

Result: 在量子化之前，一般经典系综由2×2矩阵描述；投影后可同时保留任意布居和相干性，剩余投影分量决定首个形变修正。施加更强的双侧星号约束可重现左、右Dirac-Wigner方程，并引入ħ作为将无量纲内部代数转换为物理角动量的尺度。

Conclusion: 结果表明旋量结构存在非量子起源，为协调相对论协变性、经典相空间输运和量子自旋提供了可能途径。

Abstract: Spin is usually regarded as one of the most intrinsically quantum phenomena, while its lack of a natural analogue in classical physics presents an obstacle to phase-space deformation-quantisation accounts of its origin. We show that this difficulty may arise from imposing an overly restrictive scalar Hamiltonian structure on relativistic phase space. Requiring the complete massive mass-shell constraint to be represented by a single finite-dimensional expression that is linear in all four components of momentum forces its coefficient matrices to satisfy a Clifford algebra. The minimal complex representation of this algebra is four-dimensional. Requiring statistical completeness within the rank-two subspace selected by the mass-shell factor then leads to a four-by-four matrix-valued ensemble distribution. At each on-shell momentum, the linear mass-shell operator selects a two-dimensional subspace, so a general classical ensemble is described by a $2 \times 2$ matrix before quantisation. Projecting the Weyl-ordered Liouville equation into this subspace gives relativistic transport while preserving arbitrary populations and coherences and the remaining projector components determine the first deformation correction. Expansion of the matrix Moyal star commutator yields the symmetrised classical matrix Liouvillian at leading order. If the stronger two-sided star constraints are imposed, they reproduce the left and right Dirac-Wigner equations and introduce $\hbar$ as the scale converting the dimensionless internal algebra into physical angular momentum. These results suggest a non-quantum origin for spinor structure and a route to reconciling relativistic covariance, classical phase-space transport, and quantum spin.

</details>


### [4] [Alphabet-Dependent Bounds for Pure Quantum $(r,ρ)$-Locally Recoverable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28650)
*Vijay Kumar,Ramakrishna Bandi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了通过Hermitian CSS构造得到的纯(r,ρ)-量子局部可恢复码的三个与字母表相关的上界：Griesmer型、Plotkin型和球堆积型界，并建立了它们之间的渐近层次关系以及各自给出最紧码率约束的相对距离区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的(r,ρ)-量子局部可恢复码的界（Singleton型和GG Singleton型）与字母表无关，因此对小到中等规模的量子位维度过于宽松，需要推导与字母表相关的更紧上界。

Method: 针对通过Hermitian CSS构造的纯(r,ρ)-qLRC，分别推导了Griesmer型、Plotkin型和球堆积型三类字母表相关的上界，并分析了这些界之间的渐近关系。

Result: 成功推导出三个字母表相关的上界，确定了这些界之间的渐近层次结构，并识别出每个界在相对距离的哪些区间内提供最紧的码率约束。

Conclusion: 所提出的字母表相关上界比现有与字母表无关的界更紧，为小到中等量子位维度下的(r,ρ)-qLRC设计提供了更精确的性能极限指导。

Abstract: A quantum $(r,ρ)$-locally recoverable code ($(r,ρ)$-qLRC) is a quantum code in which every qudit can be recovered from at most $r+ρ-1$ other qudits, even after $ρ-1$ additional erasures inside the recovery set. The bounds currently known for this class, namely the Singleton-like and the GG Singleton-like bounds, are alphabet independent and are therefore loose for small-to-moderate qudit dimensions. In this letter, we derive three alphabet-dependent upper bounds for pure $(r,ρ)$-qLRCs obtained through the Hermitian CSS construction: a Griesmer-like, a Plotkin-like, and a sphere-packing-like bound. We further establish the asymptotic hierarchy among these bounds and identify the relative-distance regions in which each of them yields the tightest rate constraint.

</details>


### [5] [Quantum synchronization of spin-1 system: Enhanced synchronization due to additive Lindblad operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28808)
*Tran Duong Anh-Tai,S. Zhong,X. Molenda,A. M. Marino,D. Blume*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过实验研究了最简单量子系统之一——受外部驱动的等间距能级有效自旋-1系统的量子同步现象。通过耦合激发辅助态实现了与非常规耗散路径相关的加法有效Lindblad算符，证明了在某些参数区间可增强量子同步。实验采用MOT中的冷^87Rb原子，并通过专门仿真对标了同步提取协议，实验与仿真结果高度一致。该耗散工程技术可推广到自旋-3/2或自旋-2等高自旋系统，对量子同步及相关量子科学技术应用有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子同步在最简单量子系统（有效自旋-1）中的实验实现，并研究通过耗散工程（非常规耗散路径）增强量子同步的可行性，填补该领域实验验证的空白。

Method: 使用MOT中的冷^87Rb原子构建受外部驱动的等间距能级有效自旋-1系统；通过耦合激发辅助态实现加法的有效Lindblad算符，引入非常规耗散路径；结合专用仿真模拟对同步提取协议进行详细基准测试。

Result: 在特定参数区间内，非常规耗散路径确实能够增强量子同步；实验与仿真结果之间取得令人信服的一致性。

Conclusion: 所建立的耗散工程方法可便捷扩展到更多能级系统（如自旋-3/2或自旋-2），对高自旋系统量子同步研究以及量子科学技术应用具有广泛意义。

Abstract: We experimentally investigate quantum synchronization for one of the simplest possible quantum systems, namely an externally driven few-level system with equally spaced energy levels effectively acting as a spin-1 system. Coupling to excited auxiliary states, we realize additive effective Lindblad operators that are associated with non-conventional dissipative pathways, which are shown to enhance, in certain parameter regimes, quantum synchronization. The experimental set-up, which utilizes cold $^{87}$Rb atoms in a MOT, and associated synchronization extraction protocol are benchmarked carefully through dedicated simulations. Convincing agreement is found between experiment and simulations. The dissipation engineering approach established in our work can be readily extended to systems with more energy levels, such as effective spin-$3/2$ or spin-$2$ systems, and has implications for quantum synchronization studies in higher-spin systems as well as for a wide range of quantum science studies and technology applications.

</details>


### [6] [BAHAMAS: A Control Plane for Optimization and Execution of Variational Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28811)
*Amit Samanta,Mohammad Abrarul Hasanat,Jason Ludmir,Ryan Stutsman,Tirthak Patel,Rohan Basu Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出BAHAMAS，一个在线控制框架，通过基于共识的保真度估计自适应选择物理映射来稳定变分量子算法（VQAs）的噪声暴露，无需模拟器、离线训练或先验执行。在真实量子设备上，BAHAMAS提升了优化可靠性，并支持推理时在漂移下的重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQAs面临不稳定的优化问题，其根源在于时间上的噪声漂移和静态量子比特映射会扭曲跨迭代的梯度信号，导致优化过程不可靠。

Method: BAHAMAS是一种在线控制框架，通过基于共识的保真度估计（consensus-based fidelity estimation）自适应选择物理映射，从而稳定噪声暴露。该方法无需模拟器、离线训练或先前执行经验，采用逐迭代（per-iteration）级别的鲁棒控制。

Result: 在真实量子设备上的实验表明，BAHAMAS能够提升优化的可靠性，并支持在噪声漂移条件下的推理时重定向（inference-time retargeting）。

Conclusion: BAHAMAS通过动态映射选择而非静态映射，有效缓解了时间噪声漂移对VQA优化的负面影响，为量子计算中的实时噪声适应提供了一种无需预训练的在线控制方案。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) suffer from unstable optimization due to temporal noise drift and static qubit mappings that distort gradient signals across iterations. We present BAHAMAS, an online control framework that stabilizes noise exposure by adaptively selecting physical mappings via consensus-based fidelity estimation, without requiring simulators, offline training, or prior executions. Across real quantum devices, BAHAMAS improves optimization reliability and supports inference-time retargeting under drift through robust, per-iteration control.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械谐振器与量子二能级系统的超声波耦合在慢振荡器区域可引入显著的非简谐性从而实现机械量子比特，但现有理论对耗散的处理在不同参数区间（从弱非简谐到强非简谐的机械量子比特区间）缺乏一致性描述，亟需建立适用于当前纳米管机电实验所探测的大参数空间的统一开放系统框架。

Result: 发现随着近似的失效（secular approximation breakdown），声子阻塞逐渐被抑制，但该框架在此区域仍有效并能定量描述声子阻塞随耦合强度和耗散的连续演化。有限温度下，弱非简谐区域的温度诱导相干衰减率增加被抑制。在驱动下，该方法比传统缀饰态主方程适用于更强的扰动，且展示了表观经典观测量与Wigner负性共存。同时捕获了双量子点中有限失谐产生的弱非简谐区域。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从弱非简谐工作区间到强非简谐机械量子比特区间的统一耗散描述，为超强机电系统提供了具有实验预测意义的理论框架。

Abstract: Mechanical resonators ultrastrongly coupled to quantum two-level systems provide a promising route towards mechanical qubits by introducing significant anharmonicity to the mechanical modes, particularly in the slow-oscillator regime. Although the resulting hybrid system is well described by the quantum Rabi model, a consistent treatment of dissipation remains challenging across the broad parameter space routinely probed in current nanotube electromechanical devices. Here, we investigate dissipation in the open quantum Rabi model using a Born-Markov framework based on the slowly varying bath spectrum approximation, yielding a Lindblad master equation applicable far beyond conventional descriptions while recovering them in their respective limits. Using this framework, we analyze experimentally accessible observables across this parameter space. As the secular approximation breaks down, phonon blockade progressively washes out. Our approach remains valid in this regime, enabling a quantitative description of the continuous evolution of phonon blockade with coupling strength and dissipation. At finite temperature, we find a suppression of the temperature-induced increase of coherence decay rate for weak anharmonicity. Under driving, our approach remains applicable to substantially stronger perturbations than conventional dressed-state master equations and shows that an apparently classical observable can coexist with Wigner negativity. We further capture the weakly anharmonic regime arising from finite detuning in the double-quantum dot. These results establish a unified description of dissipation from weakly anharmonic operating regimes to the strongly anharmonic mechanical-qubit regime and provide experimentally relevant predictions for ultrastrong electromechanical systems.

</details>


### [8] [Representation Learning with Quantum Signal Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28828)
*Junqi Wang,Junyu Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

By considering standard quantum state tomography, we numerically verify the presence of an optimal resource distribution, which also depends on the purity of the state under consideration. Our results identify finite-copy work extraction as a genuinely task-dependent inference problem, in which estimation strategies should be judged by thermodynamic performance rather than reconstruction fidelity alone.

Abstract: We present FIREQ (FPGA Instrumentation for Readout and Qubit control), an open-source RFSoC-based framework for the control and readout of superconducting qubits. FIREQ combines a modular AXI-compliant firmware architecture with a PYNQ-based software stack designed to support extensible hardware integration, deterministic experiment timing, and low-overhead execution of repeated calibration and characterization workflows. The firmware implements direct RF synthesis and acquisition, trigger-based sequencing, programmable pulse generation, frequency-multiplexed readout, and memory-efficient acquisition and waveform buffering. The software adopts a client-server architecture with streamed data transfer and dependency-aware configuration updates to reduce host-device and reconfiguration overhead during parameter sweeps. On an AMD Zynq UltraScale+ RFSoC ZCU216, FIREQ generates RF pulses up to 9.3 GHz with a pulse-duration resolution of 107 ps and an event-timing resolution of 1.7 ns. FPGA resource utilization is compared with representative open-source RFSoC control frameworks, showing a low BRAM footprint while retaining full-rate I/Q generation and acquisition. The RF output is characterized in terms of phase noise, noise spectral density, and inter-channel timing skew. End-to-end operation is validated on a superconducting qubit through resonator spectroscopy, Rabi, Ramsey, and relaxation measurements, yielding T1 = 6.94 us and T2* = 13.50 us. FIREQ can therefore be used both as a qubit-control platform and as an experimental environment for evaluating alternative control and readout IP architectures.

Abstract: We bound the magic of a post-selected logical measurement by the resource that produced it. The witness reads the accepted effect against the free set of the resource theory of magic, outcome by outcome and not on the averaged channel, since a channel can be free while one of its outcomes measures the magic axis. Our first bound is unconditional. The accepted magic is at most a constant times the summed distance of the cells from the free set. The second is the main result. When the resource cells sit inside a bounded-spread exact-recovery skeleton, the recovery puts every insertion history below a threshold onto a single free branch, transcript by transcript, so only connected clusters reaching the threshold contribute and an exact-component expansion controls their weight. With a threshold linear in the code distance, polynomially many cells of bounded insertion degree and per-cell dilation amplitude $O(1/d)$, the accepted magic times the acceptance probability is at most $\exp[-Ω(d\log d)]$. Post-selection is disposed of before accepted transcripts are summed, so a branch of vanishing probability cannot be amplified into a magic effect. The threshold is certified from a circuit, and we run it on one exact round of stabilizer measurement followed by a split readout, which measures logical $X$ on the accepted fibre and logical $Z$ on the rejected ones. That certifies a threshold equal to the code distance for every single-layer pattern of weak $Z$-rotations, one per data qubit, so the hypotheses are met by a family and not one design. A member carries magic only if its support contains a logical $Z$ string. One member attains the exponent, again at the level of the accepted effect. Suppression is set by the threshold and not by the topology of the block.

Abstract: Pessiland is a world where NP is hard on average but one-way functions (OWFs) do not exist [Impagliazzo 1995]. Because almost all classical cryptographic primitives imply OWFs [Impagliazzo and Luby 1989], there is almost no classical cryptography in Pessiland. On the other hand, quantum cryptography can exist even when OWFs do not [Kretschmer 2021; Morimae and Yamakawa 2022; Ananth, Qian and Yuen 2022]. Is there a quantum analogue of Pessiland where NP is hard on average but even quantum cryptography does not exist? In this paper, we show that such a miserable world, Quantum Pessiland, exists: there is a quantum oracle relative to which $UP\cap coUP$ is hard on average against quantum polynomial-time algorithms with quantum advice, yet auxiliary-input EFI pairs do not exist. We also show that there is a classical oracle relative to which $UP\cap coUP$ is hard on average against quantum polynomial-time algorithms with quantum advice, yet classically-secure auxiliary-input one-way puzzles (OWPuzzs) do not exist. Almost all quantum cryptographic primitives imply EFI pairs or OWPuzzs, and therefore these results mean that there is almost no quantum cryptography relative to these oracles. We further show that relative to the classical oracle, SampBQP = SampBPP, and therefore there is no sampling-based quantum advantage in Quantum Pessiland. Finally, because our average-case hardness of $UP\cap coUP$ implies $P^{\#P}\not\subseteq i.o.BQP/qpoly$, our result also implies that a non-relativizing proof technique is necessary to construct OWPuzzs solely from $P^{\#P}\not\subseteq i.o.BQP/qpoly$, which gives a partial negative answer to the open problem of [Khurana and Tomer 2025].

</details>

Abstract: The enhancement of atom-photon interactions with F-P cavities provides a suitable platform for studying quantum optics and atomic physics. Abstract: Dynamical instability in parametrically driven bosonic systems is generally associated with diverging occupations and the absence of a stationary state, but its implications for long-time entanglement remain unclear. We further characterize the finite asymptotic entanglement and show that asymmetric local dissipation shifts both the stability boundary and the threshold for nonzero asymptotic entanglement. For identical local dissipation, the presence of a common bath leaves the dynamical-region boundaries unchanged while qualitatively reshaping the long-time entanglement landscape, producing broad enhancement regions within the stable regimes and a narrow enhancement region within the unstable regime. In the common-bath-only limit, this enhancement is attributed to an exact dark-mode (bound-state-in-the-continuum, BIC) protection mechanism, which reduces to a quasi-BIC remnant under independent dissipation. Our results establish the non-Hermitian dynamical spectrum as a framework for connecting stability, nonequilibrium dynamics, and asymptotic entanglement in open quadratic bosonic systems.

Abstract: The bosonic Kitaev chain under open boundary conditions has attracted recent attention due to its realization in driven-dissipative systems and its intriguing non-Hermitian boundary physics. The model is known to be solvable via local squeezing transformations in the position-momentum representation. In this paper, we present an alternative, purely algebraic solution that relies entirely on the standard bosonic Bogoliubov transformation. For an $N$-site chain, we propose an asymmetric plane-wave ansatz with unequal left- and right-moving momenta to analytically solve the associated $2N\times 2N$ non-Hermitian ``associated matrix". By carefully resolving these degeneracies using the bosonic commutation relations, we construct the $N$ physical Bogoliubov quasiparticle operators. The construction reveals non-uniquenesses that in special cases exactly mirror the freedom in the local squeezing transformations of the original approach. The diagonal form of the Hamiltonian is obtained explicitly and is shown to be equivalent to the original Hamiltonian. The proposed asymmetric plane-wave ansatz and degeneracy-resolution technique are not limited to the present model and can be generalized to other bosonic pairing systems, including those with inhomogeneous pairing or hopping terms.

Collective measurements, {which coherently process the received starlight across multiple time bins in a single joint readout}, remove the array-size penalty up to an order-unity factor, yielding an asymptotic \(O(\sqrt{N})\) enhancement for the {directional-averaged} SNR of visibility measurement. Abstract: In 2010, Pál and Vértesi found a family of finite-dimensional strategies for the $I_{3322}$ Bell inequality whose optimized values appeared to converge as the local Hilbert-space dimension grew. They conjectured that this limit is the supremum over all finite-dimensional quantum strategies, but that no finite-dimensional strategy attains it. We prove both claims. The proof uses the symmetry of the Bell functional to associate every strategy with a finite matrix of probabilities, one for each pair of spectral subspaces of Alice and Bob. This matrix gives an upper bound on the Bell value, and finite-dimensional strategies built from the repeating structure found by Pál and Vértesi approach it as the dimension grows. If the bound were attained exactly in finite dimension, the optimality conditions would then require a state that cannot be normalized. Consequently, the set of finite-dimensional quantum correlations is not closed in the $(3,3,2,2)$ scenario, the smallest Bell scenario where this can happen. Moreover, approaching the supremum requires unbounded local dimension. The core of the proof was formalized in Lean~4.

Finally, we formulate the corresponding BBM92 quantum key distribution (QKD) secret-key-rate analysis for a continuous-wave-pumped broadband source, with true and accidental coincidences evaluated between detector channels at the two endpoint users and relative layer pair-generation rates fixed by the source spectrum. This framework, therefore, provides a direct route from exact network resource laws to the design and comparison of passive entanglement architectures under experimentally specified hardware constraints.

Abstract: Lattice surgery compilation plays a central role in translating fault-tolerant quantum programs into efficient surface code realizations, where both spatial and temporal resources directly determine the cost of execution. However, existing compilation restricts ZX reduction to preserve diagram structures that can be directly embedded as lattice surgery junctions. We present SpiderLS, which extends prior approach by leveraging full ZX reduction. To translate the resulting diagram into executable lattice surgery operations, SpiderLS applies a sequence of compiler passes that derives an execution order, generates target code by grouping compatible interactions into multi-target operations, and lowers the target code to Pauli-product measurements. The resulting explicit patch and Pauli-boundary requirements guide logical scheduling and structure-aware spacetime routing. Across representative algorithmic and random workloads, SpiderLS achieves average reductions of 49.2% in spacetime volume and 99.8% in compilation time compared with the prior ZX-based compiler.

Abstract: Purified Gibbs states provide a bridge between finite-temperature physics, dissipative dynamics, and ground-state methods. In this work, we study the exact finite sum-of-squares (SoS) construction of their parent Hamiltonians and the associated Lindbladian based on modular annihilators. Given a finite set of Hermitian generators, the corresponding modular annihilators yield a frustration-free SoS representation without continuous time integrals or an explicit decomposition into Bohr-frequency sectors. Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic consequences of longitudinal-transverse cross-spectral reservoir correlations in a finite-time quantum Otto engine with a two-level working medium. Each reservoir couples through excitation-relaxation and dephasing channels whose fluctuations are characterized by a Hermitian positive-semidefinite spectral-density matrix, with the off-diagonal elements encoding their cross correlations. The finite-time isochoric dynamics is derived within the second-order time-convolutionless framework, without imposing the Markov limit at the outset, so that finite reservoir-memory effects can enter through time-dependent dissipative and reservoir-induced coherent contributions. The resulting dynamics is then recast in Bloch-vector form to construct the stroke-resolved cycle dynamics. At fixed auto-spectral densities, cross-spectral correlations modify the populations and coherences of the working medium and thereby its thermodynamic performance. Increasing the correlation strength can enhance the output power, with the enhancement controlled by the cross-spectral phase and characteristic frequency scale. The correlations also reshape the transient cycle-to-cycle evolution and the approach to periodic operation, while the limit-cycle efficiency remains fixed at the Otto value for the population-preserving unitary strokes considered here. These results establish off-diagonal reservoir spectra as an additional resource for controlling finite-time quantum thermal machines.

Abstract: The Monte Carlo Projective Quantum Eigensolver (MC-PQE) was recently introduced as an alternative to hybrid algorithms like the variational quantum eigensolver (VQE). However, in the context of VQE, numerous techniques have been developed to reduce the measurement overhead by joint measurement of multiple observable. In this work, we extend these approaches to the asymmetric expectation values required in MC-PQE and assess the performance of different techniques for various molecular systems of up to 12 qubits. Abstract: We present a universal logical gate set for finite-energy Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) qubits encoded in the harmonic motional states of trapped neutral atoms. A Transient Rydberg excitation-mediated atomic dipole-dipole interaction enables a controlled-Z gate without invoking the blockade mechanism. The gates operate under magic-trapping conditions, allowing continuous trapping throughout the gate sequence, thereby preserving the motional encoding without intermediate measurement or feedforward. We assess the experimental feasibility of the proposed protocols using neutral $^{88}\mathrm{Sr}$ atoms and obtain average gate fidelities of $0.986$ for single-qubit phase gates and $0.985$ for the controlled-Z gate at a finite squeezing parameter of $Δ=0.25$ (12 dB of squeezing), increasing to $0.997$ and $0.995$ respectively as $Δ$ reaches $0.1$. These results provide a route toward universal, measurement-free logical control of motional GKP qubits in neutral-atom architectures.

Abstract: Quantum cooling is important for reliable quantum computation but involves a trade-off between cooling performance and implementation resources. Although reservoir memory can improve particular aspects of cooling performance, the associated resource cost, particularly for circuit implementation, remains insufficiently understood. Here, we investigate how reservoir memory affects the trade-off between cooling performance quantified by the cooling-step count and geometric implementation cost in single-qubit heat-bath algorithmic cooling. Using a pseudomode mapping, we represent the non-Markovian damped Jaynes--Cummings dynamics by a repeated collision circuit and evaluate its geometric implementation cost. Using matrix-based simulations and an implementation on the ibm_kawasaki Heron r2 processor, we identify a trade-off: suppressing reservoir memory reduces the cooling-step count but generally increases the geometric protocol cost. Our work provides a resource-based perspective on reservoir engineering for algorithmic cooling.

Abstract: The contact process is a paradigmatic example of nonequilibrium dynamics, with broad applications ranging from chemistry to sociology. Its quantum counterpart, the quantum contact process (QCP), extends the classical model to include coherent processes. Despite sustained interest, the nature of the transition in the one-dimensional (1D) QCP remains debatable. Here, combining Liouvillian spectral analysis, the tensor jump method, exact quantum jump Monte Carlo, and truncated Wigner simulations, we show that 1D QCP undergoes a continuous absorbing-state phase transition, with critical exponents distinct from the classical case. We further find Liouvillian gap closes well below the critical point, highlighting that spectral gap analysis alone cannot distinguish a phase transition from metastability in the QCP. Crucially, the 1D QCP is weakly entangled, yet even this weak entanglement is indispensable for capturing the correct critical behavior, whereas semiclassical methods artificially stabilize the active state and predict a spurious first-order transition. Our work establishes the quantum origin of the phase transition in the 1D QCP and underscores the essential role of entanglement in dissipative quantum many-body systems.

Method: 本文首先讨论正交保持操作（OPOs），给出简洁证明表明所有OPOs都是等距嵌入，由酉算子/等距算子的组合与附加固定态构成。基于此，进一步证明所有FPOs要么是到某个固定平坦态的常数映射，要么是OPOs的一个特例（其中附加的固定态必须为平坦态）。此外还分析了平坦态集合上平坦凸组合的结构约束。

Result: 给出了OPOs的完整刻画（等距嵌入结构），进而完全刻画了FPOs（常数映射或特殊OPO），并证明了平坦态的平坦凸组合仅可能由正交平坦态按照其纯度作为权重组合而成。

Conclusion: 本文解决了反平坦性资源理论中自由操作（FPOs）的识别问题，为建立完整的反平坦性量子资源理论奠定了关键基础。该刻画不仅阐明了FPOs的数学结构，也为后续研究反平坦态的转化、度量等资源理论核心问题提供了前提。

Abstract: A quantum state is called flat if it is proportional to a projector. There has been recent interest in studying antiflatness, the property of diverging from flat states, and establishing a resource theory for it. Identifying the free operations (the Flatness-Preserving Operations (FPOs)) remained an open problem. For this purpose, we first discuss Orthogonality-Preserving Operations (OPOs), of which trivial examples are unitary operations in an isolated system. More generally, we give a simple proof that all OPOs are isometric embeddings consisting of combinations of unitaries/isometries and appending a fixed state. We then show that all FPOs are either constant maps to some fixed flat state or a special case of an OPO, where the appended fixed state must be a flat state. We also show that the only possible flat convex combinations of flat states are those of orthogonal flat states with weights given by their purities.

</details>

At a representative asymmetric operating point informed by prior experiment, we show numerically that matching DAHR's single-photon-pair performance requires two to three rounds of BBPSSW purification. Abstract: Localization-delocalization transitions have recently been proposed for building a class of efficient quantum many-body critical sensors. The crossover length decreases with increasing temperature. We then consider imperfections in the lattice hopping strengths and find a qualitatively similar crossover. There, the quantum-enhanced regime, identified with super-extensive scaling, gives way to an extensive scaling-a classical-limited weaker form-at sufficiently large system sizes. Thus, distinct noise mechanisms lead to a common limitation on the scalability of quantum-enhanced sensing: increasing the probe size beyond a noise-dependent limit can destroy the metrological quantum advantage.

Abstract: Markovian open quantum systems are well modeled by the Lindblad Master Equation (ME) $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} ρ_t = \mathcal{L} ρ_t$, where $\mathcal{L}$ is a linear (super-)operator and $ρ_t$ is the system state, a positive matrix. When designing or characterizing a quantum system, one is usually interested in the steady state $ρ_\infty$ (such that $\mathcal{L} ρ_\infty = 0$), the first few excited states, and trajectories $t\mapsto ρ_t$. In finite dimension, $ρ_t$ is an $n\times n$ matrix, $\mathcal{L}$ thus typically costs $n^4$ to store explicitly as a dense matrix, and $O(n^6)$ to diagonalize or invert exactly, making standard linear algebraic techniques expensive for large systems. However, $\mathcal{L}$ usually costs only $O(n^3)$ to apply. This makes iterative methods appealing, but they do not work without a good preconditioner. In this article, our main observation is that a part of the Lindblad equation, corresponding to the so-called no-jump evolution $\mathcal{S}$, can be inverted efficiently. Using this inverse map, we introduce an auxiliary completely positive trace-preserving (CPTP) map $Φ$ whose fixed point is directly related to $ρ_\infty$, all the other eigenvalues having smaller magnitude. The map $Φ$ is thus well suited to iterative methods, and $ρ_\infty$ can be found in a few Arnoldi iterations. Using the same inverse map $\mathcal{S}^{-1}$ as preconditioner, we compute the low-lying spectrum efficiently via shift-invert Arnoldi, and, as a proof of concept, build an implicit time integrator that is competitive on stiff systems in the low-precision regime. For the steady-state and low excited states problems, our methods scale like $O(n^3)$ per iteration and offer state-of-the-art performance on CPU and GPU.

</details>


### [76] [Constrained minimax approximation for quantum signal processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30937)
*Yulong Dong,James B. Larsen,Lin Lin,Rahul Sarkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子信号处理（QSP）框架中经典设计阶段的约束极小极大逼近问题。该问题可视为一个带半无限约束的优化问题。文章提出了两种方法解决离散化导致的可行性不足：Remez交换法（结合主动约束集）在多数实例上高效但不稳定；随后引入非线性Fourier回缩方法，利用QSP补全和相位合成将近似可行多项式转化为可行QSP多项式且不增加度。回缩方法在代表性问题中保持近似精度，且在Remez启发式不稳定的实例上仍然有效，最终集成于qsppack软件包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QSP的经典设计阶段需要求解一个带半无限约束（在[0,1]整个定义域上幅值不超过1）的极小极大多项式逼近问题。离散化将该问题转化为线性规划，但有限采样点的可行性无法保证全域可行性，尤其是当最优逼近器达到可行集边界时。这一困难需要更稳健的求解方法。

Method: 作者探索了两种方法：(1) Remez交换方法结合主动约束集（active-set）强制执行，在多数测试实例上高效，但稳定性取决于目标函数和问题几何结构；(2) 非线性Fourier回缩方法，利用QSP补全（completion）和相位合成（phase synthesis），将近似可行的多项式转化为满足幅值约束的可行QSP多项式，且不增加多项式度。

Result: 在代表性问题上，非线性Fourier回缩方法在很大程度上保持了逼近精度，并且在Remez启发式不稳定的实例上仍然有效，表现出更强的鲁棒性。两种方法均已在qsppack软件包中实现。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典极小极大逼近与半无限优化和非线性Fourier分析联系起来，提供了从近似可行多项式到严格可行QSP多项式的系统性转换途径，为QSP设计提供了更可靠的数值流程。

Abstract: Quantum signal processing (QSP) provides a simple and efficient framework for implementing polynomial transformations using quantum circuits. Its classical design stage leads to a constrained minimax approximation problem: find a polynomial of prescribed parity that approximates a target function uniformly on a fitting set while remaining bounded in magnitude by one on the domain $[0,1]$, which can be viewed as a semi-infinite constraint. Discretization converts the problem into a linear program, but feasibility at a set of finitely many sampled points does not ensure feasibility on the whole domain, especially when an optimal approximant reaches the boundary of the feasible set. We investigate two approaches to address this difficulty. A Remez exchange method combined with active-set constraint enforcement is efficient on many tested instances, but its stability depends on the target and problem geometry. We then introduce nonlinear Fourier retraction, which uses QSP completion and phase synthesis to turn a nearly feasible polynomial into phase factors for a feasible QSP polynomial without increasing the degree. Across representative problems, retraction largely preserves approximation accuracy and remains effective on instances where the Remez heuristic is unstable. The resulting workflow connects classical minimax approximation and semi-infinite optimization with nonlinear Fourier analysis, and is implemented in the qsppack software package.

</details>

Abstract: Thermodynamics is operational in the sense that the very concept of thermal equilibrium depends crucially on the choice of observables, while the amount of extractable work is defined relative to the allowed operations. Here we show that isolated quantum many-body states admit a thermodynamic hierarchical structure of observables and allowed operations, where the same state can be thermal at one level of the hierarchy and athermal at another. Enlarging the set of allowed operations therefore renders such hidden athermality a potential resource for work extraction; the resulting work gain is bounded in terms of the difference between the entropy densities of the two levels. This entropy difference takes the form of the mutual-information density. We establish the bounds for three extensions of operational access: increased spatial resolution, increased duration of control, and nonlocal connectivity, which provide access to position-state Holevo information, correlations between neighboring regions and spatially nonlocal correlations, respectively. Thus the difference between entropies at different levels of the thermodynamic hierarchy governs the bound on the work gain associated with moving to a less restricted level.

The protocol is non-interactive and publicly verifiable with a classical verifier, and is based on Yamakawa and Zhandry's proof of quantumness [JACM'24]. We prove unconditional security of this protocol against adversaries making subexponentially-many adaptive quantum queries to the random oracle.
  Prior work on certified randomness relative to a random oracle additionally assumed the Aaronson--Ambainis conjecture or proved security only against low query-depth adversaries.

</details>


### [84] ["Train classical, deploy quantum" requires rethinking generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31117)
*Snehal Raj,Natansh Mathur,Alejandro Perdomo-Ortiz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，量子生成模型使用矩匹配损失（如MMD²）训练时，虽然能训练成功且采样被认为在经典计算上难以复现，但其泛化能力系统性地不如基于似然训练（likelihood-trained）的模型。作者在高达30量子比特的基数约束数据集和基因组单核苷酸变异（SNV）数据集上进行了基准测试，结果表明收敛的矩匹配损失并不能可靠地衡量泛化性能，并质疑"经典训练-量子部署"这一工作流程的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究主要关注量子生成模型能否被训练、其采样是否对经典计算机困难，但对于最小化矩匹配目标能否得到一个真正泛化的模型——而非仅仅复现训练统计数据——仍缺乏认识。这是量子生成模型实际应用中不可回避的关键问题。

Method: 作者基准测试了一组广泛的量子和经典生成模型，采用直接采样的方式来评估泛化性能。对比两类训练范式：一类使用矩匹配损失（MMD² / Pauli-Z关联），另一类使用似然训练。实验在两个具有应用启发的数据集上进行：一是高达30个量子比特的基数约束数据集，二是基因组单核苷酸变异（SNV）数据集（其合法集合即观测数据本身）。

Result: 实验表明，采用矩匹配损失训练的模型在泛化能力上普遍逊于似然训练的模型。该结论在两个数据集（30量子比特的基数约束数据与SNV基因组数据）上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 收敛的矩匹配损失并不能作为泛化能力的可靠指标。对于"经典训练-量子部署"的工作流程，需要直接以泛化为目标的方法；但目前尚不清楚是应该设计更好的训练目标函数，还是必须改变模型架构本身。这一开放问题有待进一步探索。

Abstract: Generative models have become central across science and industry, from image and text synthesis to the design of molecules and materials. Quantum generative models are considered one of the most promising applications for quantum computers, since a quantum circuit naturally produces samples from the distribution it encodes, and for suitable circuits that distribution is believed to be hard for any classical computer to reproduce. A leading strategy trains these models on a classical computer and reserves the quantum device for generating samples at deployment. This is possible when the training loss can be evaluated on a classical computer. A prime example is the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD$^2$), a moment-matching loss that compares the model and the data through their Pauli-$Z$ correlations. Research so far has asked whether such models can be trained and whether their sampling is hard; whether minimizing such an objective yields a model that generalizes, rather than one that merely reproduces the training statistics, remains poorly understood. We benchmark a broad set of quantum and classical generative models by direct sampling and show that models trained with a moment-matching loss generally show worse generalization than the likelihood-trained models. We show this on two application-inspired datasets: first a cardinality-constrained dataset at up to $30$ qubits and second a dataset of genomic single-nucleotide variants, whose valid set is the observed data. These results indicate that a converged moment-matching loss is not a reliable measure of generalization, and that train-classical, deploy-quantum workflows will need approaches that target generalization directly, leaving open whether better training objectives suffice or whether the model architectures themselves must change.

</details>


### [85] [Engineering multi-photon dissipation with a dc-voltage-biased Josephson junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31154)
*Marco Paradina,Ambroise Peugeot,Roberto Negrin,Oscar Novat,Tristan Villain,Anil Murani,Jean-Loup Ville,Sébastien Jezouin,Raphaël Lescanne,Audrey Bienfait,Benjamin Huard*

TL;DR: 本文提出使用直流电压偏置的SQUID来工程化多光子耗散，替代传统的参数泵浦约瑟夫森耦合器方式，从而消除寄生非线性项，并成功实现了高Q腔中一到四个光子向损耗模式单光子转换的激活。

Method: 使用直流电压偏置的SQUID来工程化多光子耗散。由于直流偏置使得寄生项在时间平均中相互抵消，从而避免了传统参数泵浦方案中的谱外非线性问题。实验中激活了高Q模式中一、二或四个光子到损耗模式的单光子转换过程。

Result: 通过Wigner断层扫描技术表征了两光子耗散过程，证实了直流偏置约瑟夫森结可以作为储层工程的资源，并为猫态量子比特的稳定化提供了一条可行路径。

Conclusion: 直流电压偏置的约瑟夫森结为多光子耗散的实现提供了一种干净的替代方案，避免了寄生非线性项的干扰，并展示了其在玻色子量子比特（尤其是猫态量子比特）稳定化方面的应用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-photon dissipation -- a key resource for bosonic qubits -- is usually realized by parametrically pumping a Josephson coupler at the cost of spurious nonlinear terms. Here we instead engineer it using a dc-voltage-biased SQUID, such that these parasitic terms average out. We activate the conversion of one, two, or four photons of a high-Q mode into a single photon of a lossy mode. We characterize the two-photon dissipation by Wigner tomography, establishing dc-biased junctions as a resource for reservoir engineering and a viable route to cat-qubit stabilization.

</details>


### [86] [Bosonic codes from compact phase spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31156)
*David Roberts,Aaron Slipper,Alireza Parhizkar,Victor V. Albert,Mohammad Hafezi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了亏格为二的黎曼曲面上的玻色子量子纠错码的代数结构。作者将码字显式构造为自守形式，并在所有权重下解析地生成了完整的码空间塔。文章证明了一个基本的不可行定理：对于任何亏格大于一的曲面，稳定子群是非可遗的（non-amenable），导致稳定子哈密顿量存在严格正的能隙，因此不存在归一化的量子态能满足所有稳定子条件。这与标准GKP码形成鲜明对比——GKP码中稳定子群Z²的可遗性保证了任意精度的近似码字存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP)码作为连续变量量子纠错中的关键方案，其成功依赖于稳定子群Z²的可遗性。作者试图探索在更高亏格黎曼曲面上是否也能构造类似的玻色子量子纠错码，以及其代数结构是否支持拥有近似码字的GKP型纠错方案。这一探索对于理解量子纠错码的几何与群论基础具有重要意义。

Method: 作者采用代数与解析结合的方法：(1)在亏格为二的黎曼曲面上，将码字明确构造为自守形式；(2)通过解析方法在所有自守形式权重下系统地生成完整的码空间塔；(3)利用群论性质分析稳定子群的结构，从可遗性（amenability）角度证明稳定子哈密顿量必然具有严格正的能量间隙。

Result: 作者证明了一个核心的不可行定理：任何亏格大于一的黎曼曲面上，其稳定子群是非可遗的，这强制稳定子哈密顿量产生严格正的谱能隙。由于该正能隙的存在，不存在任何归一化的量子态能同时满足所有稳定子条件，即无法构造物理上有意义的近似码字。

Conclusion: 亏格大于一的曲面上的量子码在原则上无法支持GKP式的近似码字方案，这与亏格为一（环面）情况下稳定子群Z²的可遗性本质不同。该结果揭示了量子纠错码代数结构与曲面拓扑之间的深层联系，暗示GKP码的成功可能依赖于特定的几何/拓扑条件。

Abstract: We present the algebraic structure of bosonic quantum error-correcting codes on genus-two Riemann surfaces. We explicitly construct the code words as automorphic forms and analytically generate the full tower of code spaces at all weights. We prove a fundamental no-go theorem: for any genus greater than one, the stabilizer group is non-amenable, forcing a strictly positive spectral gap in the stabilizer Hamiltonian. Consequently, no normalizable quantum state can satisfy all stabilizer conditions. This sharply contrasts with standard Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes, where the amenability of the stabilizer group $\mathbb{Z}^2$ permits approximate code words with arbitrary precision.

</details>


### [87] [Efficient search for excitable zero-modes in constrained systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31165)
*Jean-Yves Desaules*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在动力学约束系统中高效寻找平移不变可激发零模（EZM）解析表达式的方法，该方法基于局域无约束哈密顿量的特征分解，并在一个装饰性Rydberg链上展示了其效力：直接产生位于零能量本征空间的连续矩阵乘积态流形，该流形的跨度随系统指数增长，且在奇数系统尺寸下覆盖整个零动量零能量本征空间。此外，该方法还允许推导非零动量下的零模解析表达式以及E=±√3处多项式数量的精确量子多体疤痕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学约束系统（如 blockade 区域的 Rydberg 原子阵列）因其谱中存在非典型本征态而备受关注，表现为量子多体疤痕以及异常大的零模子空间。一些零模是可激发零模（EZM），可在开放边界条件下被提升到非零能量。然而，寻找这些EZM的解析表达式一直是一个挑战，作者希望提出一种系统高效的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 作者提出了一种基于局域无约束哈密顿量特征分解的协议：通过构造系统地寻找平移不变的EZM解析表达式。该方法被应用于一个装饰性Rydberg链模型，直接生成了一个位于周期边界条件下零能量本征空间中的连续矩阵乘积态（MPS）流形。作者还展示了该流形的物理结构（与局域本征基相关联）如何进一步用于推导非零动量零模和E=±√3处精确疤痕的解析表达式。

Result: 在装饰性Rydberg链中，该协议直接产生了一个连续矩阵乘积态流形，完全位于零能量本征空间（周期边界条件）。该流形的跨度随系统尺寸指数增长，且在奇数系统尺寸下覆盖了全部零动量零能量本征空间。此外，该方法还成功导出了非零动量下的ZM解析表达式，以及在E=±√3处多项式数量的精确疤痕态。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于局域特征分解的方法提供了一种系统高效的框架，用于在约束系统中发现和构造平移不变的EZM以及相关的量子多体疤痕，该方法在装饰性Rydberg链中展示了极强的适用性和解析能力。

Abstract: Kinetically constrained systems, such as those representing Rydberg atom arrays in the blockade regime, have gathered considerable attention due to the presence of atypical eigenstates in their spectrum. The latter manifests itself through the presence of quantum many-body scars as well as unusually large zero-mode (ZM) subspaces which contain analytically tractable eigenstates with various entanglement scalings. In particular, some of the latter are excitable zero-modes (EZMs), meaning that they can be promoted to a non-zero energy for open boundary conditions. In this work, I present an efficient protocol for finding analytical expressions for translation-invariant EZMs in constrained systems, based on the eigendecomposition of the local unconstrained Hamiltonian. I demonstrate the power of this method on a decorated Rydberg chain. In that model, my protocol directly produces a continuous matrix-product-state manifold located entirely in the zero-energy eigenspace for periodic boundary conditions. The span of that manifold grows exponentially with the system, and for odd system sizes it covers the entire zero-momentum eigenspace with zero energy. I then show how the physical structure of the manifold, which is tied to the local eigenbasis, also allows one to derive analytical expressions for ZMs at non-zero momentum and for a polynomial number of exact scars at $E=\pm\sqrt{3}$.

</details>

### [88] [Numerical simulation of a two-frequency-driven superlattice Faraday-wave pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31141)
*Debashis Panda,Nicolas Périnet,Abdullah M. Abdal,Lyes Kahouadji,Seungwon Shin,Jalel Chergui,Damir Juric,Omar K. Matar,Laurette S. Tuckerman*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过全三维Navier-Stokes方程自由表面直接数值模拟，研究了Arbell & Fineberg发现的SSS-I超晶格模式（双频驱动Faraday波），证明该模式在超临界25%强迫振幅下可从不同初始条件收敛到同一模式，且此后演化为动态漂移版本，验证了SSS-I模式的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSS-I超晶格模式此前仅被实验观测发现，其形成机制无法用弱非线性或弱粘性近似解释，缺乏理论及数值层面的验证，需要全三维数值模拟来揭示其动力学路径与结构本质。

Method: 对带自由表面的全三维Navier-Stokes方程进行直接数值模拟（DNS），在Faraday波起始阈值以上25%的强迫幅度下，使用两种不同的准六边形初始条件进行长时间演化（约250个强迫周期至稳态，额外100周期演化失稳过程）。通过空间Fourier分解剖析模式结构与六边形波及次谐波模的关系。

Result: 两种不同初始条件经不同路径（一种经历多个长寿命瞬态及不同六边形模式和另一超晶格模式，另一种经历杂散无序状态）最终均收敛到同一SSS-I超晶格模式；约100个强迫周期后该模式失稳，转变为动态版本，其中超晶格沿骨架方向整体调制漂移而基本形状得以保持。实验大几何结构及数值小几何结构中的收敛均证实了SSS-I的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SSS-I是双频Faraday波系统中一种鲁棒的自组织超晶格结构，其列状排布的斑块在时间上于"山丘"和"湖泊"间交替，由酷似DNA骨架的长链结构连接。该模式无法由弱非线性或弱粘性理论描述，且最终发展为保持形状的漂移动态版本，体现出丰富时空动力学特性。

Abstract: The formation of a superlattice pattern in two-frequency-driven Faraday waves discovered and named SSS-I by Arbell & Fineberg (1998, 2002) is investigated by means of Direct Numerical Simulations (DNS) of the full three-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations with a free surface. Two simulations with distinct quasi-hexagonal initial conditions run at a forcing amplitude $25\%$ above the Faraday-wave onset followed quite different routes, but both led eventually to the same superlattice pattern after around 250 forcing periods. This regime is inaccessible to the approximations of weak nonlinearity or viscosity. The standing-wave pattern contain rows of patches, alternating in time between hills and lakes that are connected by a long skeleton resembing the backbone of DNA strands. The patches and skeleton of the pattern can be related to its spatial Fourier decomposition, which combines hexagonal modes with a spatially and temporally subharmonic mode. One of the transition routes passes through several fairly long-lived transients including different hexagonal patterns and another superlattice pattern; the other passes only through erratic and disordered states. After another 100 periods, the pattern became unstable and was succeeded by a dynamic version of SSS-I in which the superlattice is modulated and drifts in the direction of the backbone, while preserving its basic shape. Convergence to SSS-I states both experimentally in a large geometry and numerically from two different initial conditions and in a minimal geometry demonstrates the robustness of the SSS-I pattern.

</details>


### [89] [Fluxes through a uniform double-diffusive staircase at low Prandtl number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28679)
*Arstanbek Tulekeyev,Pascale Garaud*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过三周期性域直接数值模拟，系统研究了τ≤Pr≪1条件下扩散型双扩散对流的统计稳定阶梯态。发现阶梯态可在远大于此前报道的密度比下存在，提出新的长寿命分层态判据（弱依赖于Ra）。确认中到大密度比下通量比γ近似恒定但弱依赖于Ra，并发现Nusselt数按Ra^{1/3}标度（区别于低R_ρ下的(RaPr)^{1/3}），基于界面边缘密度异常上升与湍流混合提出了新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双扩散对流的扩散型机制常呈现长寿命分层阶梯态，广泛存在于北极海洋、火山湖及巨行星内部。然而此前对低Prandtl数（Pr≪1）下阶梯态的存在范围与通量标度规律认识不足，缺乏统一的判据与物理模型，亟需系统性数值研究。

Method: 采用三周期性域中的直接数值模拟（DNS），系统变化密度比R_ρ与Rayleigh数Ra，覆盖τ≤Pr≪1的参数区间；测量总密度、温度与组分通量及通量比γ；分析Nusselt数的标度关系；并提出基于界面边缘密度异常上升及湍流混合的解析模型。

Result: （1）阶梯态可在显著更大的密度比R_ρ下存在，新判据对Ra依赖性微弱；（2）中到大R_ρ下通量比γ近似常数但弱依赖Ra；（3）组分与温度Nusselt数按Ra^{1/3}标度，不同于此前低R_ρ下的(RaPr)^{1/3}标度；（4）新提出的密度异常上升-湍流混合模型可较准确解释已有低Pr数据。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了低Prandtl数双扩散阶梯态的已知存在区间，修正了高密度比极限下的通量标度律，并提供了一个能统一解释数据的物理模型，对理解行星内部与海洋中的双扩散分层现象具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Double-diffusive convection in the diffusive regime is often observed to be in a long-lived layered state, well-known in the Arctic ocean and in volcanic lakes, and thought to exist in the interiors of giant planets. The density profile resembles a `staircase', with stacks of convective layers of uniform density separated by strongly stratified interfaces. The governing parameters are the Prandtl number $\mathrm{Pr}$, diffusivity ratio $τ$, density ratio $R_ρ$, and Rayleigh number $\mathrm{Ra}$. In this work, we perform direct numerical simulations in triply periodic domains to systematically investigate the properties of statistically stationary double-diffusive staircases with $τ\le \mathrm{Pr} \ll 1$. We demonstrate that they can exist for significantly larger density ratios than previously reported, and propose a new criterion for the existence of long-lived layered states. Notably, this criterion depends weakly on $\mathrm{Ra}$. In all simulations, we measure the total fluxes of density $F^{\mathrm{tot}}_ρ$, temperature $F^{\mathrm{tot}}_T$ and composition $F^{\mathrm{tot}}_C$, and the flux ratio $γ=F^{\mathrm{tot}}_C/F^{\mathrm{tot}}_T$. We confirm that $γ$ is approximately constant for moderate to large $R_ρ$ but also depends weakly on $\mathrm{Ra}$. We show that the Nusselt numbers for composition and temperature scale as $\mathrm{Ra}^{1/3}$ in this limit, which notably differs from the $(\mathrm{RaPr})^{1/3}$ scaling previously found at lower $R_ρ$. We propose a new model for these findings that is based on the rise and turbulent mixing of density anomalies that form at the edges of the interfaces. The model explains the existing low $\mathrm{Pr}$ data with reasonable accuracy.

</details>


### [90] [Internal heating in rapidly rotating convection is not a shortcut to geostrophic turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28756)
*Justin A. Nicoski,Tobias G. Oliver,Stefano Maffei,Michael A. Calkins*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用渐近约化的准地转模型，在Ekman数趋于零的极限下检验"内热驱动可作为进入扩散无关的地转湍流(GT)标度捷径"的假设。结果表明该捷径不存在：Nusselt数和Reynolds数并不比边界加热情形更接近扩散无关预测，Pr=7时甚至更远；预测的Pr^{-1/2}塌缩失效；前置因子依赖加热形式。作者进一步论证体输运标度(如Nu)不是GT的完整诊断指标，而内部平均温度梯度的饱和及垂直速度峰度的饱和才是可靠指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星与恒星中的对流湍流常由内部加热驱动，有研究提出这种强迫机制可以在适度强迫下进入地转湍流(GT)的扩散无关标度区。作者希望检验这一假设是否成立——即内热驱动是否真的能作为低强迫下到达GT区的"捷径"。

Method: 采用渐近约化的准地转模型(QG模型)，该模型在Ekman数趋于零的极限下形式有效，并保留Prandtl数作为独立参数。分别考虑无滑移/应力自由边界条件，在宽广的强迫范围内系统扫描约化Rayleigh数和Prandtl数，并与Hadjerci et al. (2024)的辐射驱动数值模拟逐例对照。

Abstract: Convective turbulence in planets and stars is often driven by internal heating. This forcing mechanism has also been proposed as a means of accessing the diffusion-free scalings of the geostrophic turbulence (GT) regime at modest forcing. We test this with the asymptotically reduced quasi-geostrophic model, which is formally valid in the limit of vanishing Ekman number, $Ek \rightarrow 0$, and retains the Prandtl number as an independent parameter. We find no such shortcut: the Nusselt and Reynolds numbers are no closer to their diffusion-free predictions than in the boundary-heated case, and at $Pr = 7$ they are further from them; the predicted $Pr^{-1/2}$ collapse fails; and the prefactor depends on the form of the heating. Our no-slip/stress-free cases track the radiatively driven simulations of Hadjerci et al. (2024) case by case, yet a $25\%$ variation in $Nu$ persists between sweeps that share the same reduced Rayleigh and Prandtl numbers but differ in Ekman number. Those data span only the narrow range of forcing over which the compensated Nusselt number is stationary; across the wider range accessible to the reduced model it rises to a maximum and then falls. The similarity solutions that govern each pinch contain none, the cascade of stretching and iterated satellites introduces none, and $\ell_μ$ belongs to the singularity rather than to the droplets. A size distribution built on such a process can inherit a scale from one place only, the cavity that launched the jet. Computations of bubble bursting, confined and unconfined, bear this out. The floor they display is the one the mesh imposes, not a property of the fluid, and wherever the mesh looks below $\ell_μ$ it finds droplets. The census of unique emitted droplets is exponential, with a single scale set by the cavity radius, indifferent both to how vigorous the event is and to how much liquid surrounds it. An exponential is the least structured census compatible with a prescribed mean, so the fragmentation keeps the size of the cavity and no other memory of its parent. Sustained stretching does keep such a memory, imprinting the corrugation of the parent thread and yielding the peaked, gamma-like distributions reported for ligament-mediated fragmentation. </details>

TL;DR: 本文提出将矩阵乘积态（MPS）这一张量网络方法作为降阶建模框架，用于压缩各向同性湍流数据。通过对三维不可压缩流动及其守恒被动标量场进行MPS编码（基于截断奇异值分解），在仅用5%（速度场）和15%（标量场）原始内存的情况下实现了99.8%的重建保真度，且各阶统计量（动能、耗散率、结构函数等）均得到良好恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流直接数值模拟（DNS）数据量极其庞大，存储和传输成本高昂，亟需高效的降阶建模与数据压缩方法。张量网络（TN）框架在量子多体系统模拟中展现出强大的高维场近似能力，但将其用作湍流数据的约化建模框架尚未得到充分探索。本文旨在验证MPS张量网络对复杂湍流数据压缩重建的可行性与适用性。

Method: 选取两个DNS数据集：三维不可压缩流体力学场及其中的守恒Fickian标量场。首先对输运变量的空间张量索引采用交错排序（interleaved ordering），以主导张量间的相关性。随后将每个场通过一系列奇异值分解（SVD）编码为MPS，在此过程中丢弃小奇异值实现截断。将截断后的表示收缩回全网格，重建的场与DNS结果进行对比，并系统检验低阶和高阶统计量（速度梯度、耗散、结构函数等）。

Result: 交错索引排序有效增强了互张量相关性定位。速度场重建在仅使用5%原始DNS内存时达到99.8%的保真度，标量场在同一保真度下需15%内存。在此压缩水平下，总动能和标量能量恢复的相对误差均低于0.2%；平均耗散率与平均标量耗散率与DNS值的偏差保持在约10%以内。

Conclusion: MPS方法在极高压缩率下仍能保持湍流高阶统计量的精度，证明了其作为可扩展降阶分析工具用于复杂湍流数据的适用性，并为张量网络方法在计算湍流领域的进一步探索提供了有力动机。

Abstract: Tensor networks (TNs), originally developed for simulating many-body quantum systems, provide a systematic framework for approximating high-dimensional fields. This is achieved by factorizing the field into interconnected tensors with small bond dimensions, thereby restricting the correlations captured across field bipartitions. Belonging to the family of TNs, the matrix product state (MPS) ansatz is utilized here as a reduced-order modeling framework to construct truncated representations of isotropic turbulent flow data. Two direct numerical simulation (DNS) datasets are considered: the hydrodynamic field of an incompressible three-dimensional flow, and a conserved Fickian scalar in a similar flow. Each field is encoded as an MPS through a sequence of singular value decompositions (SVDs) in which small singular values are discarded. The truncated representation is contracted back to the full grid, and the resulting reconstructed field is compared against DNS. An interleaved ordering of the spatial tensor indices of the transport variables is applied prior to decomposition in order to localize the dominant inter-tensor correlations. Velocity reconstructions achieve $99.8\%$ fidelity using only $5\%$ of the original DNS memory, while the scalar field reaches the same fidelity at $15\%$ memory usage. A wide range of lower- and higher-order statistics, including velocity gradients, dissipation, and structure functions, are systematically examined. At these compression levels, the total kinetic energy and the scalar energy are both recovered within $0.2\%$ relative error, while the mean dissipation and mean scalar dissipation remain within approximately $10\%$ of the DNS generated values. These findings support the suitability of MPS for scalable reduced-order analysis of complex turbulent datasets and motivate further exploration of TN-based methods in computational turbulence.

### [93] [NeuralFlowNet: Towards Data-Free Physics-Informed Neural Network Solutions of Navier-Stokes Equations Across Low and High Reynolds Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28935)
*Jayanga T. Samarasinghe,Luis De la Fuente,Laura Alvarez*

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeuralFlowNet，一种无数据、物理信息驱动的概念验证框架，能够在不依赖外部流场数据的情况下求解稳态Navier-Stokes问题，覆盖从低到高雷诺数范围，并在具有强压力梯度的复杂基准问题上与参考数值解高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI流场模型依赖大量仿真或实验数据且通常针对特定问题，泛化能力和物理可靠性受限；传统物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在高雷诺数条件下表现不佳，限制了其在CFD中的实际应用。因此需要一种无数据、物理一致且能处理高雷诺数流动的求解框架。

Method: NeuralFlowNet采用无数据的物理信息神经网络框架（data-free PINN），直接从Navier-Stokes方程和预设边界条件中学习流场解，无需外部仿真或实验数据集。该框架通过嵌入物理方程到网络学习过程中来保持物理一致性，并针对高雷诺数条件进行了方法层面的设计改进。

Result: 在物理和几何复杂度逐步增加的多个基准问题上，NeuralFlowNet能够在广泛的雷诺数范围内准确恢复稳态流场，包括具有强压力梯度的工况，且无需外部流场数据训练，与参考数值解保持良好的一致性。

Conclusion: NeuralFlowNet被确立为一种可靠的物理信息模拟框架，为未来非定常和更复杂仿真的测试奠定了基础，并为开发可信、高效的高雷诺数流体动力学AI求解器提供了依据。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a compelling pathway toward trustworthy artificial-intelligence-based computational fluid dynamics (CFD) by embedding governing equations directly into the learning process. Many existing AI flow models require large simulation or experimental datasets and often remain problem-specific, limiting their generalization and physical reliability. Data-free PINN frameworks offer an alternative by learning flow solutions from the Navier-Stokes equations and prescribed boundary conditions, potentially reducing dependence on expensive CFD datasets while retaining physical consistency. However, traditional PINN frameworks have struggled at high Reynolds numbers, limiting their application in CFD. In this work, we present NeuralFlowNet, a data-free, physics-informed proof-of-concept framework designed to solve steady Navier-Stokes problems across low- to high-Reynolds-number conditions. We describe the proposed methodological framework and demonstrate its applicability using benchmark problems with increasing physical and geometric complexity. The results demonstrate that NeuralFlowNet can accurately recover steady flow fields across a broad Reynolds-number range, including cases with strong pressure gradients, without training on external flow-field data and while maintaining good agreement with reference numerical solutions. These findings establish NeuralFlowNet as a reliable framework for future testing of unsteady and more complex simulations and could provide a basis for developing trustworthy and efficient AI solvers for high-Reynolds-number fluid dynamics.

</details>

*Shubhangi Bansude,Jay Patel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文综述了物理引导机器学习（PGML）在湍流燃烧中湍流-化学相互作用（TCI）建模中的应用，系统阐述了PGML如何通过整合先验物理知识（训练数据、模型架构、损失函数、混合闭合结构与求解器感知验证）来克服纯数据驱动模型在物理一致性与外推能力上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流燃烧的预测性模拟面临巨大挑战：刚性化学动力学与湍流输运、分子混合及热释放之间在未解析尺度上存在非线性耦合。在滤波/平均形式下，反应速率项不闭合，详细化学机理的积分构成主导计算成本。传统闭合模型（混合限制模型、火焰面/流形方法、条件矩闭合及输运PDF/FDF格式）依赖结构假设，在标定范围外可能失效。而纯数据驱动模型可能违反守恒律和热化学一致性、外推能力差且可能使CFD求解器失稳，因此需要物理引导的机器学习方法。

Method: 本文采用综述形式，提出并系统梳理了PGML用于TCI建模的整体框架，将物理先验知识的引入贯穿建模全流程的五个层面：训练数据与输入特征构建、神经网络模型架构设计、损失函数中嵌入物理约束、混合闭合结构（将局部物理模型与数据驱动组件结合），以及求解器感知的验证协议。

Result: 文章系统性地评述了PGML在TCI建模中的各类实现方法，展示了数据驱动能力如何在保持守恒律与热化学一致性的前提下用于放松受限的闭合假设、学习未解析尺度的非线性映射，并显著加速详细化学机理的计算。

Conclusion: PGML通过将先验物理知识系统地嵌入建模管线的各个环节，提供了一条兼顾数据驱动力和物理可解释性、鲁棒性与泛化能力的可行路径，有望成为湍流燃烧TCI建模的重要发展方向。

Abstract: Predictive simulation of turbulent combustion remains challenging because stiff chemical kinetics, turbulent transport, molecular mixing, and heat release interact nonlinearly across unresolved scales. In the filtered or averaged formulations, reaction rate is unclosed, and the integration of detailed chemistry constitutes the dominant computational cost. Conventional closures such as mixing-limited models, flamelet and manifold methods, conditional moment closure, and transported probability/filtered density function (PDF/FDF) formulations, encode substantial physical insight, but rely on structural assumptions that may lose validity outside their calibrated regimes. Machine learning offers a complementary approach because of its ability to approximate complex nonlinear functions. In turbulent combustion, this capability can be used to relax restrictive closure assumptions, learn unresolved nonlinear mappings from data, and accelerate expensive computations such as detailed chemistry computation. However, purely data-driven models may violate conservation laws and thermochemical consistency. They may also extrapolate poorly outside the training domain and destabilize the CFD solvers in which they are embedded. Physics-guided machine learning (PGML) addresses these failure modes by incorporating prior knowledge throughout the modeling pipeline: in the training data and input features, the model architecture, the loss function, the hybrid closure structure, and the solver-aware validation protocol. This article reviews PGML for turbulence-chemistry interaction (TCI) modeling within this framework.

</details>

Abstract: Operator-learning surrogates have been benchmarked largely on single-field, single-interface problems, leaving unclear whether architectural choices validated in those settings transfer to constrained, multiphase flows. SEDONet reduces the test relative L2 error by 16.8% compared with FEDONet and by 24.0% compared with DeepONet, while improving all five output channels. Spatial and temporal error analyses localize the principal gains near the diffuse interfaces and after bubble breakthrough. The results indicate that the Chebyshev representation is particularly effective for the strongly non-periodic wall-normal and temporal structure of this three-phase flow.

</details>

Abstract: Three-dimensional conjugate simulations were conducted to investigate saturated flow boiling in a rectangular mini-channel, with particular emphasis on the role of inlet Reynolds number on boiling mode selection and transition. The global heat transfer characteristics and peak heat transfer capacity are further interpreted in conjunction with boiling mode transition, clarifying the respective roles of wall dryout and volumetric vapor fraction in heat transfer deterioration. Among all cases, Re=2000 provides the most favorable overall thermal response. Overall, within the rectangular mini-channel configuration and operating range considered in this study, Re is closely associated with vapor organization, boiling transition, wall dryout, and global heat transfer performance.

*Ulises Rosales-Gallegos,Laura Oropeza-Ramos,Francisco Manuel Sánchez-Arévalo,Miguel A. Quetzeri-Santiago*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了20%硅油水包油乳液液滴在倾斜55°的平滑玻璃和两种间距（50和100μm）的微柱阵列表面上的撞击动力学，涵盖不同润湿性（含超疏水涂层）和宽范围韦伯数（18≤We≤565）。识别出五种撞击模式，发现表面倾斜、微结构和乳液分散相共同调控铺展、回缩、润湿和破碎行为，证明乳液撞击行为无法仅由传统无量纲数预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液滴撞击通常研究的是水平光滑表面，而实际应用（如农用喷雾和涂层技术）常涉及倾斜且有微结构的表面，同时乳液型液滴的撞击行为比纯液滴更为复杂。三项因素（表面倾斜、微结构、流体组成）对乳液撞击的综合影响此前缺乏系统理解。

Method: 实验制备20%（v/v）硅油在水中的乳液液滴，撞击平滑玻璃和两种间距（50和100μm）的微柱阵列，表面倾斜55°；部分样品涂覆超疏水涂层以考察润湿性影响。利用高速成像表征撞击动力学（18≤We≤565），使用光学显微镜量化撞击后的残留润湿面积。

Result: 识别出五种撞击模式：沉积、滑动、部分回弹、完全回弹和飞溅。表面倾斜导致铺展与回缩的不对称；微结构通过减小液固接触面积降低粘附并促进回弹。与纯水相比，乳液在光滑表面更易沉积，在无涂层微结构上更易滑动，在高We数下更早产生液丝及二次破碎。分散油相局部改变润湿和润滑，形成液桥和异质回缩；在微结构表面，毛细约束限制了投影润湿面积，但纹理内部滞留液体增多。乳液撞击行为不能仅由传统无量纲数预测，局部相再分布从根本上改变能量耗散、润湿和破碎。

Conclusion: 乳液在倾斜微结构表面的撞击行为受表面倾斜、几何微观结构和分散相局部分布的共同作用。研究揭示了乳液不同于纯液滴的撞击机制，为农用喷雾和倾斜纹理表面喷涂技术提供了新见解。

Abstract: Droplet impacts rarely occur on horizontal smooth surfaces, yet the combined influence of surface inclination, microstructure, and fluid composition remains poorly understood for emulsions. Here, we experimentally investigate the impact of 20% (v/v) silicone oil-in-water emulsion droplets on smooth glass and micropillar arrays with two interpillar spacings (50 and 100 $μ$m) inclined at 55$^\circ$. The role of surface wettability is further examined using a superhydrophobic coating. High-speed imaging is employed to characterise the impact dynamics over a wide range of Weber numbers ($18 \leq We \leq 565$), while optical microscopy is used to quantify the residual wetted area after impact. Five impact regimes are identified: deposition, sliding, partial rebound, complete rebound, and splashing. Surface inclination introduces asymmetry in spreading and recoil, whereas microstructured substrates reduce adhesion and promote rebound by decreasing the liquid-solid contact area. In contrast to water, the emulsion exhibit enhanced deposition on smooth surfaces, increased sliding on uncoated microstructures, and earlier ligament formation and secondary breakup at high Weber numbers. The dispersed oil phase locally modifies wetting and lubrication, producing liquid bridges, heterogeneous recoil, and larger residual wetted areas on smooth substrates. On microstructured surfaces, however, capillary confinement within the texture limits the projected wetted area despite increased liquid retention inside the microstructure. These results demonstrate that the impact behaviour of emulsions cannot be predicted solely from conventional dimensionless groups, as local phase redistribution fundamentally alters energy dissipation, wetting, and fragmentation. The findings provide new insight into spray deposition on inclined textured surfaces relevant to agricultural spraying and coating technologies.

</details>


### [98] [Competing instabilities in wide-gap viscoelastic Taylor-Couette flow: Taylor vs. helical vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29552)
*Alexander Proskurin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过直接数值模拟，针对同心圆柱间（内柱小半径旋转）聚合物流体流动的稳定性进行了非线性研究。流体运动采用Oskolkov模型（Kelvin-Voigt模型的特例，适用于极稀聚合物溶液）。研究发现：临界雷诺数与线性理论预测一致；随着弹性力增大，螺旋扰动比轴对称扰动更具危险性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究极稀聚合物溶液（由Oskolkov/Kelvin-Voigt模型描述）在同心圆柱旋转流动中的稳定性。传统线性理论只能预测失稳的起始条件，无法捕捉非线性演化行为。作者采用全非线性方法进行直接数值模拟，旨在验证线性理论结果并探索弹性力对失稳模态（轴对称vs螺旋）的影响。

Method: 采用Oskolkov模型（Kelvin-Voigt模型的特例，假设延迟时间远小于问题特征时间且弹性力远小于粘性力）描述极稀聚合物溶液的运动。在小半径内柱旋转的同心圆柱几何中进行直接数值模拟，以有限持续时间白噪声形式引入扰动，通过完全非线性方法考察稳态运动的稳定性。扫描不同Reynolds数以判别扰动衰减或增长。

Result: 牛顿流体和非牛顿流体的临界Reynolds数均与线性理论预测一致。研究发现弹性力的增强使得螺旋扰动（helical perturbations）比轴对称扰动（axisymmetric counterparts）更具危险性，即弹性效应改变最不稳定扰动的模态结构。

Conclusion: 直接数值模拟验证了线性稳定性理论对于该粘弹性流动的临界失稳预测的有效性；同时揭示了弹性力对扰动模态选择的影响——高弹性力条件下失稳模态由轴对称轉向螺旋结构，这加深了对聚合物溶液在Taylor-Couette流动中失稳机理的理解。

Abstract: This paper presents a numerical study of the stability of a polymer solution flow between concentric cylinders with a rotating inner cylinder. The case of a small-radius inner cylinder is considered. The fluid motion is described using a specific case of the Kelvin-Voigt model, often referred to as the Oskolkov model. This model is applicable to very dilute polymer solutions, where the retardation time is much smaller than the characteristic time of the problem and elastic forces are much smaller than viscous forces. The stability of the steady-state motion is investigated using a fully nonlinear approach by means of direct numerical simulation of a perturbation introduced as finite-duration white noise. Depending on the Reynolds number, the perturbation either decays or grows. The critical Reynolds numbers obtained for both Newtonian and non-Newtonian fluids are found to be in agreement with the predictions of the linear theory. It is also shown that an increase in the elastic forces makes helical perturbations more dangerous than their axisymmetric counterparts.

</details>


### [99] [Lagrangian particles and particle clusters in large-aspect-ratio turbulent Rayleigh-Bénard convection experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29729)
*Prafulla P. Shevkar,Mitanjali,Roshan J. Samuel,Christian Cierpka,Jörg Schumacher*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文在湍流Rayleigh-Bénard对流（Γ=10, Ra=9.3×10^7, Pr=4.8）中进行了拉格朗日粒子追踪实验，追踪约50,000个中性浮力粒子长达150个Kolmogorov时间尺度。通过足够长的轨迹捕获了从短时弹道区到长时扩散区的单粒子均方位移，并分析了速度结构函数和粒子对扩散。研究还通过主成分分析考察了粒子云的各向异性扩散行为，并通过图拉普拉斯的谱分析和k-means聚类识别粒子连接网络动力学，最后评估了加速度分量的概率密度函数及其间歇性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在深入理解湍流热对流的拉格朗日粒子运动特性，特别是覆盖从耗散尺度到对流尺度的完整运动学行为。该工作试图填补在大展弦比Rayleigh-Bénard对流中缺乏长时间、大样本拉格朗日测量的空白，从而系统性地刻画单粒子扩散、粒子对相对弥散、多粒子云形变以及加速度间歇性等关键统计特征。

Method: 采用拉格朗日粒子追踪实验方法，在展弦比Γ=10的Rayleigh-Bénard对流系统中，追踪约50,000个具有浮力中性粒子，持续时间达150个Kolmogorov时间。通过计算单粒子均方位移、拉格朗日速度结构函数和粒子对相对弥散来表征扩散行为；利用主成分分析（PCA）研究初始半径约8和20个Kolmogorov长度的粒子云的各向异性展开与形态演化；通过对粒子连接网络的图拉普拉算子进行谱分析并结合k-means聚类来识别粒子群动力学特征；最后统计三个加速度分量的概率密度函数及其二阶、四阶矩的收敛性以评估间歇性。

Result: 实验成功捕获了从短时弹道到长时扩散的完整单粒子扩散过程。在类Richardson弥散区，粒子对弥散指数超过3，尤其在水平（横向）方向更为显著。粒子云在横向方向较垂直方向扩散更强，初始球形云团在约10个Kolmogorov时间内变形为扁椭球体，展现明显的各向异性弥散。加速度统计分析显示，横向加速度分量比重直分量具有更重的尾部（即更高的间歇性），而基于体区（远离壁面）的条件统计比全量测量体积的统计间歇性略低，归因于壁面附近高度间歇的羽流喷发与聚集事件。

Conclusion: 该研究系统揭示了Rayleigh-Bénard湍流对流中拉格朗日粒子运动的多尺度时空特征：扩散呈现出从弹道到扩散的完整转变过程，粒子对弥散表现出超经典的各向异性行为（指数>3），多粒子云强烈各向异性形变，且横向方向的间歇性强于垂直方向。壁面羽流事件是增强间歇性的关键来源，这体现了大尺度热对流结构对拉格朗日湍流统计的显著影响。

Abstract: We perform Lagrangian particle-tracking experiments in turbulent Rayleigh-Bénard convection at aspect ratio Gamma = 10, Rayleigh number Ra = 9.3 x 10^7, and Prandtl number Pr = 4.8. Approximately 50,000 neutrally buoyant particles are tracked for durations of up to 150 Kolmogorov times. The resulting trajectories are sufficiently long to capture the mean-square displacement of single particles from the short-time ballistic regime to the long-time diffusive regime. We further analyse Lagrangian velocity structure functions and particle-pair dispersion, both of which exhibit scaling behavior consistent with theoretical expectations in their respective temporal regimes. In the Richardson-like regime, the pair-dispersion exponent exceeds 3, most clearly in the lateral direction. Multi-particle statistics are investigated by analysing particle clouds with initial radii of approximately 8 and 20 Kolmogorov lengths using principal component analysis. The clouds spread more strongly in the lateral directions than in the vertical direction and deform from initially spherical shapes into oblate ellipsoids within approximately 10 Kolmogorov times, revealing pronounced anisotropic dispersion. The dynamics is identified from the particle-connectivity network through a spectral analysis of the graph Laplacian followed by k-means clustering. Furthermore, we examine the probability density functions of all three acceleration components and assess the statistical convergence of their second and fourth moments. The lateral acceleration components are more intermittent than the vertical component, as indicated by their heavier tails. Acceleration statistics conditioned on the bulk region are slightly less intermittent than those obtained over the full measurement volume, which we attribute to the highly intermittent plume-ejection and plume-aggregation events occurring near the walls.

</details>


### [100] [Splashing-regime transitions and secondary-droplet scaling in oblique drop impacts on a deep pool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29762)
*Ying Xiong,Yang Zhang,Lingling Xie,Xiaolei Li*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究斜向液滴撞击深液池的行为，覆盖Weber数41-1790和倾角10°-90°，识别出五种后撞击形态（沉积、前飞溅、侧飞溅、侧前飞溅和冠飞溅），建立了基于切向惯性参数Ks和法向Weber数的形态转变判据，并揭示次级液滴破碎遵循统一的毛细-惯性标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斜向液滴撞击液池会产生不对称的冠形、定向射流和飞溅转变，这些现象无法仅用总撞击惯性来表征。理解斜向撞击的物理机制对于喷雾冷却、农药喷洒、喷墨打印等工业应用至关重要，但此前缺乏系统的数值研究来揭示倾斜角度与Weber数共同作用下的形态转变规律和碎裂标度。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法，研究水液滴撞击静止深液池的行为，参数范围为41≤We≤1790（Weber数）和10°≤θ≤90°（撞击倾角）。通过系统地扫描We-θ参数平面，识别不同的后撞击形态区域，并利用切向惯性参数K_s=We·cosθ和法向Weber数We_N对形态转变进行定量表征。此外，通过液滴尺寸分布统计和二阶速度结构函数分析方法研究次级液滴的碎裂动力学。

Result: 模拟复现了实验观察到的主要特征，在We-θ平面识别出五种形态：沉积、前方飞溅、侧面飞溅、侧前方飞溅和冠飞溅。沉积-前方飞溅转变由切向惯性参数K_s=We·cosθ描述，临界值K_s^c≈120；前方至侧前方飞溅转变由法向撞击惯性主导，临界法向Weber数We_N^c≈318。次级液滴尺寸分布呈正偏态，中位直径满足d_s,med/D~We^{-3/5}；二阶速度结构函数支持尺度依赖的毛细-惯性碎裂描述，结合质量守恒给出N_s~We^{9/5}的次级液滴数量标度。

Conclusion: 定向撞击惯性（切向和法向分量）主导飞溅形态的宏观选择：切向惯性决定下游冠缘惯性vs.毛细回缩（Taylor-Culick速度）的竞争，从而控制沉积-飞溅转变；法向惯性则控制飞溅模式的转变。而不同飞溅形态下产生的次级液滴群体展现出统一的毛细-惯性碎裂标度律，表明宏观形态选择与微观碎裂动力学由不同的物理机制分层控制。

Abstract: Oblique drop impact onto a deep liquid pool produces asymmetric crowns, directional jetting, and splashing transitions that cannot be characterized by the total impact inertia alone. We numerically investigate water drops impacting a quiescent deep pool over $41\leq We\leq1790$ and $10^\circ\leqθ\leq90^\circ$. The simulations reproduce the principal features observed experimentally and identify five post-impact regimes in the $We$--$θ$ plane: deposition, front splashing, side splashing, side-front splashing, and crown splashing. The deposition--front-splashing transition is described by the tangential-inertial parameter $K_s=We\cosθ$, with $K_s^c\approx120$. This criterion follows from the competition between downstream crown-rim inertia and capillary retraction at the Taylor--Culick velocity. The transition from front to side-front splashing is instead governed primarily by normal impact inertia, with a critical normal Weber number $We_N^c\approx318$. Beyond these regime transitions, the secondary-droplet statistics reveal fragmentation behavior common to the different splashing regimes. The droplet-size distributions are positively skewed, and the median diameter follows $d_{s,\mathrm{med}}/D\sim We^{-3/5}$. Second-order velocity structure functions support a scale-dependent capillary--inertial description of rim and ligament breakup. Combined with mass conservation, this scaling gives $N_s\sim We^{9/5}$, providing a numerical explanation for the secondary-droplet-number scaling observed experimentally. Thus, directional impact inertia governs the macroscopic selection of splashing regimes, whereas the secondary-droplet populations across these regimes exhibit a common capillary--inertial fragmentation scaling.

</details>


### [101] [Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold networks for viscoelastic fluid equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29895)
*Suryanshu Singh,Midhuna Suresh,Akanksha Gupta*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（PI-KAN）框架，用于求解粘弹性流体方程的前向问题。该框架基于广义流体动力学模型（适用于尘埃等离子体领域），以Taylor-Green流动及其修正形式为基准，系统研究了不同网络架构、超参数、配点分布和随机种子初始化对求解精度与收敛性的影响（粘弹性参数τ_m范围1-20），为PI-KAN在粘弹性流体方程中的设计与实现提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性流体方程具有强非线性流体场耦合特性，是复杂流体动力学应用中的关键挑战。传统多层感知机（MLP）在求解此类物理方程时存在可解释性不足和精度瓶颈。KANs基于Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理，通过边上的可学习激活函数替代固定节点激活，提供了MLPs的可解释替代方案，但其在粘弹性流体方程中的适用性尚未被系统研究。

Method: 提出PI-KAN框架用于求解粘弹性流体方程的前向问题，其中采用广泛用于尘埃等离子体领域的广义流体动力学模型来刻画粘弹性流体方程。选取Taylor-Green流动及修正Taylor-Green流动作为基准问题，系统研究不同网络架构、超参数设置、配点分布以及随机种子初始化对求解精度和收敛行为的影响，覆盖粘弹性参数τ_m从1到20的范围。

Result: 通过对不同网络架构、超参数、配点分布和随机种子初始化的系统消融研究，获取了PI-KAN在求解粘弹性流体方程时的精度与收敛性表现，涵盖了粘弹性参数τ_m=1-20的广泛范围，揭示了各因素对训练效果的影响规律。

Conclusion: 研究成果为设计和使用物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（PI-KAN）求解粘弹性流体方程提供了有价值的指导性建议，有助于该领域研究人员合理选择网络架构和训练配置以实现最优求解效果。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs), inspired by the Kolmogorov Arnold representation theorem, provide an interpretable alternative to multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) by using learnable activation functions on edges rather than fixed node activations. We propose a Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (PI-KAN) framework for solving forward problem of viscoelastic fluid equations, which arise in many complex fluid dynamics applications and are characterized by strong nonlinear coupling between fluid fields. For viscoelastic fluid equations, we adopt the generalized hydrodynamic model, which is well established in the field of dusty plasma. To evaluate the performance of the proposed framework for viscoelastic fluid, we consider benchmark problem based on the Taylor-Green (TG) flow and a modified Taylor-Green flow. We systematically investigate the effects of different network architectures, hyperparameters, and collocation point distributions on the accuracy and convergence behavior of PI-KANs for the range of viscoelastic parameter ($τ_m = 1$--$20$). We also study the impact of random seed initialization on training outcomes. The obtained results provide useful guidance for the design and implementation of physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (PI-KANs) in solving viscoelastic fluid equations

</details>


### [102] [Data-Driven Design Optimization of Streaming-Potential-Mediated Electrokinetic Transport of Viscoelastic Fluids in Microchannels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29939)
*Ankan Basu,Sumanta Banerjee*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn
