TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于代理模型的框架，用于优化狭缝微通道中Phan-Thien-Tanner黏弹性流体压力驱动电动力输运的设计。通过生成高保真数值数据库并训练机器学习代理模型来预测流动电势，结合多目标优化策略同时最大化能量转换效率和体积流量，显著加速了参数探索过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解析和半解析模型虽然提供了有价值的物理洞察，但在探索大规模设计空间和识别最优操作条件时需要重复的数值计算，效率较低。因此需要一种能够快速实现设计优化的方法。

Method: 首先，在广泛的无量纲参数范围内（包括Zeta电位、Debye参数、Dukhin数和黏弹性参数）生成高保真数值数据库；随后，训练机器学习代理模型以准确逼近控制参数与流动电势之间的非线性关系；基于代理预测的流动电势，通过闭式方程计算体积流量和水力发电能量转换效率；最后，结合多目标优化策略确定同时最大化能量转换效率和体积流量的操作条件。

Result: 所提出的方法相比跨参数重复数值模拟能够显著加速参数探索，能够识别出同时最大化能量转换效率和体积流量的最优操作条件，为电动力微流控设备提供了实用的设计指南。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了将计算流体力学与数据驱动的代理建模相结合用于高效工程设计优化的潜力。

Abstract: Streaming-potential-mediated transport of viscoelastic fluids has attracted research attention owing to its applications in electrokinetic energy conversion and microfluidic transport. Existing analytical and semi-analytical models in published literature provide valuable physical insights, but require repeated numerical evaluations for exploring large design spaces and identifying the optimal operating conditions. In this work, a surrogate-assisted framework is developed for rapid design optimization of pressure-driven electrokinetic transport of simplified Phan-Thien-Tanner fluids in a slit microchannel. A high-fidelity numerical database is generated over a broad range of governing dimensionless parameters, which includes the zeta potential, the Debye parameter, the Dukhin number, and the viscoelastic parameter. A Machine Learning surrogate model is subsequently trained to accurately approximate the nonlinear relationship between the governing parameters and the streaming potential, while the volumetric flow rate and hydroelectric energy conversion efficiency were calculated from closed form equation by using the streaming potential predicted by the surrogate. This is coupled with a multi-objective optimization strategy to identify operating conditions that simultaneously maximize energy conversion efficiency and volumetric flow rate. The proposed methodology can significantly accelerate parametric exploration compared with repeated numerical simulations across different parameters and provides practical design guidelines for electrokinetic microfluidic devices. The study demonstrates the potential of combining computational fluid mechanics with data-driven surrogate modeling for efficient engineering design and optimization.

</details>


### [103] [Solute dispersion in magnetically influenced multiphase flow through a porous tube: axial transport and microrotational effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29946)
*Sohel Ahmed,Nanda Poddar,Jyotirmoy Rana,Kajal Kumar Mondal,Niall Madden*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过理论推导和Brownian动力学模拟验证，构建了包含微极流体核心、Newtonian血浆外围及Brinkman/Darcy多孔层的两相磁流体动力学管流模型，研究了广义溶质扩散在多孔层动脉管流中的行为，揭示了微旋转动力学对溶质输运的先前未被探索的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物流体和环境流体动力学中，溶质在多孔层动脉管流中的传播行为尚未被充分理解，特别是在磁场、微极（非Newtonian）流体与多孔动脉结构三者耦合作用下，微旋转动力学对溶质输运的影响此前未被探索。作者希望填补这一理论空白，为磁性纳米颗粒介导的靶向药物递送等临床应用提供理论基础。

Method: 构建两流体解析模型：以代表红细胞旋转行为的微极流体为核心，以嵌入Brinkman和Darcy多孔结构的Newtonian血浆为外围（对应糖萼和内皮层），并引入横向磁场。基于Sankarasubramanian & Gill广义扩散框架推导解析解，研究耦合轴向速度场及微旋转动力学对溶质输运的影响；通过Brownian动力学模拟独立验证零阶和一阶输运矩随时间演化的解析预测。

Result: 解析预测与Brownian动力学模拟在零阶和一阶输运矩的时域演化上表现出极佳一致性。研究揭示了微旋转动力学对溶质浓度、对流传质系数和有效扩散系数的先前未被探索的影响，阐明了流体的平动与旋转运动在生物流体输运中的耦合作用。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了磁场、微极流体与多孔动脉结构在生物流体输运领域的理论空白，建立了一个统一的广义理论框架，为生物流体输运、靶向药物递送和临床工程应用提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: This study presents a theoretical investigation of generalized solute dispersion in magnetohydrodynamic multiphase tube flow with porous layers. A two-fluid analytical model is developed for applications in biofluid and environmental fluid dynamics. The model comprises a micropolar (non-Newtonian) fluid core representing the rotational behaviour of red blood cells and a Newtonian plasma periphery embedded with Brinkman and Darcy porous structures, corresponding to the glycocalyx and endothelial layers with distinct permeability characteristics. A transverse magnetic field is incorporated to investigate how magnetic-field-induced modifications of the carrier flow influence solute localisation, with potential relevance to magnetic nanoparticle-mediated drug delivery. Using the generalised dispersion framework of Sankarasubramanian & Gill, analytical solutions are derived to investigate how the coupled axial velocity field and associated microrotational dynamics influence solute transport. The analytical predictions are independently validated through Brownian dynamics simulations, demonstrating excellent agreement for the temporal evolution of the zeroth and first transport moments. The results reveal the previously unexplored influence of microrotational dynamics on solute concentration, convection coefficients and effective dispersion, providing new insights into the coupled roles of translational and rotational fluid motion in biofluid transport. This work bridges an important gap in the literature and establishes a generalized theoretical framework linking magnetic fields, micropolar fluids and porous arterial structures for biofluid transport, targeted drug delivery and clinical engineering applications.

</details>


### [104] [Coexisting chaos and order in micro-textured elastic flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30286)
*Giulio Foggi Rota,Ricardo Arturo Lopez de la Cruz,Simon J. Haward,Amy Q. Shen,Marco Edoardo Rosti*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟与微流体实验相结合，揭示了黏弹性流体在覆盖密集微柱阵列（如纤毛、绒毛等微纹理表面）的微纹理表面上流经时产生的复杂流动动力学——在柱冠上方自发形成弹性湍流，同时该混沌状态与弹性波共存，两者是黏弹性流体流动的互补表现形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黏弹性流体在微纹理表面（如气道纤毛或肠道绒毛）上的流动支撑着关键的生物过程（运输、混合、吸收），但流体弹性与复杂几何结构之间的相互作用动力学尚未被充分探索，这构成了研究的核心动机。

Method: 采用完全分辨的数值模拟与微流体实验相结合的方法，研究低雷诺数下黏弹性液体流过致密微柱阵列（冠层）上方所建立的流动动力学，覆盖广泛的流动条件与冠层构型。

Result: 观察到冠层顶端上方的流动自发发展为弹性湍流，同时该混沌状态与弹性波共存，后者源自流体弹性与冠层几何诱导的非均匀剪切之间的耦合；这种有序流体运动在广泛的流动条件和冠层构型下均持续存在。

Conclusion: 相干波传播与弹性湍流是黏弹性流体流动的互补表现。这些机制对生物及工程环境中的输运具有广泛意义，并揭示了结构化几何如何利用黏弹性流体的自发动力学来操控复杂流动。

Abstract: Viscoelastic fluid flows over micro-textured surfaces - densely covered by slender protrusions like cilia lining the body airways or villi covering the intestinal epithelium - underpin essential biological processes including transport, mixing, and absorption. Despite their ubiquity, the dynamics generated by the interplay between fluid elasticity and these complex geometries remain largely unexplored. Here we combine fully resolved numerical simulations with microfluidic experiments to reveal the flow dynamics established above dense arrays of microscopic pillars (canopies) immersed in the low-Reynolds-number flow of a viscoelastic liquid. We observe that the flow above the canopy tips spontaneously develops elastic turbulence. Remarkably, the chaotic state coexists with elastic waves emerging from the coupling between fluid elasticity and the heterogeneous shear induced by the canopy geometry. These ordered fluid motions persist across a broad range of flow conditions and canopy configurations. Our results demonstrate that coherent wave propagation and elastic turbulence are complementary manifestations of viscoelastic fluid flow. Beyond their fundamental significance, these mechanisms have broad implications for transport in biological and engineered environments, and reveal how structured geometries can harness the spontaneous dynamics of viscoelastic liquids to manipulate complex flows.

</details>


### [105] [A Physics-Informed, PIV-Based Method for Surface Pressure Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30432)
*Abbas Daliri,Fulvio Scarano*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息的PIV表面压力重构方法，该方法结合最小二乘优化与局部和全局物理约束，并通过表面法向压力梯度的标准差作为物理意义的权重函数，同时引入物理全局约束来重构吸力区外的表面压力。结果表明与压力孔数据吻合良好，前缘附近重构误差降低了三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于PIV（粒子图像测速）的表面压力重构通常精度有限，尤其是在前缘等关键区域，缺乏物理约束来保证重构结果的可靠性与准确性。因此需要一种融合物理知识的方法来提升压力重构的精度。

Method: 提出一种物理信息驱动的表面压力重构方法，其核心包括：(1) 将最小二乘优化与局部和全局物理约束相结合；(2) 引入表面法向压力梯度的标准差作为具有物理意义的加权函数；(3) 引入物理全局约束用于吸力区以外的表面压力重构。

Result: 重构结果与压力孔（pressure taps）实测数据吻合良好；在前缘附近，表面压力重构误差减少了三分之一。

Conclusion: 所提出的物理信息方法能够有效提高PIV表面压力重构的精度，尤其在前缘区域效果显著，验证了在优化过程中引入物理加权与全局约束的有效性。

Abstract: A physics-informed method for PIV-based surface pressure reconstruction that combines least-squares optimization with both local and global physical constraints is proposed. The standard deviation of the surface--normal pressure gradient is introduced as a physically meaningful weighting function in the optimization process. Moreover, a physical global constraint is also introduced to reconstruct surface pressure outside the suction region. The results show very good agreement with data obtained from pressure taps. Near the leading edge, the surface pressure reconstruction error has been reduced by one-third.

</details>


### [106] [Drag reduction and subcritical turbulence in controlled pipe flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30452)
*Emanuele Gallorini,Daniele Massaro,Philipp Schlatter,Maurizio Quadrio*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过直接数值模拟（DNS）研究了在雷诺数Re_b=4900下，管道流受流向行波方位角壁面速度控制的减阻行为。研究发现管道流的响应与通道流有根本性差异：适当强迫下管道流可实现层流化，而通道流则不能。受控流场可呈现过渡管道流特有的空间局域湍流状态（湍流泡），其可在未受控情况三倍高的雷诺数下持续存在。研究还发现，体雷诺数不能单独决定局域化起始，减阻率也不足以确定普适起始判据；基于空间湍动能方差的间歇性指标可将局域湍流与低壁面摩擦关联起来。受控湍流泡虽与典型湍流泡定性相似，但具有显著不同特性，如波前传播可快于体流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注减阻率本身，但对受流向行波方位角壁面速度控制的管道流中减阻与湍流状态（尤其局域化湍流和层流化）之间的关系缺乏系统理解。论文旨在填补这一空白，全面表征受控管道流中亚临界湍流状态及其湍流泡行为，为发展旨在实现流层流化的控制策略提供新视角。

Method: 采用直接数值模拟（DNS），设置体雷诺数Re_b=4900，对管道流施加流向行波形式的方位角壁面速度控制。通过系统改变控制参数，对比分析减阻表现、湍流脉动统计量，并引入基于横截面湍动能空间方差的间歇性指标来评估流场的局域化程度。

Result: ①管道流在适当控制下可完全层流化，而通道流不能，表明两者控制响应存在根本差异；②受控流可呈现空间局域化湍流（湍流泡），其可在未受控情况三倍以上的雷诺数下维持湍流；③体雷诺数不能单独决定局域化起始，减阻率也不足以作为普适起始判据；④间歇性指标可将局域湍流起始与低壁面摩擦相关联，但对应关系并非一一对应；⑤受控湍流泡具有独特性质，例如其波前传播速度可快于体流速度。

Conclusion: 本文提供了受控管道流中亚临界湍流状态及湍流泡的全面表征，证明了壁面控制不仅可显著减阻，还能通过调整壁面摩擦关系诱导层流化或形成增强的局域湍流态。研究为设计以层流化为目标的控制策略提供了新的理论基础和判别框架。

Abstract: Pipe flow controlled by streamwise-travelling waves of azimuthal wall velocity is studied using direct numerical simulations at a bulk Reynolds number $Re_b=4900$. A comprehensive analysis of drag reduction shows that the flow response differs fundamentally from that of channel flow. Under suitable forcing, pipe flow relaminarizes, whereas channel flow does not. Depending on the control parameters, the flow exhibits the spatially localized turbulent state characteristic of transitional pipe flow, with turbulent puffs persisting at bulk Reynolds numbers up to three times higher than in the uncontrolled case. The bulk Reynolds number alone does not determine the onset of localization. Moreover, drag reduction, which alters the natural relation between bulk and friction velocities, is insufficient to identify a universal onset criterion. An intermittency indicator based on the spatial variance of the cross-sectional turbulent kinetic energy relates the emergence of localized turbulence to the low wall friction produced by the control, although the correspondence is not one-to-one. Despite their qualitative resemblance to canonical turbulent puffs, the controlled puffs exhibit distinct properties; for example, their fronts may propagate faster than the bulk flow. Overall, this work provides a comprehensive characterization of the subcritical turbulent state and its turbulent puffs in controlled pipe flow, and offers a new perspective on control strategies that aim at flow relaminarization.

</details>


### [107] [Evolution of inertial particle clustering in decaying turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30545)
*Martín Obligado,Sofía Angriman*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过直接数值模拟研究自由衰减均匀各向同性湍流中惯性粒子的团簇行为，发现粒子优先集中不能仅用瞬时Stokes数描述，而是由瞬时流场状态和初始状态共同决定，聚类统计量在整个衰减过程中保留可测量的历史依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有湍流中惯性粒子动力学通常在统计稳态均匀各向同性湍流下用瞬时粒子Stokes数解释，但在自由衰减湍流中Kolmogorov时间尺度连续演化，有效Stokes数随时间变化。粒子优先集中是否仅由瞬时St唯一确定，还是依赖于流动先前的演化历史，这一问题尚缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 采用单向耦合极限下的重质点粒子直接数值模拟，研究各向同性湍流衰减过程中的惯性粒子团簇行为。通过三维Voronoi镶嵌量化团簇程度，考察两种粒子群：从统计稳态团簇状态演化而来的粒子群和初始随机分布的粒子群。同时对比常规强迫均匀各向同性湍流和基于物理信息神经网络重建的速度场。

Result: 粒子滑移速度遵循经典稳态下对瞬时St的依赖关系，但聚类统计量在衰减全程保留显著的历吏依赖性。现有稳态标度律仍适用于团簇尺寸演化，但需通过依赖历史的预因子修正。不同初始条件下演化的粒子分布在较大空间尺度上快速收敛到相似的组织结构，而更精细的聚类统计量则保留先前演化的印记。

Conclusion: 非平稳湍流中粒子优先集中由瞬时流场状态及其初始状态共同决定，仅凭瞬时Stokes数无法完整描述这一过程。载体流场决定优先集中的几何形态，而初始条件影响更精细的聚类统计量。

Abstract: Inertial particle dynamics in turbulence are commonly interpreted in terms of the instantaneous particle Stokes number, as supported by studies in statistically stationary homogeneous isotropic turbulence. But in freely decaying turbulence the Kolmogorov time scale evolves continuously, causing the effective Stokes number, St, to vary even though particle properties remain unchanged. Whether preferential concentration remains uniquely determined by this instantaneous St or depends on the flow's previous evolution has remained largely unexplored. We investigate inertial-particle clustering during the decay of homogeneous isotropic turbulence using direct numerical simulations of heavy point particles in the one-way coupling limit. Clustering is quantified through 3D Voronoï tessellations for populations evolving either from statistically stationary clustered states or initially random distributions. We consider both conventionally forced homogeneous isotropic turbulence and velocity fields reconstructed using physics-informed neural networks. We show that preferential concentration cannot be described solely by the instantaneous St. While particle slip velocity follows the classical steady-state dependence on the instantaneous St, clustering statistics retain a measurable history dependence throughout decay. Existing stationary scaling laws remain applicable to cluster-size evolution, but only through history-dependent prefactors. Particle distributions evolving from different initial conditions rapidly converge towards similar large-scale spatial organisation, indicating that the carrier flow determines the geometry of preferential concentration while finer clustering statistics preserve imprints of the previous evolution. These results demonstrate that preferential concentration in non-stationary turbulence is governed jointly by the instantaneous flow state and its initial state.

</details>


### [108] [Guided navigation in turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30675)
*Jingran Qiu,Kristian Gustavsson*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究均匀各向同性湍流中游泳者的垂直导航问题。相比单纯向上游动，利用局部流场信息的策略可将平均垂直速度提升约10%的均方根流速，而基于预计算最优轨迹的引导策略则可提升约40%。研究表明这一增益受限于游泳者拦截有利轨迹所需的时间，尤其是慢速游泳者。进一步发现预计算轨迹并非必需——游泳者可动态识别群体中表现优异的成员并将其作为导航目标，对足够快的游泳者而言，这种集体策略仅利用瞬时群体状态即可保留预计算引导的大部分收益。结果展示了分布式轨迹信息如何弥补局部感知在湍流导航中的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流中的导航极具挑战性，因为局部流动测量只能提供流相关尺度之外的有限有利路径信息，传统基于局部信息的导航策略性能受限。

Method: 在均匀各向同性湍流中系统比较三种垂直导航策略：(1) 朴素的向上游动；(2) 利用局部流场信息的策略；(3) 基于预计算高表现轨迹的引导策略。随后提出一种集体策略，让游泳者动态识别并跟踪群体中高表现成员。

Result: 局部策略比朴素上泳提升约10%均方根流速的平均垂直速度，预计算轨迹引导可达约40%增幅。此增益受限于拦截有利轨迹的时间，尤其对慢速游泳者。群体动态识别策略对足够快的游泳者可保留预计算引导的大部分收益。

Conclusion: 分布式轨迹信息（群体间分享）可以克服局部感知在湍流导航中的固有局限，为设计无需全局预计算的集体导航策略提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Navigation in turbulence is challenging because local flow measurements provide limited information about favorable paths beyond the flow correlation scales. We investigate vertical navigation by swimmers in homogeneous isotropic turbulence and compare strategies using local flow information with strategies guided by high-performing trajectories. Compared with naive upward swimming, local strategies enhance the mean vertical velocity by about 10% of the root-mean-square flow velocity, while guidance by precalculated trajectories reaches enhancements of about 40%. This gain is limited by the time required to intercept favorable trajectories, particularly for slow swimmers. We then show that precalculated trajectories are unnecessary: swimmers can dynamically identify high-performing members of a swarm and use them as targets. For sufficiently fast swimmers, this collective strategy keeps much of the benefit of precalculated guidance using only the instantaneous swarm state. Our results demonstrate how distributed trajectory information can be used to overcome limitations of local sensing for navigation in turbulence.

</details>


### [109] [Real Schur Flow Acoustic Directivity and Helicity Effects within](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30840)
*Pan Xu,Jian-Zhou Zhu,Min Zhu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过结合绝对统计平衡与声类比理论，研究了螺旋度对三类维度约化流（两种真实Schur流及其进一步约化的解放Schur流）远场声辐射方向性（指向性）的影响，发现螺旋度会放大维度约化诱导的各向异性指向性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性湍流的统计描述和声学诊断具有挑战性。真实Schur流作为分量式维度约化流的原型模型，为研究此类问题提供了有效平台。理解螺旋度对声辐射指向性的影响，有助于改进湍流的声学诊断手段。

Method: 将绝对统计平衡与声类比相结合，研究螺旋度对三类分量式维度约化流的远场声辐射指向性的影响。通过Wick定理，从绝对平衡高斯系综计算涡声源频谱，并据此评估远场指向性。

Result: 在所有分量式维度约化流中，螺旋度均放大了由维度约化引起的指向性函数的各向异性，且不同的流型呈现各异的指向性模式。

Conclusion: 螺旋度对维度约化流声辐射指向性具有系统性放大效应，所提出的统计平衡与声类比相结合的方法可有效描述各向异性湍流的声学特性。

Abstract: The statistical description and acoustic diagnostics of anisotropic turbulence remain challenging. Real Schur flows (RSFs), prototype models of component-wise dimensionally reduced flows (CWDRFs), provide effective platforms for studying such problems. We combine absolute statistical equilibrium with the acoustic analogy to investigate helicity effects on the far-field directivity of sound radiated from three types of CWDRFs: two different RSFs, and a further reduction of them (liberated Schur flow). We evaluate the directivity from the vortex-sound source spectrum which is calculated with the Wick theorem from the absolute equilibirum Gaussian ensemble. Helicity amplifies the dimensional reduction induced anisotropy of the directivity functions across all CWDRFs, with distinct patterns.

</details>


### [110] [The Structure of Merging Turbulent Jets Beneath a Small Quadrotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30900)
*Anoop Kiran,Nora Ayanian,Kenneth Breuer*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过粒子图像测速（PIV）对悬停Crazyflie 2.1四旋翼的下洗尾流进行了详细研究。四个转子射流在z/l≈5处合并为单柱，平均速度剖面在z/l≈13处收敛于典型圆形射流的自相似形式。中心线衰减和半宽增长遵循典型标度定律，有效源直径D_eff=2.29l，有效雷诺数Re=3×10^4，处于典型射流标度已确立范围的低端。然而，湍流正应力在整个测量域内保持由四转子源引起的双峰、切口依赖特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对四旋翼下洗尾流的研究主要表征平均流场，使用了单点风速测量、体积跟踪或部分转子系统的平面切片。合并尾流的高阶湍流统计量及其与典型射流标度的关系尚未解决，特别是对于处于低雷诺数范围末端的小型四旋翼。

Method: 对悬停的Crazyflie 2.1四旋翼（臂长l=46 mm）的下洗流进行PIV测量。沿两条采样路径：对角线切口（沿四旋翼对称轴穿过转子）和前转子切口（穿过相邻转子）。分析平均速度剖面、中心线衰减、半宽增长以及湍流正应力。

Result: 四个转子射流在z/l≈5处合并为单柱；平均速度剖面在z/l≈13处按局部中心线速度和半宽缩放后收敛至典型圆形射流的自相似形式。中心线衰减和半宽增长遵循典型标度律，D_eff=2.29l，Re=3×10^4，扩展和衰减常数在典型圆形射流范围内。湍流正应力在两个切口中均保持双峰特征，反映四转子源效应。

Conclusion: 尽管平均流在足够下游可收敛到典型圆形射流自相似形式，但湍流统计量在整个测量域内仍保留四转子源的双峰与切口依赖特征，表明合并尾流并未完全“忘记”其多转子来源，这对多旋翼编队的间距设计和飞行安全有重要影响。

Abstract: The downwash wake of a hovering quadrotor governs both the vehicle's own performance and the safe spacing of multi-rotor formations. Prior measurements have largely characterized the mean flow, using single-point anemometry, volumetric tracking, or planar cuts through part of the rotor system. Higher-order turbulent statistics of the merged wake, and how they relate to canonical jet scaling, have remained unresolved, particularly for small quadrotors at the low-Reynolds-number end of the size range. Here, we present a detailed particle image velocimetry (PIV) study of the downwash of a hovering Crazyflie 2.1 quadrotor (arm length, $l = 46$ mm), sampled along a diagonal cut, passing through rotors along the symmetry axis of the quadrotor, and a front-rotor cut, passing through adjacent rotors. The four rotor jets merge into a single column by $z/l \approx 5$, beyond which the mean velocity profiles progressively approach the canonical round-jet self-similar form, collapsing by $z/l \approx 13$ when scaled by the local centerline velocity and half-width. Centerline decay and half-width growth follow canonical scaling laws with an effective source diameter $D_\text{eff} = 2.29\,l$, effective Reynolds number $Re_{D_\text{eff}} = 3 \times 10^4$, at the low end of the range over which canonical jet scaling has been established, and spreading and decay constants nonetheless within the canonical round-jet range. Resolving both cuts shows that the turbulent normal stresses retain a bimodal, cut-dependent signature of the four-rotor source throughout the measurement domain.

</details>


### [111] [Learning dynamically consistent flow reconstructions from limited observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30909)
*Lu Zhu,Jacob Page*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出TraCTra框架，利用轨迹一致性原理，仅通过部分观测序列和可微前向模型实现无标签训练的重构网络，无需完整状态训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验科学中的核心逆问题是从稀疏或间接测量推断隐藏动力学状态，深度学习方法虽有潜力，但机器学习重构方法通常需要完整状态数据进行训练，这在许多实际场景中难以获得。

Method: TraCTra要求网络重构的状态与动力学演化相互一致：将网络预测的状态前向推进以匹配后续观测，并在全状态空间中与更晚时刻的独立重构结果保持一致。通过可微前向模型实现无标签训练。

Result: 在四个流体系统中展示了通用性：从粗粒化场重构三维湍流、从密度涨落观测重构速度、从二维投影阴影图序列重构三维密度和速度，以及从小空间窗口观测恢复全局涡度。该方法优于仅数据同化和物理信息神经网络方法，保持动力学重要的多尺度结构，在优化窗口之外仍保持准确，可迁移到未见的测试时刻并泛化到未见流场。

Conclusion: 轨迹一致性可作为一种通用的监督原理，用于在没有完整状态训练目标的情况下重构隐藏动力学状态。

Abstract: A core inverse problem in the experimental sciences is the inference of a hidden dynamical state from sparse or indirect measurements. There is a natural opportunity for deep learning methods here, but machine-learnt reconstruction methods typically require full state data for training. We present Trajectory-Consistent Network Training (TraCTra), a label-free framework that trains reconstruction networks using only partial observation sequences and a differentiable forward model. TraCTra requires the network reconstruction and dynamical evolution to be mutually consistent: network-predicted states are marched forward in time to match subsequent observations and to agree in the full state space with independent reconstructions at later times. Across four fluid systems, the same objective reconstructs three-dimensional turbulence from coarse-grained fields, velocity from observations of density fluctuations, and three-dimensional density and velocity from sequences of projected two-dimensional shadowgraphs, while also recovering global vorticity from observations confined to a small spatial window. TraCTra outperforms assimilation-only and physics-informed neural approaches, preserves dynamically important multiscale structure, and remains accurate beyond the optimisation window. It transfers to held-out times in the three-dimensional shadowgraph problem and, when trained across trajectories, generalises to unseen flows in the two-dimensional problem. The results establish trajectory consistency as a general supervision principle for reconstructing hidden dynamical states without full state training targets.

</details>


### [112] [A neural network architecture and training algorithm to predict viscoelastic stresses from vortical data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30926)
*Lu Zhu,Jacob Page*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于卷积神经网络与同化训练算法相结合的方法，通过涡度测量时间序列预测聚合物构象场，适用于弹性湍流中的平行剪切流，无需离线参考构象场库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性湍流中聚合物应力薄片形成的"箭头状"结构（精确相干态）尚未在实验中直接观测到，因为同时测量速度和聚合物构象极具挑战性。需要一种能从可测量的涡度数据中重构聚合物构象场的方法。

Method: 采用两组件方法：其一为卷积神经网络架构，输入涡度场并输出正定构象张量；其二是改编自Zhu & Page (2026)的基于同化的训练算法，无需预生成的离线参考构象场库，仅利用涡度测量进行训练。训练过程中要求时间推进网络预测上的测量与保存的时间序列匹配，同时迟后时刻求解器与网络预测需自洽。

Result: 在二维Kolmogorov流的多个工况（从行波到完全混沌状态）中，该方法均能稳健预测聚合物拉伸；标准的未正则化变分同化方法失效。在混沌情形下，网络无需进一步优化即可泛化到远大于训练所用"最小单元"的域。

Conclusion: 所提出的同化式训练CNN方法能够利用纯涡度数据在无需已知准确模型和参数的情况下进行聚合物构象场重构，且具备跨域泛化能力，为实验中观测弹性湍流的相干结构提供了可行的计算工具。

Abstract: Numerical simulations of elastic turbulence in parallel shear flows of polymer solutions indicate that the phenomena is associated with the formation and instability of exact coherent states dominated by thin sheets of polymer stress. However, these ``arrowhead'' structures are yet to be seen directly in experiments, where simultaneous velocity and polymer conformation measurements are challenging to obtain. Motivated by these challenges, we introduce a method for the prediction of the polymer conformation field given a time series of vorticity measurements. Our approach consists of two components: the first is a convolutional neural network architecture which takes vorticity fields and outputs a positive definite conformation tensor. The second is the adaptation of an assimilation-based training algorithm (Zhu \& Page, 2026) which does not require a pre-generated `offline' library of reference conformation fields, but is trained only using the vorticity measurements. This is particularly important in viscoelastic problems, where the appropriate model and parameters to compare to the experiments may need to be determined as part of the solution. In training, measurements made on a time-marched network prediction are required to match the saved time series, while the output of the solver and network predictions at later times are required to be self-consistent. We apply these ideas to two-dimensional Kolmogorov flow in a range of regimes, from simple traveling waves to a fully chaotic state. In all cases, our method produces robust predictions of the polymer stretch, while standard, unregularised variational assimilation is ineffective. In the chaotic case we show that our networks generalise to much larger domains -- without further optimisation -- than the `minimal' units in which they were trained.

</details>


### [113] [Growth of helicity in salt-finger convection in the two-dimensional three-component limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31010)
*Smiron Varghese,Benjamin Miquel,Wouter Bos*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过解析方法研究了二维三组分框架下盐指不稳定性相关的全局螺旋度预算，发现当瑞利比超过临界阈值时会发生螺旋度放大，为近期数值模拟中观测到的螺旋度自发出现提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期数值模拟观察到盐指不稳定系统中存在螺旋度的自发涌现，但缺乏相应的理论解释。作者希望通过解析方法来阐明螺旋度产生（放大）的物理机制与条件。

Method: 采用解析分析方法，在二维三组分框架下对盐指不稳定性引发的全局螺旋度预算进行理论推导，并通过瑞利比这一参数来表征盐度与温度梯度的相对强度，系统考察螺旋度演化的解析行为。

Result: 在对应于盐指现象的参量空间中，发现当瑞利比超过某个关键阈值时会出现螺旋度放大现象。

Conclusion: 该发现为数值模拟中观察到的螺旋度自发涌现提供了一种可能的理论解释。

Abstract: We present an analytical investigation of the global helicity budget associated with the salt-fingering instability within the two-dimensional, three-component framework. Our analysis shows that in the region of parameter space corresponding to salt-fingering, helicity amplification occurs when the Rayleigh ratio--quantifying the relative strength of salinity to temperature gradients--exceeds a critical threshold. This finding provides a possible theoretical explanation for the spontaneous emergence of helicity, observed in recent numerical simulations.

</details>


### [114] [A multiscale theory of convective momentum transport in the tropical atmosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31055)
*Edward Goldsmith,Levi Dittman,Joseph Biello*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文采用多重尺度渐近框架分析小尺度对流环流与大尺度热带环流之间的相互作用，推导出控制大尺度流场的封闭方程。未解析对流的影响通过一个非局域动量扩散算符进入，该算符从第一性原理表征对流动量输运，并自然地耦合大尺度环流的垂直正压模态。经典Matsuno-Gill模型可作为极限近似被恢复，从而将经典理论置于系统性的第一性原理基础之上。文章进一步验证了该广义理论的两个应用：局域赤道对称加热驱动的稳态环流中，对流引起的垂直模态耦合会激发额外正压模态，显著改变Walker环流的垂直结构；以及对赤道波谱的分析表明，特征模是经典赤道波族的混合体，导致色散关系失去分支结构并出现系统性频移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大尺度热带环流理论中对流的影响通常以经验性的瑞利阻尼来处理（如Gill模型中的现象学阻尼），缺乏从第一性原理出发的严格推导。作者旨在建立一个系统性的封闭框架，从数学上刻画小尺度对流对大尺度环流的动量和能量输运作用，从而将经典现象学模型置于严格的渐近理论基础之上。

Method: 采用多重尺度渐近（multiple-scales asymptotic）框架，将流场分解为小尺度对流与大尺度流场，通过渐近分析推导出控制大尺度流动的封闭方程。核心贡献是得到一个非局域动量扩散算符，该算符自然耦合各阶垂直正压模态；在极限情形下可还原为Gill引入的现象学阻尼项。通过两个具体应用（局域加热驱动的稳态Walker环流和赤道波谱分析）验证理论的预测能力。

Result: (1) 推导出包含非局域动量扩散项的封闭大尺度流场方程组，该扩散算符表征对流动量输运并耦合垂直模态；(2) 经典Matsuno-Gill模型被证明是该理论在特定极限下的近似，从而获得第一性原理基础；(3) 局域赤道对称加热下，对流诱导的垂直模态耦合会激发额外正压模态，显著改变Walker环流的垂直结构；(4) 赤道波谱分析显示新特征模态为经典赤道波族的混合态，色散关系失去独立分支结构并表现出系统性频移。

Conclusion: 对流诱导的动量输运会系统性地改变稳态热带环流的垂直结构和赤道波动的色散特征。该研究从第一性原理出发为对流-大尺度流动相互作用提供了严格的数学封闭框架，同时将经典Matsuno-Gill理论纳入统一的渐近理论体系中，为热带动力学的理论建模提供了更完备的基础。

Abstract: A multiple-scales asymptotic framework is employed to analyze the interaction between a field of small-scale convective circulations and the large-scale tropical circulation. Closed equations governing the large-scale flow are derived, providing a systematic closure for the influence of unresolved convection. The influence of unresolved convection enters through a non-local momentum diffusion operator that provides a first-principles representation of convective momentum transport and naturally couples the vertical baroclinic modes of the large-scale circulation. The classical Matsuno--Gill model is recovered as a limiting approximation in which the derived momentum transport operator reduces to the phenomenological damping introduced by Gill, thereby placing the classical theory on a systematic first-principles foundation. Two applications of the generalized theory are considered. First, steady-state circulations driven by localized equatorially symmetric heating are examined. Convectively induced vertical-mode coupling is shown to excite additional baroclinic modes beyond those directly forced, substantially modifying the vertical structure of the Walker circulation. Second, the equatorial wave spectrum is analyzed. The resulting eigenmodes comprise mixtures of the classical equatorial wave families, causing the dispersion relations to lose their distinct branch structure and exhibit systematic frequency shifts. These results demonstrate how convectively induced momentum transport systematically modifies both steady tropical circulations and equatorial wave dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Neural Multichannel Distant Speaker Diarization and Source Separation with Beta Speaker Activity Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28661)
*Sicheng Mao,Mathieu Fontaine,Anthony Larcher,Roland Badeau*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文针对远场说话人日志化（diarization）任务，提出了一种基于贝叶斯框架的模型驱动方法，对神经FCASA模型进行增强，通过引入Beta先验于说话人活动并构造变分下界目标函数，替代原有交叉熵损失进行训练。在AMI数据集上，Diarization Error Rate相对降低16%（绝对降低3%以上），Jaccard Error Rate相对降低20%（绝对降低4%以上）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远场说话人日志化因不利声学条件、说话人数量变化和重叠语音而极具挑战性。虽然数据驱动方法表现强劲，但模型驱动方法通过利用多通道录音的空间信息提供了有吸引力的替代方案。本文致力于为模型驱动方法neural FCASA提出贝叶斯日志化模型以增强其鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一个贝叶斯日志化模型：在说话人活动上引入Beta先验，推导出变分下界目标函数，该目标可视为正则化的连续说话人活动分数（regularized continuous speaker activity score），用以替代原始交叉熵损失来训练日志化模型。

Result: 在AMI数据集上的实验表明，与基线相比，Diarization Error Rate至少绝对下降3%（相对下降16%），Jaccard Error Rate至少绝对下降4%（相对下降20%）。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Beta先验和变分推断的正则化训练目标能显著提升neural FCASA模型在远场说话人日志化任务上的性能，验证了贝叶斯模型驱动方法在该任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Distant speaker diarization remains challenging due to adverse acoustic conditions, varying numbers of speakers and overlapping speech. While data-driven approaches have shown strong performance, model-driven methods offer a compelling alternative by leveraging spatial information from multichannel recordings. This paper is motivated to propose a Bayesian diarization model for a model-driven method called neural FCASA to enhance its robustness. Specifically, we propose a beta prior over speaker activity and hence a variational lower bound objective that can be seen as a regularized continuous speaker activity score in place of the original cross-entropy loss to train the diarization model. Our experiments show significant improvements in terms of Diarization Error Rate by at least 3% (16% relatively) and Jaccard Error Rate by at least 4% (20% relatively) on the AMI dataset compared to the baseline.

</details>


### [116] [Evoking Harmony via Convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28851)
*Michael Gogins*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出通过将源声音与一个特殊构造的脉冲响应（每个音高类在各八度音域内对应一个窗化正弦波颗粒）进行卷积，从而从任意源声音中唤起和弦的音高类内容，同时最小化伪影。作者用Csound用户定义操作码chord_convolver实现，混合干式Dirac分量并应用分段卷积，并与线性梳状滤波器和恒定Q谐振器组对比，在拉加德城堡废墟旁黄昏野外录音上演示了音乐应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从任意声源（非调性/非谐波）中唤起特定和弦音高内容，且希望尽量减少处理伪影，传统的梳状滤波器和谐振器组各有局限，需要一种更直接有效的卷积方法。

Method: 将源声音与一个脉冲响应做卷积，该脉冲响应的粒子（grains）为——在人类可听的每个八度上、对每个音高类放置一个窗化的正弦波，从而提取相应音高类；同时将干式Dirac分量混入脉冲响应以减少伪影，并采用分区卷积（partitioned convolution）在Csound的chord_convolver自定义用户操作码中实现。论文与线性频域梳状滤波器及恒定Q谐振器组进行了效应对比。

Result: chord_convolver效应可以与线性梳状滤波器及恒定Q谐振器组区分开；该方法在实际音乐场景（拉加德城堡废墟的黄昏野外录音）中显示出可用的音乐效果。

Conclusion: 基于窗化多八度正弦波颗粒构造的脉冲响应卷积能够有效地从任意源声音中唤起和弦音高内容，且配合干信号混合与分区卷积能减少伪影，是一种可用于创意音乐处理的可行技术。

Abstract: I show how to evoke the pitch-class content of a chord from an arbitrary source sound by convolving the source with an impulse response whose grains are one windowed sinusoid per pitch-class, across each octave of hearing range; while, at the same time, minimizing artifacts. A Csound user-defined opcode, chord_convolver, mixes a dry Dirac component into that response, and applies partitioned convolution once. I contrast the effect with a linear-frequency comb filter and with a generic constant-Q resonator bank, and I demonstrate musical use on a twilight field recording alongside the ruins of Chateau de Lagarde.

</details>


### [117] [Diagnose, Then Refine: A Closed-Loop TTS System with AudioLLM-Guided Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28970)
*Zeyang Song,Tianchi Liu,Tianrui Wang,Chenglin Xu,Steven Y. Guo,Haizhou Li*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: LoopTTS 是一个由 AudioLLM 法官驱动的闭环语音合成（TTS）系统，采用滤波器-法官-精炼器框架。当基础 TTS 模型的初始输出存在韵律缺陷时，AudioLLM 法官先识别问题并生成结构化改进指令，再由受指令控制的精炼器（Refiner）基于初始音频、目标文本和指令进行表达性重合成。为训练精炼器，作者构建了含 42K 条音频 LLM 注释数据的 Refiner-DB 数据集。人工评估表明，LoopTTS 在修复低质量语音方面优于原始音频和开环重生成基线，且精炼器在音调和停顿控制的指令跟随能力上表现更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TTS 系统采用开环、单遍生成模式，会产生局部韵律缺陷（如重音错置、不自然停顿、语调扁平化），而这些缺陷往往无法被发话语级别的评估指标所捕捉。作者旨在解决开环生成无法针对局部韵律错误进行修正的问题。

Method: 提出 LoopTTS 闭环框架，包含三个组件：(1) AudioLLM Judge：利用听觉语言模型识别显著韵律问题并输出结构化精炼指令；(2) Refiner：一个细粒度指令跟随的 TTS 模型，以初始话语、目标文本和指令为条件执行表达性重合成；(3) Refiner-DB：包含 42K 条由 AudioLLM 注释的数据集，提供词级韵律弱监督用于训练 Refiner。

Result: 人工评估显示，LoopTTS 能够检测感知上显著的韵律错误并用 Refiner 加以纠正，在恢复质量上优于原始生成音频和实际开环重生成基线。精炼器在针对性的韵律修改（如重音与停顿控制）上展现出更强的指令跟随能力。

Conclusion: 闭环的“法官-精炼”机制能够有效定位并修复 TTS 输出中那些指标难以捕获的局部韵律缺陷，为提升语音合成质量提供了一种可持续迭代的恢复方案。

Abstract: Current TTS systems typically rely on open-loop, single-pass generation and can produce sporadic local prosodic defects, such as misplaced stress, unnatural pauses, or flattened intonation, that utterance-level metrics often fail to expose. We present LoopTTS, a judge-guided Filter-Judge-Refiner framework for recovering low-quality TTS outputs diagnosed by an AudioLLM. Given an initial utterance from a base TTS model, an AudioLLM Judge identifies salient prosodic issues and generates structured refine instructions; a Refiner, our fine-grained instruction-following TTS model, then performs guided expressive re-synthesis conditioned on the initial utterance, target text, and instruction. To train the Refiner, we construct Refiner-DB, a 42K-example AudioLLM-annotated dataset with word-level prosodic weak supervision. Human evaluation on diagnosed low-quality utterances shows that LoopTTS can detect perceptually salient errors and correct them with the Refiner, outperforming raw generated audio and practical open-loop re-generation baselines in recovery quality. The Refiner also demonstrates stronger instruction-following ability for stress and pause control in targeted prosody modification.

</details>


### [118] [What Are You Listening to? Temporal Music Grounding for Audio-to-Text Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29480)
*Kun Fang,Ziyu Wang,Ichiro Fujinaga*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出时间音乐定位（temporal music grounding）任务，要求音频-语言模型返回与查询音符、事件或模式对应的一个或多个时间跨度。作者构建了MusicGroundingBench基准，通过将算法生成的钢琴MIDI渲染为音频获得精确的音符-音频对齐，包含MGBench-3N和MGBench-2B两个子集。实验表明当前音频-语言模型在该任务上仍面临挑战，而任务特定的训练能带来显著提升，并初步探索了定位监督与音乐理解之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频-语言模型虽然能产生流畅且音乐上合理的回应，但现有系统难以判断其回答是否真正基于音频输入中的证据。这暴露了模型可能产生"幻觉式"或未锚定在输入音频上的回应这一问题。作者希望通过引入时间音乐定位任务和相应基准，来评估模型是否能够将回应扎根于时间上局部化的音乐证据中。

Method: 作者定义了一个新的时间音乐定位任务，要求模型返回与查询对应的一个或多个时间跨度。为了受控地评估该能力，他们通过渲染算法生成的钢琴MIDI来创建基准套件MusicGroundingBench，从而获得精确的符号到音频对齐。基准包含两个子集：MGBench-3N评估最多三个音符片段中的音符级定位，MGBench-2B评估两小节摘录中的结构化定位和短形式音乐理解。

Result: 实验发现，时间音乐定位对当前的音频-语言模型而言仍然具有挑战性，而采用任务特定训练能带来显著的性能提升。此外，作者还报告了关于定位监督与音乐理解之间关系的探索性证据。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了MusicGroundingBench作为一个受控测试平台，用于评估音频-语言模型是否能够将其回应扎根于时间上局部化的音乐证据中，为后续在时间定位与音乐理解交叉方向的研究提供了基础性资源。

Abstract: Large audio-language models can produce fluent and musically plausible responses, yet it often remains unclear whether those responses are grounded in the audio input. We introduce temporal music grounding, a task in which a model returns one or more time spans corresponding to a queried musical note, event, or pattern. To evaluate this capability, we present MusicGroundingBench, a controlled benchmark suite built by rendering algorithmically generated piano MIDI to audio, yielding exact symbolic-to-audio alignment. The suite comprises two subsets: MGBench-3N, which evaluates note-level grounding in clips containing up to three notes, and MGBench-2B, which evaluates structured grounding and short-form music understanding in two-bar excerpts. Experiments show that temporal music grounding remains challenging for current audio-language models, whereas task-specific training yields substantial gains. We further report exploratory evidence on the relationship between grounding supervision and music understanding. These results establish MusicGroundingBench as a controlled testbed for assessing whether audio-language models ground their responses in temporally localized musical evidence.

</details>


### [119] [How Well Do Generative Music Models Follow Emotion Conditioning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29987)
*Morteza Heydari*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一评估流程，系统检验生成音乐对情感提示的遵循程度。通过在GTZAN全量1000首曲目上，利用DashengLM提取语义描述、Music2Emotion估计源曲愉悦度与唤醒度，生成情感感知提示词，并用三个系统（Stable Audio Open、MusicGen、InspireMusic）进行文本与音频条件生成，通过绝对值误差和欧氏距离度量生成音频与源曲在愉悦度-唤醒度空间中的差异。结果表明文本条件生成优于音频条件，MusicGen（文本）和InspireMusic（文本）表现最佳；愉悦度比唤醒度更易保持，且情感遵循度在不同音乐类型间差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有生成音乐模型通过文本和音频条件提供了日益精细的控制能力，但其对预期情感线索的遵循程度仍是未解问题。现有评估多依赖整体质量或提示相关性等通用指标，缺乏直接针对情感可控性的统一评测方法。

Method: 构建统一的情感遵循评估管道：(1) 使用GTZAN全部1000首曲目作为源；(2) 用DashengLM音频描述模型提取语义描述；(3) 用Music2Emotion估计源曲的愉悦度（valence）和唤醒度（arousal）；(4) 将语义描述与排名靠前的情感标签结合构建情感感知文本提示词；(5) 用Stable Audio Open、MusicGen和InspireMusic三个系统生成30秒音频，分别评估文本条件和音频条件两种生成模式；(6) 计算生成音频的愉悦度和唤醒度，与源曲比较，使用绝对值误差和愉悦度-唤醒度空间中的欧氏距离作为评估指标。

Result: 文本条件生成在情感遵循方面一致优于音频条件生成；MusicGen（文本）和InspireMusic（文本）取得最佳效果；音频条件生成的变体稳定性较差。此外，愉悦度比唤醒度被保持得更可靠，且情感遵循能力在不同音乐类型间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究强调应直接评估生成模型的感可控性（affective controllability），而非仅依赖整体质量或提示相关性等通用指标，为生成音乐模型的情感建模评估提供了系统化方案与基准。

Abstract: Recent generative music models offer increasingly fine-grained control through text and audio conditioning, yet how faithfully they follow intended emotional cues remains an open question. We address this gap with a unified evaluation pipeline for emotion-following in generated music. Using all 1000 tracks in GTZAN, we extract semantic audio descriptions with DashengLM, an audio captioning model, and estimate source-track valence and arousal with Music2Emotion, a music emotion recognition model. We construct affect-aware text prompts by combining descriptions with top-ranked emotion tags and generate 30-second outputs with three systems, Stable Audio Open, MusicGen, and InspireMusic, evaluating both text- and audio-conditioned generation. To measure emotion-following, we compute valence and arousal on generated audio and compare them with the source tracks using absolute error and Euclidean distance in valence-arousal space. Text-conditioned generation consistently outperforms audio conditioning, with MusicGen (text) and InspireMusic (text) achieving the best performance, while audio-conditioned variants prove less stable. We further find that valence is preserved more reliably than arousal and that emotion-following varies substantially across genres. These findings underscore the importance of evaluating affective controllability directly rather than relying solely on general quality or prompt-relevance metrics.

</details>


### [120] [PhysWave: Physics-Guided Latent Diffusion Models for Controllable Spatial Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29549)
*Lingfeng Yao,Chenpei Huang,Xingke Yang,Ziye Geng,Changqing Luo,Hao Wang,Jiang Liu,Miao Pan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysWave，一种物理引导的潜在扩散模型，用于可控的文生FOA（一阶环境声）空间音频生成。通过统一的路径-字幕表示整合自然语言与轨迹控制，并引入球谐方向一致性和平方反比距离一致性两个可微声学先验增强扩散训练。作者还构建了包含30万片段的FOA数据集，实验证明物理先验能提升空间一致性且保持音频质量，并可在推理时用作无需训练的空间细化引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生FOA方法高度依赖数据驱动，可能生成违背声源方向与距离之间声学关系的音频；同时将描述性控制与参数化控制分离，迫使使用者在易用性与精确性之间权衡取舍。

Method: PhysWave采用物理引导的潜在扩散模型，其创新包括：(1) 通过共享的航点-字幕（waypoint-caption）表示统一自然语言与轨迹控制；(2) 在扩散训练中引入两个可微声学先验——球谐方向一致性先验和平方反比距离一致性先验；(3) 构建30万片段的FOA数据集，覆盖多样声音类别与声源轨迹，支持动态空间生成。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提出的物理先验帮助PhysWave生成空间一致性强且音频质量具有竞争力的FOA音频。进一步分析显示，这些物理先验在训练阶段提升空间一致性，同时也可作为推理期引导用于免训练的精细空间优化。

Conclusion: 物理引导的扩散建模是提升文生空间音频物理一致性的有效途径，其统一控制范式与可迁移的推理期物理先验为空间音频生成提供了可靠方向。

Abstract: Text-to-spatial audio generation, such as text-to-First-Order Ambisonics (FOA), provides a convenient way to create spatial audio for billion-dollar gaming and film industries. However, existing text-to-FOA methods are largely data-driven and may produce audio that violates acoustic relations between source direction and distance. They also separate descriptive and parametric control, forcing users to trade usability for precision. In this paper, we present PhysWave, a physics-guided latent diffusion model for controllable text-to-FOA generation. PhysWave unifies natural-language and trajectory control through a shared waypoint-caption representation, and augments diffusion training with two differentiable acoustic priors: spherical-harmonic direction consistency and inverse-square distance consistency. To support dynamic spatial generation, we further construct a 300K-clip FOA dataset with diverse sound categories and source trajectories. Extensive results show that the proposed priors help PhysWave generate spatially consistent FOA audio while maintaining competitive audio quality. Further analyses show that these physics priors improve spatial consistency during training and can also be used as inference-time guidance for training-free spatial refinement.

</details>


### [121] [Perceptually Better, Semantically Worse: Measuring Speech Enhancement Impact on LLM-Based Voice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30348)
*Randy Frans Fela,Pejman Mowlaee*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出了输出发散率（ODR）指标，用于衡量语音增强（SE）如何改变LLM的意图分类结果。通过在2,974条SLURP语音片段上测试五种条件，发现所有条件下的ODR均显著高于零（p<0.001），其中MetricGAN+虽提升了PESQ但ODR翻倍（0.318 vs 0.135），未消除的回声通过说话人替换达到0.836的ODR——这是WER无法捕捉的失败模式。音频质量指标与ODR仅呈零到中等相关性，说明标准音频质量指标不足以评估LLM流水线的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音增强常被用作口语AI流水线的预处理步骤，其基本假设是更好的音频质量能提升下游任务性能。然而，语音增强引入的失真是否会传播并影响下游LLM任务表现仍然是一个未解的问题。现有研究缺乏系统性地量化语音增强如何影响LLM意图分类的方法。

Method: 引入Output Divergence Rate（ODR）指标，衡量语音增强改变LLM意图分类结果的频率（相对于干净语音）。在2,974条SLURP录音上，使用Whisper large-v3和wav2vec2-large级联架构，测试五种条件：未增强的带噪语音、MetricGAN+增强、回声条件等，并通过二项检验评估显著性。

Result: 所有条件下ODR均显著高于零（p<0.001）。MetricGAN+将ODR从0.135提高到0.318（翻倍以上），尽管PESQ得到改善；未消除的回声达到0.836的ODR，源于说话人替换（speaker substitution）——这是WER无法捕捉的失败模式。音频质量指标与ODR的相关性从近零到中等（SQUIM-MOS ρ=-0.068，PESQ ρ=-0.467）。MetricGAN+和回声的结果在两种ASR架构中均得到复现。

Conclusion: 标准音频质量指标（如PESQ、SQUIM-MOS）不足以评估LLM流水线的质量。语音增强虽可能改善听觉/传统音频质量指标，却可能损害下游LLM任务性能。需要专门为LLM流水线设计质量评估方法与指标，不能再默认"更好音频质量"等于"更好下游表现"。

Abstract: Speech enhancement (SE) is commonly applied as a preprocessing step in spoken AI pipelines under the assumption that better audio quality improves downstream task performance. Whether SE-induced distortions propagate to downstream LLM task performance remains an open question. We introduce Output Divergence Rate (ODR), which measures how often SE changes an LLM's intent classification relative to clean speech, and benchmark five conditions on 2,974 SLURP clips using Whisper large-v3 and wav2vec2-large cascades. Every condition produces ODR significantly above zero ($p < 0.001$, binomial test). MetricGAN{+} more than doubles ODR versus unenhanced noisy speech (0.318 vs. 0.135) despite improving PESQ, and unmitigated echo reaches an ODR of 0.836 through speaker substitution, a failure WER cannot capture. Audio quality metrics range from near-zero to moderate correlation with ODR (SQUIM-MOS $ρ=-0.068$, PESQ $ρ=-0.467$). The MetricGAN{+} and echo results replicate across ASR architectures, indicating that standard audio quality metrics are insufficient for LLM pipeline quality.

</details>


### [122] [Textual Acoustic Grounding for Generalizable LLM-Based Deepfake Voice Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30622)
*Yassine El Kheir,Xin Wang,Wanqing Ge,Tim Polzehl,Sebastian Moeller,Junichi Yamagishi*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于音频大语言模型（ALLM）的深度伪造语音检测问题。作者对多种音频编码器与Qwen LLM（0.5B至7B）的组合进行了系统评测，发现仅微调LLM会导致域外过拟合，而冻结LLM是更强的资源高效基线。为解决音频嵌入与LLM语义空间之间的模态差距，作者提出了跨模态提示策略，将openSMILE提取的基于语言知识驱动的声学特征以结构化文本token注入，显式的文本接地增强了冻结基线并提升了微调效果。该方法在域外ITW和MLAAD基准上实现了SOTA鲁棒性，较现有ALLM基线在Macro-F1上获得超过16.2%的绝对提升，同时保持有竞争力的域内性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造语音检测在跨域场景下泛化能力差。尽管音频大语言模型有潜力，但连续音频嵌入（捕捉细微声学细节）与LLM语义空间之间的模态差距是关键的、尚未充分探索的瓶颈。作者旨在解决这一模态差距问题，以提升深度伪造语音检测的域外泛化能力。

Method: 1）系统性地对多种音频编码器与Qwen LLM（参数规模从0.5B到7B）进行组合评测。2）对比LLM微调与冻结LLM两种策略，揭示微调的域外过拟合风险。3）提出跨模态提示策略（Cross-Modal Prompting），通过openSMILE提取语言知识驱动的声学特征，将其作为结构化文本token注入LLM，弥合模态差距。

Result: 在域外ITW和MLAAD基准上实现了SOTA鲁棒性，相比现有ALLM基线在Macro-F1上取得超过16.2%的绝对提升，同时保持了有竞争力的域内性能。所有模型已公开发布。

Conclusion: 冻结LLM配合跨模态文本特征注入是一种比直接微调更有效且资源高效的深度伪造语音检测方案。显式的文本接地策略能有效弥合音频特征与LLM语义空间的模态差距，显著提升域外泛化能力。

Abstract: Deepfake voice detection suffers from poor generalization across unseen domains. While Audio Large Language Models (ALLMs) show promise, the modality gap between continuous audio embeddings which capture the subtle acoustic details necessary for deepfake detection and the semantic space of LLMs remains a critical, underexplored bottleneck. We address this by benchmarking diverse audio encoders integrated with Qwen LLMs (0.5B to 7B parameters). First, we demonstrate that fine-tuning the LLM alone risks out-of-domain overfitting, making a frozen LLM a stronger, resource-efficient baseline. Second, to explicitly bridge the modality gap, we introduce a cross-modal prompting strategy that injects linguistic-knowledge-driven acoustic features (via openSMILE) as structured text tokens. This explicit textual grounding not only enhances the frozen baseline but also makes LLM fine-tuning more effective. Ultimately, our approach demonstrates state-of-the-art resilience on the out-of-domain ITW and MLAAD benchmarks, yielding over \textbf{16.2\%} absolute improvement in Macro-F1 over existing ALLM baselines while maintaining competitive in-domain performance. All models reported in this work are \href{https://huggingface.co/01Yassine/AudioLLM-Deepfake-Detection}{publicly available}.

</details>


### [123] [TEMPO: Temporally-grounded Multi-task Post-training for Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29999)
*Apoorva Kulkarni,Kaousheik Jayakumar,Sreyan Ghosh,Utathya Aich,Ramani Duraiswami,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出TEMPO，这是首个统一处理音频、语音和音乐时间戳任务的时序感知多任务后训练模型。核心贡献包括三种创新（原子时间戳令牌、时间感知投影器、距离感知高斯损失）、合成到真实的课程训练、以及首次将GRPO强化学习用于统一音频时间戳任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型音频语言模型（LALMs）只能在剪辑级别描述音频，无法为识别出的事件、说话人或声音分配时间戳。时间戳功能对于语音识别和密集音频描述等下游任务至关重要，但却是大多数LALMs的关键限制。作者旨在解决这一模型能力缺口。

Method: TEMPO采用基于三种创新构建的监督微调（SFT）阶段：(1) 原子时间戳令牌；(2) 时间感知投影器，将正弦波壁钟编码注入音频帧嵌入；(3) 距离感知高斯损失。训练采用合成到真实的课程策略。此外，作者首次将GRPO强化学习（配以可验证的时间奖励）应用于统一音频时间戳，作为SFT之上的精调阶段。

Result: 在包含五个任务、10K样本的评估基准上，TEMPO在性能上优于Audio Flamingo Next和Qwen3-Omni这两个明确在带时间戳数据上训练的最先进模型。实验表明SFT贡献了大部分增益，GRPO提供了一致但适度的额外改进。训练数据集含119K样本。

Conclusion: TEMPO成功弥补了LALMs在时序接地能力上的空白，实现了音频、语音和音乐三类时间戳任务的统一处理。SFT是性能提升的主要来源，GRPO作为精化阶段提供适度但稳定的增益，验证了直接优化可验证时序目标的有效性。

Abstract: Large audio-language models (LALMs) describe audio at the clip level but cannot assign timestamps to the events, speakers, or sounds they identify. Despite being essential for downstream tasks like speech recognition and dense audio captioning, timestamping remains a key limitation of most LALMs. We present TEMPO (Temporally-grounded Multi-task Post-training), the first unified model to handle audio, speech, and music timestamping tasks. Our core contribution is a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) stage built on three innovations: atomic timestamp tokens, a time-aware projector that injects sinusoidal wall-clock encodings into audio frame embeddings, and a distance-aware Gaussian loss. Our training is based on a synthetic-to-real curriculum. We further introduce, to our knowledge, the first application of reinforcement learning to unified audio timestamping, using GRPO with verifiable temporal rewards that directly optimize the evaluation objectives. Rather than serving as the primary source of performance gains, GRPO acts as a refinement stage on top of the SFT checkpoint, providing modest additional improvements. To support this work, we build a training dataset containing 119K samples and an evaluation benchmark containing 10K samples, drawn from established corpora across five tasks. On this benchmark, TEMPO outperforms Audio Flamingo Next and Qwen3-Omni, two state-of-the-art LALMs explicitly trained on timestamped data. Experiments confirm that SFT delivers most of these gains, with GRPO providing consistent but moderate refinements.

</details>


### [124] [MusGU+: Toward a Musician-Centered Evaluation Framework and Discovery Tool for Generative Music AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30940)
*Laura Ibáñez-Martínez,Roser Batlle-Roca,Xavier Serra,Martín Rocamora*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出MusGU+，一个以音乐人为中心、围绕适应性（Adaptability）、可用性（Usability）和可控性（Controllability）三个维度构建的生成式音乐系统评估框架，并评估10个代表性系统，提供一个交互式发现工具帮助音乐人按标准筛选模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式音乐系统被宣称为能民主化音乐创作，但其对音乐人的实际适用性尚未被充分探索。现有工作如MusGO侧重开放性评估，以及定性研究音乐人体验，但这些方法不支持系统比较或在创作早期阶段的模型发现。

Method: 提出MusGU+框架，围绕三个维度展开：适应性（模型能否在个人数据上训练或微调）、可用性（能否集成到真实音乐工作流）、可控性（能否以有音乐意义的方式控制模型）。基于该框架评估10个代表性生成式音乐系统，并构建一个交互式模型发现工具。

Result: 对10个代表性生成式音乐系统进行了系统性评估，并开发了一个交互式探索工具，使音乐人能够根据适应性、可用性和可控性标准筛选和探索模型。

Conclusion: MusGO在促进负责任研究实践方面仍有价值，而MusGU+则支持音乐人对生成式系统进行知情选择与实际采用。

Abstract: Generative music systems are increasingly presented as tools that democratize music creation, yet their practical suitability for musicians remains underexplored. Prior work includes openness-focused evaluation frameworks, such as MusGO (Music-Generative Open AI), as well as qualitative studies of musicians' experiences with generative systems. However, these approaches do not support systematic comparison or early-stage discovery of models for creative use. Motivated by such limitations, we introduce MusGU+, a musician-centered evaluation framework organized around three dimensions: Adaptability, Usability, and Controllability. Together, these capture whether a model can be feasibly trained or fine-tuned on personal data, integrated into real-world music workflows, and controlled in musically meaningful ways. We evaluate 10 representative generative music systems and present an interactive discovery tool that enables musicians to explore and filter models according to these criteria. While MusGO remains valuable for promoting responsible research practices, MusGU+ supports informed selection and practical adoption of generative systems by musicians.

</details>


### [125] [VIBE: Video Instruction-aligned Background music gEneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30125)
*Aryan Vijay Bhosale,Vaibhavi Lokegaonkar,Vishnu Raj,Gouthaman KV,Sreyan Ghosh,Ramani Duraiswami,Lie Lu,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出VIBE，一个文本+视频到音乐的生成模型，通过Conditioning Connection（深度式跨层条件调制机制）和综合奖励建模分类法（覆盖硬性可验证约束与软性主观质量）来解决现有视频到音乐模型缺乏语义控制和指令惩罚的问题。实验表明VIBE在可控性和指令遵循方面表现出色，同时在生成保真度和多模态对齐上与大多数基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频到音乐（V2M）模型缺乏语义控制能力，且无法有效惩罚指令违规。根本原因在于：1）它们依赖重建目标函数；2）扩散自回归（DAR）架构中静态跨模态条件调制存在表征瓶颈。

Method: VIBE采用两大核心设计：（1）Conditioning Connection——一种深度式跨层条件调制机制，动态桥接规划头与扩散精炼头；（2）全面的奖励建模分类法，同时优化硬性可验证约束（如节奏、调性）和软性主观质量（如音乐性、多模态对齐），并配合结构化的5阶段训练课程。

Result: 通过音视频对齐、指令遵循、音频质量指标以及主观人类评估研究，VIBE在可控性和指令遵循方面表现增强，在生成保真度和多模态对齐上与大多数评价基线表现相当。

Conclusion: VIBE通过深度式跨层条件调制和全面的奖励建模训练框架，有效提升了文生视频到音乐生成的可控性与指令服从能力，同时保持了与基线相当的生成质量，验证了该方案在解决V2M模型语义控制缺失问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Current video-to-music (V2M) models lack semantic control and fail to penalize instruction violations, largely due to their reliance on reconstruction objectives and the representational bottleneck of static cross-modal conditioning in Diffusion Autoregressive (DAR) architectures. To resolve this, we introduce VIBE, a novel text-and-video-to-music (T+V2M) generation model that leverages: (1) Conditioning Connection, a depth-wise cross-layer conditioning mechanism that dynamically bridges the planning and diffusion refinement heads and (2) a comprehensive reward modeling taxonomy, optimizing for both hard, verifiable constraints (e.g., tempo, key) and soft, subjective qualities (e.g., musicality, multimodal alignment) with a structured 5-stage training curriculum. Upon evaluation using audio-visual alignment, instruction following, and audio quality metrics, along with a subjective human evaluation study, we observe that VIBE demonstrates enhanced controllability and instruction adherence while performing comparably to most evaluated baselines on generation fidelity and multimodal alignment.

</details>


### [126] [CoJEPA: Combining Contrastive Learning and JEPA for Global-Local Music Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30974)
*Gabriel Meseguer-Brocal,Yuexuan Kong,Romain Hennequin*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: CoJEPA将JEPA与对比学习的目标结合到单一共享骨干中，在无需EMA教师的情况下同时获得稳定训练与丰富的局部表征，在全局和局部MIR任务上均优于或匹敌单独方法。"


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA虽能通过潜在空间自监督预测学习丰富表征，但依赖EMA教师-学生架构且易产生无信息表征；对比学习训练稳定并能产生强大的全局表征，但因目标全局性限制其在局部任务上的表现。作者希望结合两者优势，取长补短。

Method: CoJEPA使用单一共享骨干网络，同时以两种目标联合训练：对掩码序列token施加JEPA目标，对分类token施加对比学习目标。对比学习梯度提供训练稳定性，从而完全移除EMA教师需求；JEPA则通过局部预测丰富序列token的表征。整个方法不引入任何额外参数，仅通过训练信号设计引导模型学习更丰富的表征。

Result: CoJEPA在全局和局部MIR（音乐信息检索）任务上均优于或匹配两种单独方法，特别在调性与和声理解等任务上表现出显著优势，且无需任何任务特定的架构改动。

Conclusion: CoJEPA证明具有互补归纳偏置的训练目标组合可以替代模型规模的增长，鼓励未来研究投入于更智能的训练目标设计，而非一味扩大模型规模。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) has shown strong performance in learning rich representations through self-supervised prediction in latent space. However, it typically relies on teacher--student architecture with an EMA to stabilise training, and can tend to yield uninformative representations. Contrastive learning is stable to train and produces strong global representations, but remains limited on local tasks by the global nature of its objective. In this work, we combine both into CoJEPA: a single shared backbone jointly trained with a JEPA objective on masked sequence tokens and a contrastive objective on the class token. The contrastive gradient provides stability, removing the need for an EMA teacher entirely, while JEPA enriches the sequence tokens via local predictions that contrastive learning alone cannot provide. Crucially, no extra parameters are added to the backbone: the same model is guided towards richer representations purely through the design of its training signal. CoJEPA takes the best of both worlds, outperforming or matching both individual methods across global and local MIR tasks, with a particularly strong advantage on tonal and harmonic understanding, and without any task-specific architectural changes. CoJEPA shows that combining objectives with complementary inductive biases can substitute for scale, encouraging future work to invest in smarter training objectives over ever-larger models.

</details>


### [127] [Parallel Time-Band Mixing with Learned Observation-Adding for Robust ASR Front-Ends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30326)
*Xingyu Shen,Runze Wang,Wei-Ping Zhu,Benoit Champagne*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Parallel Time-Band Mixer (PTBM)模块的序列并行带分裂语音增强前端，消除了块内递归展开带来的时序依赖，在DNS Challenge和CHiME-4上配合冻结Whisper后端持续降低词错误率，前端仅需0.96M参数和0.58 GMAC/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音增强常作为鲁棒ASR的前端，但现有的递归时序模块和跨带模块引入顺序依赖，限制并行效率。为了在保持掩码加残差重建接口的同时，提升时序和跨带建模的并行性。

Method: 提出PTBM模块，在一个统一的并行架构中整合带内时序混合和逐帧跨带注意力，实现跨时间和频率维度的高效上下文建模。系统保留掩码加残差重建接口，并引入学习型Observation-Adding (LOA)机制以抑制ASR敏感伪影，无需development-set调参。

Result: 在DNS Challenge和CHiME-4数据集上使用冻结Whisper后端的实验中，提出的前端相比递归带分裂基线持续降低词错误率，前端网络仅需0.96M参数和0.58 GMAC/s计算量。

Conclusion: PTBM通过并行架构消除了递归依赖，兼顾了增强效果与计算效率，为语音增强前端提供了更高效的序列并行设计方案。

Abstract: Speech enhancement is often used as a front-end for robust ASR, yet recurrent temporal and cross-band modules introduce sequential dependencies that reduce parallel efficiency. In this paper, we present a sequence-parallel band-split enhancement front-end built on a Parallel Time-Band Mixer (PTBM) block that eliminates within-block recurrent unrolling. PTBM integrates intra-band temporal mixing and per-frame cross-band attention within a unified parallel architecture, enabling efficient contextual modeling across both time and frequency dimensions. The system retains the mask-plus-residual reconstruction interface and introduces learned Observation-Adding (LOA) to suppress ASR-sensitive artifacts without development-set tuning. Experiments on DNS Challenge and CHiME-4 with frozen Whisper back-ends show that the proposed front-end consistently reduces word error rate relative to recurrent band-split baselines while requiring only 0.96 M parameters and 0.58 GMAC/s for the front-end network.

</details>


### [128] [Ouroboros: Self-Referential Backdoor Attacks on Speech Enhancement via Clean Audio Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30329)
*Yunjie Zhou,Yuheng Huang,Diqun Yan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出Ouroboros，一种针对语音增强模型的后门攻击框架，利用模型自身的理想干净输出作为天然触发器，无需外部触发器注入即可在推理阶段激活后门，实现近乎完美的攻击成功率且性能退化最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音增强模型被广泛用作实时语音服务的前端模块，但其对后门攻击的脆弱性尚未被探索。现有后门方法局限于分类任务且依赖主动触发器注入，这与语音增强模型被动处理的特性不兼容，因此需要一种新的不需要外部触发器注入的后门攻击框架。

Method: 提出Ouroboros框架，利用语音增强模型的理想干净输出作为自然触发器，使得后门可以在推理时被激活而无需外部触发器的主动注入，从而规避了传统主动触发方式与语音增强被动处理特性之间的不兼容问题。

Result: 在多种模型和数据集上的广泛评估表明Ouroboros实现了近乎完美的攻击成功率且性能退化最小；物理世界验证确认自然录制、未修改的干净音频可可靠激活后门；该框架还能推广到定向内容篡改攻击，并对常见的滤波和微调防御保持有效性。

Conclusion: Ouroboros首次揭示了语音增强模型面临的新型后门攻击风险，证明了在无外部触发器注入的被动处理场景下，利用自然干净输出即可可靠触发后门，这对语音增强模型的安全部署提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Speech enhancement models are widely deployed as frontend modules in real-time speech services, yet their vulnerability to backdoor attacks remains unexplored. Existing backdoor methods are confined to classification tasks and rely on active trigger injection, an assumption incompatible with the passive processing nature of speech enhancement models. In this paper, we propose Ouroboros, a novel backdoor attack framework that leverages the ideal clean outputs of speech enhancement models as natural triggers, enabling inference-time activation without any external trigger injection. Extensive evaluations show Ouroboros achieves near-perfect attack success rates with minimal performance degradation on diverse models and datasets. Physical-world validations confirm that naturally recorded, unaltered clean audio can reliably activate the backdoor. Moreover, Ouroboros generalizes to targeted content-tampering attacks and remains effective against common filtering and finetuning defenses.

</details>


### [129] [SPHERE: Automatic Music Upmixing via Audio Language Model Post-Training with Spatial Heuristic Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30559)
*Zixun Guo,Calvin Murdock,Sanjeel Parekh,W Owen Brimijoin,Simon Dixon,Joshua Reiss,Ishwarya Ananthabhotla*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文研究自动音乐混音（upmixing）任务，提出了一种基于音频语言模型（ALM）后训练的方法。通过拒绝采样SFT配合基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR via GRPO），并设计名为Sphere的确定性奖励套件（包含6个感知驱动的子奖励），引导输出混音达到居中、平衡、宽广的效果，无需特定任务的编码器或架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动音乐混音方法依赖特定任务的音乐编码器，缺乏对音乐语义与混音知识的深层利用。作者希望借助已有音频语言模型中丰富的表征（同时编码音乐语义与混音知识），并通过后训练方式将专家领域知识注入模型，从而避免任务特定架构的设计。

Method: 提出Sphere后训练方案：1）采用两阶段流程——首先进行拒绝采样SFT（Rejection Sampling SFT），随后使用GRPO算法进行基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）；2）设计Sphere (Spatial Heuristic Rewards)确定性奖励函数套件，包含6个感知动机的子奖励，受音乐混音惯例启发，用于约束输出混音的居中性、平衡性与空间感。

Result: 该方法在自动音乐混音任务上取得了优于依赖任务特定音乐编码器的现有方法的表现；同时更广泛地证明了专家领域知识可以被编码为可验证奖励并蒸馏进语言模型，而无需任务特定的架构。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于：1）提出了首个利用ALM后训练进行音乐混音的通用框架；2）设计了Sphere可验证奖励套件以编码混音专家知识；3）验证了将领域知识编码为可验证奖励并通过RLVR注入语言模型的通用范式，为后续多模态/跨任务的语言模型后训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the task of automatic music upmixing, wherein a system predicts spatial mixing parameters from a multi-stem recording. Different from existing methods that rely on task-specific music encoders, we approach this task via audio language model (ALM) post-training, leveraging rich representations from existing ALMs, which encode both music semantics and mixing knowledge. Specifically, we propose a post-training recipe that first employs rejection sampling SFT, followed by reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards (RLVR) via GRPO. We propose Sphere (Spatial Heuristic Rewards), a deterministic reward suite inspired by music mixing conventions, to guide our post-training. It consists of 6 perceptually-motivated sub-rewards and encourages the output mix to be centered, balanced and spacious. More broadly, our results suggest that expert domain knowledge can be encoded as verifiable rewards and distilled into language models, without task-specific architectures.

</details>


### [130] [Closing the Verification Loop: Self-Check Captioning for Long-Paragraph Detailed Audio Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30713)
*Fengji Ma,Yan Rong,Xu Li,Chen Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Check Captioning (SCC)框架，解决长段落详细音频字幕生成中视听多模态语言模型面临的"数据匮乏"和"生成模式失败"两大结构性问题。SCC包含三部分：LACap-50k数据集（50,222条剪辑、平均491.5词字幕、带ASR审计）、Layer-Curvature Supervised Fine-Tuning (LC-SFT)微调方法（首次基于中间层证据加权token，针对Late-Layer Semantic-Entropy Collapse问题）以及SCC-Verifier推理时验证器（通过音频接地自问答仲裁字幕候选）。系统在多个基准上达到开源字幕器最先进水平，并与专有基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前音频-视觉多模态语言模型无法解决长段落详细音频字幕生成任务，该任务需要密集且与转录文本忠实匹配的细粒度音频内容描述。作者将其归因于两个结构性问题：一是数据匮乏——现有公开语料无法同时提供长片段、段落级字幕和逐词转录保真度；二是生成模式失败——右音频和打乱音频的多选题准确率之间存在44.8至46.4个百分点的巨大差距，说明模型并未真正"听到"音频而是依赖表面模式。

Method: SCC统一框架将音频接地问答作为验证原语贯穿整个生命周期。具体包含三部分：(1) LACap-50k：50,222条剪辑的音频-视觉语料库，含平均491.5词的字幕及事后自动语音识别(ASR)审计确保转录保真；(2) LC-SFT：首个按策略(on-policy)监督微调方法，受Late-Layer Semantic-Entropy Collapse (SEC)现象启发，依据中间层证据对token进行加权；(3) SCC-Verifier：推理时通过音频接地自问答对多个字幕候选进行仲裁，选出最忠实候选。

Result: 在多个基准上达到开源开源字幕模型中最先进水平，并与专有基线相比具有竞争力。同时发布了LACap-50k数据集以填补长段落详细音频字幕研究的数据集资源缺口。

Conclusion: 通过将音频接地验证内嵌于数据构建、训练微调和推理验证三个环节，SCC有效解决了长段落详细音频字幕中的两大结构性问题，并开源了关键数据集以推动该领域的后续研究。识别出的Late-Layer Semantic-Entropy Collapse现象与提出的LC-SFT方案也为音频-视觉模型训练提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Long-paragraph detailed audio captioning, which requires dense and transcript-faithful descriptions of fine-grained audio content, remains unsolved for current audio-visual multimodal language models. We attribute this failure to two structural problems. The first is data poverty, as no public corpus jointly provides long clips, paragraph captions, and verbatim-transcript fidelity. The second is generation-mode failure, evidenced by a 44.8 to 46.4 percentage-point gap between right-audio and shuffled-audio multiple-choice question (MCQ) accuracy. We address both within Self-Check Captioning (SCC), a unified framework that instantiates audio-grounded question answering as the verification primitive at every lifecycle stage. SCC yields three artifacts. Long-paragraph Audio Caption 50k (LACap-50k) is a 50,222-clip audio-visual corpus with 491.5-word captions and a post-hoc automatic speech recognition (ASR) audit. Layer-Curvature Supervised Fine-Tuning (LC-SFT) is the first on-policy supervised fine-tuning method to weight tokens by intermediate-layer evidence, motivated by our identification of Late-Layer Semantic-Entropy Collapse (SEC). SCC-Verifier arbitrates among caption rollouts via audio-grounded self-answering at inference. Across multiple benchmarks, our system attains state-of-the-art among open-source captioners and is competitive with proprietary baselines. We release LACap-50k to fill the resource gap for long-paragraph detailed audio captioning research.

</details>


### [131] [Vocal Music under Phoneme-Conditional Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30823)
*Hayoon Kim,Kyogu Lee*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出"音素条件分析"方法，通过在同一首歌内比较标记音节与匹配的非标记对照，隔离具有类型学特征的音素在演唱中的声学效应。在9种语言、数千首歌曲中，基于这些效应构建的歌曲级特征可实现85.5%的平衡准确率进行无伴奏人声的语言识别（按歌手分组交叉验证）。研究揭示音系结构会系统性地在人声演唱中留下可测量的痕迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每种语言的声乐即使没有乐器伴奏也携带着独特的声音身份，但这一差异是否可量化、能否追溯到特定音素尚不清楚。作者试图证明这种语言特定的声音差异是可测量的，并能定位到具体的音素层面。

Method: 引入音素条件分析（phoneme-conditional analysis），通过在同一首歌内将典型性标记音节的声学特征与匹配的对照非标记音节进行比较，从而控制歌手、旋律和流派等混杂因素。研究覆盖9种类型学上多样化的语言与数千首歌曲，在5个声学维度上测量音素效应，并基于这些效应构建歌曲级特征轮廓用于语言分类。

Result: 在按艺术家分组折叠交叉验证的九分类任务中，基于歌曲级轮廓特征可实现85.5%的平衡准确率来识别无伴奏人声的语言。是否可分离性由音素局部效应本身累积而成仍是一个开放问题。

Conclusion: 音系结构在每种语言的演唱方式中留下了系统性的、可测量的痕迹，说明语言本身的音素特征会影响声乐的声音身份，而不只是依赖旋律、歌手或流派等因素。

Abstract: The vocal music of each language carries a distinctive sonic identity, even without instrumental accompaniment. We ask whether these differences are measurable and traceable to specific phonemes. To tackle this question, we introduce phoneme-conditional analysis, which isolates the acoustic effect of typologically distinctive phonemes by comparing marker syllables against matched non-marker controls within the same song, holding singer, melody, and genre constant. Across nine typologically diverse languages and thousands of songs, we measure effects along five acoustic dimensions. Song-level profiles built from these effects identify the language of an unaccompanied vocal at 85.5% balanced accuracy in a nine-way classification with folds grouped by artist; whether the separability arises by accumulation of the phoneme-local effects themselves is left open. Our findings suggest that phonological structure leaves systematic and measurable traces in how each language is sung.

</details>


### [132] [Playability-Aware Audio-to-Tablature Guitar Transcription via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30854)
*Riccardo Simionato,Louis Bigo*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出Noise2Fret，一种基于扩散模型的音频到吉他指法谱转录系统，通过连续潜在表示生成离散的品位和琴弦目标，并引入五种辅助损失（音高类别距离、位置距离、五度圈距离、琴弦相似性、手跨度可行性）来融合物理可演奏性与音高准确性约束。在GuitarSet和GOAT数据集上超越基线且计算更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吉他指板谱转录不仅要精确检测音高，还需将每个音符映射到具体的弦-品位位置，因为同一音高可在多个品位位置演奏。现有方法将其视为标准分类问题，忽略了控制可演奏指法序列的音乐与物理约束，导致输出缺乏实际可演奏性。

Method: 提出基于扩散模型（Noise2Fret）的生成式框架：通过连续潜在表示对离散的品位和琴弦目标进行建模，并以频谱和音频特征为条件。同时设计五种辅助损失函数——音高类别距离、位置距离、五度圈距离、琴弦相似性和手跨度可行性——直接嵌入训练目标中，弥合音高精度与物理可演奏性之间的差距。

Result: 在GuitarSet和GOAT数据集上的实验表明，该模型在性能上优于基线方法，同时计算效率更高；此外，辅助损失相对标准训练目标带来了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将音乐和物理约束以辅助损失形式引入扩散模型训练，Noise2Fret有效提升了吉他指法谱转录的准确性与可演奏性，同时保持计算效率优势。

Abstract: Guitar tablature transcription requires not only accurate pitch detection but also assigning each note to a specific string-fret position, as the same pitch can be played at multiple fretboard positions. Existing approaches treat this as a standard classification problem, ignoring the musical and physical constraints that govern playable fingering sequences. We propose Noise2Fret, a diffusion model for audio-to-tablature transcription that generates tablature through a continuous latent representation of discrete fret and string targets, conditioned on spectral and audio features. To bridge the gap between pitch accuracy and physical playability, we introduce five auxiliary losses encoding Pitch-Class Distance, Positional Distance, Circle-of-Fifths Distance, String Similarity, and Hand-Span Feasibility directly into the training objective. Experiments on GuitarSet and GOAT datasets demonstrate that the model outperforms baselines while remaining computationally more efficient, and that the auxiliary losses yield consistent gains over the standard training objective.

</details>


### [133] [Decoupled Latent Flow Matching for Few-Step Joint Vocal-Accompaniment Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30913)
*Lishi Zuo,Youzhi Tu,Lu Yi,Zezhong Jin,Chongxin Gan,Man-Wai Mak,KongAik Lee*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜空间流匹配的联合人声-伴奏分离框架，利用预训练VAE将混合信号和源信号映射到紧凑潜空间，通过分离编码器和速度解码器解耦语义分离与声学速度预测，并结合Flow2GAN启发的对抗后训练实现少步生成，在降低采样成本的同时提升感知与分离指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成式建模虽能灵活处理混合条件源分布，但迭代式扩散模型和流匹配模型对长音乐信号的计算成本过高，难以高效完成人声与伴奏分离任务。

Method: 1) 使用预训练VAE将混合信号与源信号映射到紧凑潜空间；2) 提出分离编码器（Separation Encoder）与速度解码器（Velocity Decoder）分别负责语义分离与声学速度预测，实现功能解耦；3) 在潜空间中进行联合流匹配建模，同时生成人声与伴奏潜表示；4) 借鉴Flow2GAN引入潜空间对抗后训练，以支持少步采样生成，降低采样成本。

Result: 实验表明，潜空间对抗细化能够在减少采样预算的条件下，同时改善感知质量指标和分离评估指标。

Conclusion: 潜空间流匹配结合对抗细化是一种高效的音乐源分离方案，在保持生成质量的同时大幅降低推理采样开销，为长音乐信号的联合源分离提供了可行的实用路径。

Abstract: Generative modeling provides a flexible way to model mixture-conditioned source distributions, but iterative diffusion and flow matching models are costly for long music signals. This paper studies joint vocal-accompaniment separation through latent flow matching, where a pretrained variational autoencoder (VAE) maps mixtures and sources into a compact latent space and a flow matching model generates vocal and accompaniment latents jointly. The proposed framework decouples semantic separation from acoustic velocity prediction through a Separation Encoder and a Velocity Decoder. To reduce sampling cost, we further apply latent adversarial post-training inspired by Flow2GAN for few-step generation. Experiments show that latent adversarial refinement can improve perceptual and separation metrics under a reduced sampling budget.

</details>


### [134] [Stride-k Subsampling: Train-Free Audio Token Reduction for Whisper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30927)
*Chanhee Cho,Junhyuk Choi,Bugeun Kim*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出stride-k子采样方法，通过确定性索引操作保留卷积主干或编码器Transformer后的每个第k个token，利用Whisper语音编码的冗余性将音频token减少75%，总GFLOPs降低52-58%，同时应用于多个Whisper规模及SpeechLM上仅带来轻微的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper采用固定的1500-token编码器接口，已成为ASR解码器和基于Whisper的语音语言模型（SpeechLM）的默认表示形式，但其冗余性此前未被系统研究。作者旨在探索该音频token接口是否承载了超出下游任务实际需求的容量。

Method: 提出stride-k子采样操作：一种确定性索引方法，在卷积主干或编码器Transformer输出后仅保留每第k个token。在五种规模的Whisper模型中，于两处位置均采用k=2配置，并利用CKA分析验证该操作的稳定性源于卷积主干的声学重叠以及编码器输出的注意力诱导重分布。

Result: k=2时在两个位置均保持基线WER；同时施加stride-2可将音频token削减75%，总GFLOPs降低52-58%。多数ASR基准上WER代价较小，较难基准上代价较大；在三种Whisper-based SpeechLM上，强基线仅有轻微精度下降，弱基线下降较大，端到端延迟降低19.6-27.4%。

Conclusion: stride-k子采样无需训练或辅助计算，利用了Whisper预处理阶段的冗余性，表明Whisper的音频token接口携带的容量超下游任务所需，可在保持可用性能的同时显著降低计算和延迟开销。

Abstract: Whisper exposes speech through a fixed 1500-token encoder interface, now a default representation for ASR decoders and Whisper-based speech language models (SpeechLMs), yet its redundancy remains largely unexamined. We propose stride-k subsampling, a deterministic indexing operation that retains every k-th token after the convolutional stem or encoder transformer. Across five Whisper scales, k=2 preserves baseline WER at both positions, with CKA attributing this stability to acoustic overlap at the stem and attention-induced redistribution at the encoder output. Applying stride-2 at both positions cuts audio tokens by 75% and total GFLOPs by 52-58%, with small WER costs on most ASR benchmarks and larger costs on harder ones. The same configuration extends to three Whisper-based SpeechLMs, yielding modest accuracy drops on stronger baselines and larger drops on weaker ones, while reducing end-to-end latency by 19.6-27.4%. Requiring no training or auxiliary computation, stride-k subsampling exploits Whisper's preprocessing redundancy, indicating that its audio-token interface carries more capacity than downstream tasks require.

</details>


<div id='eess.AS'></div>

# eess.AS [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Accurate Plate Reverb Parameter Estimation Using Two-Stage Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28818)
*Byunghoo Park,Jayeon Yi,Takyoung Kim,Minje Kim*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对1st DAFx参数估计挑战赛任务A的黑盒优化方法，用于从单一脉冲响应中恢复模拟金属板混响器的六个物理参数。方法分两阶段：第一阶段使用CMA-ES进化优化器在幅度归一化损失下恢复五个参数，第二阶段通过三元搜索在非归一化损失下单独估计第六个参数（表面密度）。作者还在50个验证集IR上测试了方法，讨论了病态失效模式，并通过消融实验验证了两阶段设计的合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从测量到的脉冲响应中逆向恢复物理混响器的参数（如尺寸和材料属性）具有实际意义，但这是一个高维、非线性的反问题。作者希望将参数估计视为黑盒优化问题，利用模拟器作为评估函数，通过优化方法高效地搜索参数空间，同时解决不同参数耦合及量纲差异带来的挑战。

Method: 方法包含两阶段流水线：第一阶段使用CMA-ES（协方差矩阵自适应进化策略）优化器，在幅度归一化的损失函数下同时恢复六个参数中的五个；第二阶段使用三元搜索在非归一化损失下单独估计表面密度参数，因为幅度归一化会消除该参数对响应的振幅线索。此外，作者在搜索前选择损失函数，并分析了多尺度频谱损失中压缩操作为何会降低恢复精度。

Result: 方法在50个IR组成的验证集上进行了测试，展示了有效的参数恢复性能。作者识别并讨论了一种病态失效模式，并通过消融实验表明两阶段设计优于统一的CMA-ES搜索方案，证明了分阶段策略的必要性和有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将参数估计分解为两阶段优化问题——先用幅度归一化损失处理对振幅不敏感的参数，再用非归一化损失单独恢复振幅关联参数，可以有效提升金属板混响器参数反演的精度和鲁棒性。损失函数的选择和分阶段策略对搜索性能有显著影响。

Abstract: We describe our submission to Task A of the 1st DAFx parameter estimation challenge. The task is to recover the six physical parameters of a simulated metal-plate reverberator -- its dimensions and material properties -- from a single impulse response (IR). We treat this as a black-box optimization: candidate parameter sets are fed to the simulator and scored by a loss against the target IR. The method has two stages. The first uses CMA-ES, an evolutionary optimizer, to recover five of the six parameters, comparing IRs under an amplitude-normalized loss. Amplitude normalization makes the search robust but discards the cue to the sixth parameter, the plate's surface density; a second stage therefore estimates it alone, with a ternary search on the un-normalized loss. As the choice of loss strongly affects the search, we select it beforehand, and analyze why compression in the common multi-scale spectral loss degrades recovery. Finally, we test our method on a validation set of 50 IRs, discuss a pathological failure mode, and ablate to justify having two different stages instead of a unified CMA-ES search.

</details>


### [136] [When Vocal Tone and Literal Meaning Diverge: An Acoustic-Semantic Incongruity Study for Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28966)
*Yu-Wen Chen,William Ho,Maxim Topaz,Zoran Kostic,Julia Hirschberg*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文介绍CREMA-ASIS数据集，专门用于研究声学情绪与语义情感线索不一致（如讽刺）的多模态情感识别问题。研究发现大型音频语言模型（LALMs）在处理语义-声学不一致案例时表现困难，且主要受语义信息主导；通过监督微调可显著提升LALMs在不一致案例上的表现，同时保持转录准确性和联合情绪识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感线索可能不一致（如讽刺、讥讽性赞美），仅依赖单一模态会导致误解。尽管LALMs已被应用于多模态情感识别，但它们解耦声学与语义线索的能力，尤其是在不一致情况下，尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建CREMA-ASIS数据集，将声学情绪标签与语义情感极性配对以研究跨模态不一致性。在多任务框架中评估LALM偏见，并进行逐层分析以识别各层级的模态主导性。进一步通过监督微调评估性能改进。

Result: LALMs在处理语义-声学不一致案例时表现困难，很少预测出不一致性，且模型主要受语义信息影响。监督微调显著提升了模型在CREMA-ASIS测试集上的性能，同时保持了转录准确性和联合情绪识别能力，并展现了在域外数据上增强声学和语义理解的潜力。

Conclusion: 研究表明LALMs目前存在语义模态偏见，难以处理声学-语义不一致场景；但通过针对性数据集和监督微调，可以有效提升LALMs在多模态情感识别中同时理解声学和语义线索的能力，为域外数据上的推广提供了可能。

Abstract: Affective cues across modalities may be incongruous (e.g., sarcasm or mocking praise), potentially leading to misinterpretation when relying on a single modality. Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) have recently gained popularity and been applied to multimodal emotion recognition, but their ability to disentangle acoustic and semantic cues, especially in incongruent cases, remains underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce CREMA-ASIS, a dataset specifically created to investigate incongruence between acoustic emotion and semantic sentiment cues. It pairs acoustic emotion labels with semantic sentiment polarities. Using this dataset, we evaluate LALM biases within a multitask framework and conduct a layer-wise analysis to identify modality dominance across layers. Our findings reveal that LALMs struggle with semantic-acoustic incongruent cases, rarely predicting incongruity, and that LALMs are predominantly influenced by semantic information. However, supervised fine-tuning significantly improves LALM performance on our CREMA-ASIS test set while preserving transcription accuracy and joint emotion recognition. Results demonstrate potential for enhancing both acoustic and semantic understanding on out-of-domain data.

</details>


### [137] [Beyond Speech: Dual-Domain SSL Fusion for Unified All-Type Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29021)
*Cunhang Fan,Junqin Cao,Tian Gao,Zhipeng Xie,Jun Xue,Zhao Lv,Xin Fang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向全类型音频深度伪造检测的双域SSL融合方法，将语音、环境音、歌声和音乐等多种异构音频统一映射到二分类真实性空间。该方法结合EAT-large和wav2vec 2.0 XLS-R-300M两种互补的自监督学习特征，通过层级加权融合和token级融合整合多级伪造痕迹，并采用保守语音精炼策略，最终在AT-ADD Track 2评测集上取得95.58%的Macro-F1，位居挑战赛第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频深度伪造检测方案多为语音中心或类型依赖型，无法适用于涵盖语音、环境声、歌声和音乐的全类型音频检测场景。由于测试时音频类型未知而输出仍须为单一的二元决策，需要一种能够将异构音频统一映射到共享真伪空间的方法来解决该问题。

Method: 提出双域SSL融合框架：(1) 采用EAT-large和wav2vec 2.0 XLS-R-300M作为互补的SSL特征源，分别提供广泛的声学和事件级表征以及波形级、人声和语音敏感表征；(2) 层级加权融合用于整合来自不同Transformer深度的多级伪造伪影；(3) token级融合构建统一特征池，无需在两个SSL流之间强制帧级对齐；(4) 使用多头注意力统计池化对融合token进行汇总，并通过二元MLP分类头进行分类；(5) 在该统一核心检测器之上应用保守语音精炼策略。

Result: 所提交系统在AT-ADD Track 2挑战赛评测集上达到95.58%的Macro-F1，排名第二。

Conclusion: 双域SSL融合方法能够有效解决全类型音频深度伪造检测中音频类型未知的难题，通过互补特征融合在不依赖类型先验的情况下实现统一的二元真实/伪造判定。

Abstract: Unified all-type audio deepfake detection aims to determine whether an input clip is real or fake when its audio type may be speech, environmental sound, singing voice, or music. Existing speech-centric or type-dependent solutions are insufficient for this setting because the test-time audio type is unknown, while the required output is still a single binary decision. To address these issues, this paper proposes a dual-domain SSL fusion method that maps heterogeneous audio into a shared binary authenticity space. EAT-large and wav2vec 2.0 XLS-R-300M are used as complementary SSL feature sources, providing broad acoustic and event-level representations as well as waveform-level, vocal, and speech-sensitive representations. Layer-wise weighted fusion integrates multi-level artifacts from different transformer depths, while token-level fusion forms a unified feature pool without enforcing frame-level alignment between the two SSL streams. The fused tokens are summarized by multi-head attentive statistics pooling and classified with a binary MLP head. With conservative speech refinement applied on top of this unified core detector, the submitted system achieves 95.58% Macro-F1 on the AT-ADD Track 2 evaluation set and ranks second in the challenge.

</details>


### [138] [Weakly Supervised Tabla Stroke Transcription via an Adaptive Dynamic Rhythm Language Model (ADRM)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30314)
*Rahul Bapusaheb Kodag,Vipul Arora*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱监督下的塔布拉鼓击打（Tabla Stroke）转写框架，仅使用符号化击打序列（无需时间对齐），将基于CTC的声学模型与自适应动态节奏语言模型（ADRM）结合进行解码网格重打分，并发布了新的真实演奏与合成数据集，实验证明该方法显著降低了击打错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tabla鼓击打转写对印度斯坦音乐节奏结构分析至关重要，但由于节奏组织复杂动态且强标注数据稀缺而充满挑战。现有方法依赖完整监督的onset级标注，成本高昂且难以规模化。

Method: 提出一个弱监督框架：1）基于CTC的声学模型产生解码格（lattice）；2）提出自适应动态节奏语言模型（ADRM），结合tāla条件化的符号节奏规律与局部击打动力学，对解码格进行重打分精化，思路类比自动语音识别中的语言模型重打分。同时发布了Tabla Improvisation Dataset（真实演奏）及配套合成数据集。

Result: 实验表明，相较于仅使用声学解码，结合ADRM重打分在击打错误率上取得一致且显著的降低，验证了在网格重打分中融入符号节奏规律对准确转写的有效性。

Conclusion: 将符号层面的节奏规律性（tāla条件化）引入序列级弱监督转写的解码后重打分，能有效提升TST的准确度，为缺乏强标注的大规模场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tabla Stroke Transcription (TST) is central to the analysis of rhythmic structure in Hindustani music, yet it remains challenging due to complex and dynamic rhythmic organization and the scarcity of strongly annotated data. Existing approaches largely rely on fully supervised learning with onset-level annotations, which are costly and impractical at scale. This work addresses TST in a weakly supervised setting, using only symbolic stroke sequences without temporal alignment of onsets. We propose a framework that combines a Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC)-based acoustic model with a sequence-level rhythmic language model for rescoring, similar to that used in automatic speech recognition. The acoustic model produces a decoding lattice, which is refined using an Adaptive Dynamic Rhythm Language Model (ADRM) that combines $t\bar{a}la$-conditioned symbolic rhythmic regularities with local stroke dynamics.
