Moreover, we release a new performance-recorded tabla dataset, named \emph{Tabla Improvisation Dataset}, along with a complementary synthetic dataset for sequence-level weakly supervised TST. Experiments demonstrate consistent and substantial reductions in stroke error rates with ADRM compared to those with acoustic-only decoding, confirming the benefit of incorporating symbolic rhythmic regularities during lattice rescoring for accurate transcription.

</details>


### [139] [An Optical Pathway to Movable Rydberg Atomic Quantum Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30718)
*Qihao Peng,Qu Luo,Lixia Xiao,De Mi,Neng Ye,Cunhua Pan,Pei Xiao,Cheng-Xiang Wang,Jiangzhou Wang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种光学可移动的里德伯原子量子接收机（RAQR），通过在蒸汽池内动态调控探测光和耦合光束的指向，无需机械驱动即可重构射频（RF）感知位置。作者推导了封闭形式的等效基带模型，将原子换能系数、光学转向相位和单元中心阵列响应分离为独立因子，并验证了模型与Lindblad主方程数值解的准确性。基于该模型，揭示了两种互补的信道整形机制：RF-光换能带来的固有波束整形和光学位移实现的单元级相位控制。进一步构建了关于光学位置与本地振荡器设计的非凸和速率最大化问题，并通过带解析梯度的交替优化框架求解。仿真验证了模型并展示了光学可移动性带来的显著性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射频接收架构在动态重构频率感知位置方面受限于机械驱动，缺乏灵活性与可编程性。作者旨在利用里德伯原子量子接收器的光学可移动特性，实现无需机械作动的动态信道重构，以支持未来无线网络中对可编程接收架构的需求。

Method: 首先，在每个蒸汽池内对探测光和耦合光束进行光学转向，实现射频感知位置的重构。推导封闭形式的等效基带模型，通过分离原子换能系数、光学转向相位和单元中心阵列响应三个因子建模，并用Lindblad主方程数值解验证。基于该模型揭示两种信道整形机制，随后构造非凸的和速率最大化（SRM）问题，运用带解析梯度的交替优化框架求解光学位置与本地振荡器设计。

Result: 仿真结果表明：所提出的等效基带模型与Lindblad主方程数值解高度吻合；所提交替优化算法有效收敛；相比无光学可移动性的基准方案，RAQR在系统中获得了显著的和速率性能增益，验证了光学可移动性的实用价值。

Conclusion: 光学可移动的里德伯原子量子接收机为实现程序化的未来无线接收架构提供了一种新途径。其波束整形与相位控制的双重能力以及所提出的优化框架，为设计与部署高性能量子接收系统奠定了理论与算法基础。

Abstract: This paper develops an optically movable Rydberg atomic quantum receiver (RAQR), in which the probe and coupling beams are steered within each vapor cell to dynamically reconfigure the effective radio-frequency (RF) sensing position without mechanical actuation. A closed-form equivalent baseband model is derived by separating the atomic transduction coefficient, optical steering phase, and cell-center array response into distinct factors and the accuracy of the resulting model is validated against numerical solutions of the Lindblad master equation. Based on the derived model, we reveal two complementary channel-shaping mechanisms, including intrinsic beam-pattern shaping through RF-to-optical transduction and per-cell phase control enabled by optical displacement. To further exploit these capabilities, a non-convex sum-rate maximization problem is formulated over the optical positions and local oscillator design and solved via an alternating optimization framework with analytical gradients. Simulation results validate the derived model and demonstrate substantial performance gains enabled by optical movability, highlighting its potential as a programmable receiver architecture for future wireless networks.

</details>


### [140] [Towards Balanced Spectral Reconstruction: Spectrally Adaptive Loss for Streaming Speech Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30739)
*Haixin Zhao,Nilesh Madhu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出两种频谱加权STFT损失函数用于轻量级流式语音增强，解决由幅度-相位补偿效应引起的中高频段幅度过度衰减问题，并设计HyST-Net轻量骨干网络进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于STFT的语音增强损失函数在相位感知（phase-aware）优化过程中存在幅度-相位补偿效应，导致中高频区域的幅度被过度衰减，影响语音质量和可懂度。作者旨在通过频谱加权的方式来缓解这一失真。

Method: 提出两种频谱加权损失函数：(1) sigmoid加权损失，对相位感知贡献部分施加平滑的频率相关调制；(2) 信号依赖的频谱自适应损失，进一步将调制条件建立在真实log-幅度谱上。此外设计了HyST-Net轻量级骨干网络，采用混合MHA-GRU频谱-时间建模，服务于低延迟流式场景。

Result: 实验表明两种损失在高频谱重建上均有一致的改进；频谱自适应损失进一步增强了中频区域的重建，使得全频段的频谱重建更加均衡。

Conclusion: 提出的频谱加权损失函数能有效改善语音增强中的频谱重建，频谱自适应版本尤其能实现全频段更平衡的重建效果，适合轻量级流式语音增强应用。

Abstract: This paper proposes two spectrally weighted STFT loss functions for lightweight streaming speech enhancement, addressing the magnitude over-attenuation in mid-to-high frequency regions caused by the magnitude-phase compensation effect. The proposed sigmoid-weighted loss applies a smooth frequency-dependent modulation to the phase-aware contribution, while the signal-dependent spectrally adaptive loss further conditions the modulation on the ground-truth log-magnitude spectrogram. To evaluate the proposed objectives, we additionally design HyST-Net, a lightweight and competitive backbone with hybrid MHA-GRU spectral-temporal modelling for low-latency streaming scenarios. Experimental results exhibit consistent improvements in high-frequency spectral reconstruction for both losses. The spectrally adaptive loss further enhances the mid-frequency region, resulting in a more balanced spectral reconstruction across the full frequency range.

</details>


### [141] [Likelihood-Constrained Acoustic Reranking for Training-Free Hallucination Mitigation in LLM-Based ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30776)
*Jiasheng Kuang,Linru Zheng,Hongjin Song,Zhaoqi Cui,Song Li*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出LCAR（似然约束声学重排序），一种无需训练的推理解码方法，用于增强基于LLM的ASR系统的声学锚定性，同时保留基础模型的语言先验支持。它通过先保留基础模型似然在贪心token边缘内的候选，再用注意力池化音频嵌入和LM head计算声学兼容性分数进行重排序。实验表明在δ=0.60时，LCAR可消除38.8-57.1%的幻觉失败，同时保持标准测试集上的WER/CER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的ASR系统在常规语音上表现优异，但在挑战性条件下，其强大的语言先验和多语言能力可能凌驾于声学证据之上，导致非预期的翻译、指令执行、重复或灾难性删除等问题。现有方法无法在利用语言先验和保证声学锚定之间取得平衡。

Method: LCAR是一种无需训练的解码方法。在每个解码步骤：(1) 保留基础模型似然在贪心token指定边缘内的词元；(2) 使用注意力池化音频嵌入与现有LM head计算声学兼容性分数，对这些保留词元进行重排序。通过将声学干预限制在模型支持的合理候选集内，LCAR不需要额外训练、外部检测器、参考转录或辅助模型。

Result: 在四个基于LLM的ASR系统上，使用人工审核的TTS和开源语音挑战集进行评估。在δ=0.60时，LCAR消除了38.8-57.1%的检测器识别出的幻觉失败，同时在标准开源测试集上基本保持WER/CER。

Conclusion: LCAR证明了在无需任何额外训练或外部模块的前提下，通过受限的声学重排序可以有效缓解LLM-ASR系统在挑战条件下因语言先验过强而导致的幻觉问题，在声学锚定与语言先验支持之间取得了良好权衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems achieve strong performance on conventional speech data by leveraging powerful linguistic priors and multilingual capabilities. However, under challenging conditions, these priors can override acoustic evidence, resulting in unintended translation, instruction execution, repetition, or catastrophic deletion. We propose Likelihood-Constrained Acoustic Reranking (LCAR), a training-free decoding method that improves acoustic grounding while preserving support from the base model. At each decoding step, LCAR first retains tokens whose base-model likelihood falls within a margin of the greedy token, then reranks them using an acoustic compatibility score computed from attention-pooled audio embeddings and the existing LM head. By restricting acoustic intervention to plausible, model-supported alternatives, LCAR requires no additional training, external detector, reference transcript, or auxiliary model at inference. We evaluate LCAR on four LLM-based ASR systems using human-audited TTS and open-source speech challenge suites. At $δ=0.60$, LCAR removes 38.8--57.1\% of detector-identified hallucination failures while largely maintaining WER/CER on standard open-source test sets.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Advancing Lead-Free FASnI3 Perovskite Solar Cells via Cu-Doped NiOx HTL Engineering: An Optoelectronic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29341)
*Md. Faiaad Rahman,Jabed Hasan,Md. Ashaduzzaman Niloy,Ahmed Zubair*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Cu掺杂NiOx作为空穴传输层，结合FDTD光学模拟和FEM电学建模的数值框架，对无铅FASnI3钙钛矿太阳能电池进行了系统优化，最终实现了26.06%的峰值功率转换效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无铅钙钛矿太阳能电池的效率受限于电荷传输效率低下和严重的复合损耗，亟需改善空穴提取能力并抑制载流子损耗以提高器件性能。

Method: 采用Cu掺杂NiOx作为HTL，构建FASnI3钙钛矿太阳能电池。通过耦合时域有限差分（FDTD）光学模拟和基于有限元法（FEM）的电学建模进行系统性数值分析；电学求解中耦合泊松方程、漂移-扩散方程和载流子连续性方程，并考虑辐射、非辐射及界面复合机制；对组成层的厚度和掺杂浓度进行系统优化，并集成抗反射涂层（ARC）抑制反射损耗。

Result: 优化层尺寸和载流子浓度后，器件实现了24.71%的PCE；集成ARC后反射损耗得到有效抑制，PCE相对提升了5.47%，最终峰值PCE达到26.06%，对应Jsc为28.98 mA/cm2、Voc为1.051 V、FF为85.64%。

Conclusion: Cu掺杂NiOx作为空穴传输层在提升无铅FASnI3钙钛矿太阳能电池的电荷收集能力与整体效率方面展现出极大潜力，是一种极具前景的HTL材料方案。

Abstract: Efficient lead-free perovskite solar cells remain limited by inefficient charge transport and significant recombination losses. In this work, copper (Cu) doped nickel oxide (NiOx) is employed as the hole transport layer (HTL) in a formamidinium tin iodide (FASnI3) perovskite solar cell to improve hole extraction and suppress carrier losses. The optoelectronic response of the proposed device architecture is evaluated through a coupled numerical framework combining finite-difference time-domain (FDTD) optical simulations with finite element method (FEM)-based electrical modeling. Electrical analysis solves the Poisson equation together with the drift-diffusion and carrier continuity equations, while accounting for radiative, non-adiative, and interfacial recombination mechanisms. Systematic optimization of the thicknesses and doping concentrations of the constituent layers was performed to establish a high-performance device configuration. Following optimization of layer dimensions and carrier densities, the device achieved a power conversion efficiency (PCE) of 24.71%. Integrating an anti-reflection coating (ARC) effectively suppressed reflection losses, yielding a 5.47% relative enhancement in PCE. Consequently, the optimized device achieved a peak PCE of 26.06%, with a short-circuit current density (Jsc) of 28.98 mA/cm2, an open-circuit voltage (Voc) of 1.051 V, and a fill factor (FF) of 85.64%. These findings demonstrate that Cu-doped NiOx functions as a highly promising HTL to boost charge collection and maximize overall efficiency in lead-free FASnI3 solar cells.

</details>


### [143] [Arctic Dispersion Interruption Phenomenon and Sound Source Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30360)
*Weng Jinbao,Yang Yanming,Chen Benqing,Xu Dewei,Zhou Hongtao*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了深海中浅水面层形成的正常模式波导中的声源深度估计方法。当声源或接收器位于模态特征函数节点时，会造成色散中断现象，可利用从中频分析中提取的中断频率结合节点深度与模态频率的关系来估计声源深度，并通过仿真和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深海声学中，当声源或接收器深度接近简正波特征函数的波节时，模态无法被激发或检测，导致接收信号中该模态幅度近似为零，在色散结构中表现为色散中断。基于这一物理现象，可以发展出声源深度估计的新方法。

Method: 首先，通过海洋声速剖面计算节点深度与模态频率之间的关系；其次，对接收声信号进行时频分析，从中识别出色散中断频率；最后，在已知接收器深度的条件下，利用中断频率反演声源深度。该方法结合了模式波导理论和时频分析技术。

Result: 仿真与实验均验证了色散中断现象的客观存在性，并证明了基于中断频率的声源深度估计方法的有效性和可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了利用深海表面层正常模式波导中因节点位置引起的色散中断现象，通过时频分析提取中断频率并反演声源深度的可行性。这一方法为深海声源深度估计提供了一种新的技术途径。

Abstract: The sound speed profile in the deep Arctic Ocean causes the surface layer to form a normal mode waveguide. When the depth of the sound source or receiver is near a node of the eigenfunction, the source cannot excite the mode, or the receiver cannot detect it, resulting in the modal amplitude at the receiver being approximately zero. This manifests as a dispersion interruption in the dispersion structure, which can be observed through time-frequency analysis of the received acoustic signal. Based on the interruption frequency identified from the received signal, combined with the relationship between node depth and modal frequency calculated from the ocean sound speed profile, the source depth can be estimated if the receiver depth is known. The phenomenon of dispersion interruption and the method of estimating source depth have been validated through simulations and experiments.

</details>


### [144] [Towards an effective medium theory for in-vivo Magnetic Resonance: Characterizing Admissible Magnetization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30401)
*Alessandro Sbrizzi,Miha Fuderer,Ray Sheombarsing*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有效介质策略来描述异质材料（如生物组织）在宏观尺度（~1mm³）下的磁共振动力学。该方法保留经典Larmor转矩，将弛豫项替换为满足旋转对称性、热平衡全局稳定性和耗散性等物理约束的非线性有效介质场，从而完整表征了可容许的弛豫场，并提供了生成非线性弛豫模型的构造框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Bloch方程是磁共振理论的基础，但在异质材料（如生物组织）中可能无法充分捕捉观察到的复杂有效动力学行为，因此需要更一般的理论框架来描述宏观尺度的磁共振现象。

Method: 提出有效介质策略：保留经典Larmor转矩项，将线性弛豫项替换为一般非线性场（有效介质项），并施加三个关键物理约束——旋转对称性、热平衡全局稳定性、耗散性——以导出可容许弛豫场的完整刻画，并建立非线性弛豫模型的构造性框架。

Result: 理论框架完整刻画了满足物理约束的所有可容许弛豫场，经典Bloch动力学作为特例被恢复，同时提供了一个原则性的基础用于异质材料中磁共振现象的有效介质描述。

Conclusion: 该工作从第一性原理层面将经典Bloch方程推广到非线性有效介质框架，为生物组织等异质材料中的磁共振建模提供了新的理论基础，可能对MRI等应用中的信号解释和模型精度提升具有重要意义。

Abstract: The classical Bloch equation forms the foundation of magnetic resonance theory but may be insufficient to capture the complex effective dynamics observed in heterogeneous materials such as biological tissue. Here an effective-medium strategy is proposed to describe magnetic resonance dynamics from observations at macro-scale (order 1 mm$^3$). In this framework, the classical Larmor torque is retained while the relaxation term is replaced by a general nonlinear field (i.e. the effective medium term). This field is required to preserve key physical properties, including rotational symmetry, global stability of thermal equilibrium, and dissipativity. These requirements lead to a complete characterization of admissible relaxation fields and provide a constructive framework for generating nonlinear relaxation models. The resulting theory recovers classical Bloch dynamics as a special case and provides a principled foundation for effective-medium descriptions of magnetic resonance phenomena.

</details>


### [145] [A model-inversion control strategy to attain desired equilibrium points when synthesizing nonlinear resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30626)
*Maxime Morell,Emanuele De Bono,Emmanuel Gourdon,Manuel Collet,Alireza Ture Savadkoohi,Claude-Henri Lamarque*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对确定非线性系统的初始条件不可控导致平衡点不可达的问题，提出一种基于外系统（exosystem）定义理想非线性阻抗与合成外部激励的控制策略，结合模型逆的前馈控制在实际电声谐振器中实时强制执行外系统响应，从而确保系统收敛至期望平衡点。实验结果在准开放声环境和封闭腔体中均验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性非线性系统的初始条件会决定最终达到的渐近解，当非线性行为由主动控制合成且初始条件无法预先约束时会产生问题。电声谐振器的广义阻抗控制虽可在低激励水平下实现非线性机械响应，但其初始条件难以获取，导致实际呈现的平衡点不利于真实噪声源激励下腔体的噪声衰减。

Method: 提出一种控制策略，通过定义一个由理想非线性阻抗和合成外部激励刻画的"外系统"（exosystem），实时积分外系统动力学，并采用基于模型逆的前馈控制策略将外系统的响应强制施加于实际系统（电声谐振器）上，从而克服初始条件不可用的问题，达到期望平衡点。

Result: 该算法在实际电声谐振器中实现，并通过数值与实验双重验证。实验在准开放声学环境以及封闭腔体中对声模态衰减进行了测试，均验证了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过外系统定义与模型逆前馈控制相结合的控制策略，能够使确定性非线性系统摆脱对初始条件的依赖，收敛到期望平衡点，从而提升电声谐振器在真实噪声激励下的声学性能。

Abstract: The initial conditions of a deterministic nonlinear system affects the attained asymptotic solutions. This is a problem when the nonlinear behaviour is achieved by active control synthesis, and the initial conditions are not accessible for pre-constraining. Meanwhile, the generalized impedance control is recently gaining increasing attention in acoustics, and has been enlarged to achieve nonlinear mechanical responses of Electroacoustic Resonators at low excitation levels. Nevertheless, as the initial conditions of Electroacoustic Resonators are hardly accessible, the equilibrium points actually showcased by the synthetic nonlinear dynamics are not advantageous for noise attenuation in cavities excited by realistic noise sources. In this paper, we propose a control strategy being able to attain the desired equilibrium points, due to the definition of an exosystem characterized by a desired nonlinear impedance and a synthetic external excitation. The exosystem dynamics is integrated in real time and its response is enforced in the actual system (in our case, the Electroacoustic Resonator), by a model-inversion feedforward strategy. The proposed algorithm is implemented in the Electroacoustic Resonator, and is validated both numerically and experimentally. In particular, the experimental testing is conducted both in a quasi-open acoustic environment, and in enclosed cavities for acoustic mode attenuation.

</details>


### [146] [Bounds on Shaping Partially Coherent Microwaves with Programmable Scattering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30729)
*Philipp del Hougne*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了可编程散射系统操控部分相干微波的界限。通过结合多端口网络理论（MNT）与半定松弛（SDR），分别推导了将部分相干输入功率集中到单一输出端口的原型感知界，以及针对一般相干矩阵合成的架构无关界、原型特化标量细化和原型感知帕累托前沿界。在四个多达100个1比特可编程元件的实验性RIS参数化MIMO系统上验证了这些界限的紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程散射系统（如可重构智能表面RIS）用于操控微波，但现有分析多假设完全相干输入，而实际微波常为部分相干。需要为部分相干微波的波域处理与能量采集提供经过认证的理论极限，以指导系统设计和评估性能上限。

Method: 将多端口网络理论（MNT）与半定松弛（SDR）相结合，推导原型感知的功率集中界；针对一般相干矩阵合成问题，分别推导架构无关界、原型特化标量界及原型感知的有用强度-保真度帕累托前沿SDR界。原型感知公式考虑互耦、损耗、离散可调性和静态散射。使用从测量估计的代理MNT模型，在四个实验性RIS参数化MIMO系统（至多100个1比特可编程元件）上评估。

Result: 对于部分相干功率集中，原型感知界紧密；对于相干性合成，部分更简单的界在某些情形下比完全原型感知的界更紧，凸显互补性。四组实验系统的结果总体上与可行离散优化结果相比具有紧密性，提供了经过认证的性能上限。

Conclusion: 所推导的界限为部分相干微波的波域处理与能量采集提供了经过认证的极限，原型感知公式与简化公式互为补充，可指导可编程散射系统在实际部分相干条件下的设计与性能评估。

Abstract: We derive bounds on manipulating partially coherent microwaves with programmable scattering systems. We first consider the concentration of power from a partially coherent input into a single output port and derive a prototype-aware bound by combining multiport network theory (MNT) with semidefinite relaxation (SDR). We then address general coherency-matrix synthesis, deriving architecture-independent bounds on fidelity and useful strength, prototype-specific scalar refinements thereof, and fully prototype-aware SDR bounds on the useful-strength--fidelity Pareto frontier. The prototype-aware formulations account for mutual coupling, loss, discrete tunability, and static scattering. We evaluate the bounds on four experimental RIS-parametrized MIMO systems with up to 100 1-bit-programmable elements, using proxy-MNT models estimated from measurements. The resulting bounds are generally tight compared with feasible discrete-optimization outcomes. Interestingly, for coherency synthesis, the simpler bounds can in some cases be tighter than the fully prototype-aware bounds, highlighting their complementarity. Our results provide certified limits for wave-domain processing and harvesting of partially coherent microwaves.

</details>
